\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)=(1+|v|)^{c(\rho)}\big(1+|v|^{1/\delta_{1}}+|x|\phi(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})\big)^{200N_{0}-10(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|)},

(2.93)

\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)=(1+|v|^{1/\delta_{1}})^{100N_{0}-10(|\mathcal{L}|+|\alpha|)},

(2.94)

where
c(\rho)
and
\tilde{c}(\rho)
are defined in (
2.22
) and the cutoff function
\phi:\mathbb{R}_{+}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is defined as follows,

\phi(x):=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}0&\textup{if}\,\,x\leq 1\\
(x-1)^{2}&\textup{if}\,\,x\in(1,2]\\
3-2e^{-(x-2)}&\textup{if}\,\,x\in(2,\infty)\\
\end{array}\right.,\quad\Longrightarrow\phi^{\prime}(x)\geq 0,\quad\partial_{t}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\leq 0.

(2.95)

The correction term “
{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(t,v)
”, which is introduced to get around a losing derivative issue, is defined as follows,

{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(t,v):=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\frac{1}{2}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(s,x+s\hat{v})\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u^{\mathcal{L}}(s,x,v)dxds}&\textup{if\,\,}|\alpha|+|\mathcal{L}|=N_{0}\\
&\\
0&\textup{if\,\,}|\alpha|+|\mathcal{L}|<N_{0}.\\
\end{array}\right.

(2.96)

2.7.
Bootstrap argument and the proof of the theorem
1.1

Assume that
(g,f)
is a solution of the Einstein-Vlasov system (
1.2
) on time
[0,T]
such that the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
) and (
2.91
) are valid, the existence of
T>0
is ensured by the local existence result of Choquet-Bruhat
[
8
]
.

Firstly, from the estimate (
6.12
) in Proposition
6.1
and the estimate (
7.3
) in Proposition
7.1
, the bootstrap assumption for the Vlasov part is improved. Secondly, from the estimate (
4.12
) in Proposition
4.1
, the estimate of
\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{i},\vartheta_{mn}\}
is improved. Moreover, from the estimate (
3.2
) in Proposition
3.1
, the weighted estimate of
V^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}(t)
is improved. Lastly, recall (
2.39
), (
2.41
), and (
2.57
), from the estimate (
3.186
) in Lemma
3.19
and the improved estimate of
\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{i},\vartheta_{mn}\}
and the improved estimates of the Vlasov part, the estimate of
U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}(t))
is also improved.

Hence closing the bootstrap argument, we can extend the time interval
[0,T]
to
[0,\infty)
, i.e., the global regularity holds.

3.
Estimates of nonlinearities for any fixed time

In this section, one of main goals is to prove the following proposition
3.1
, in which we prove rough estimates for the nonlinearity of the metric part, which are convenient at places where it’s not necessary to be sharp. We also reduce the goal of improving the bootstrap assumption on
P_{k}(x_{i}V^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}})(t)
to the energy estimates of the metric part and the Vlasov part and the the
Z
-norm estimate of the Vlasov part, see (
3.2
).

Moreover, as byproducts, we obtain some estimates of the error type terms in the double Hodge decomposition in subsection
3.8
and an improved estimate for the modified profile at low frequency in subsection
3.9
.

Proposition 3.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumption
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following
estimate holds for any fixed
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n}
,
\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}-10}P_{l},

\forall l\in[0,N_{0}-|L|]\cap\mathbb{Z},\quad 2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\big(\|\partial_{t}\widehat{\widetilde{V^{L\tilde{h}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\partial_{t}\widehat{{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}}}(t,\xi)]\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.1)

\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(N_{0}-|\tilde{L}|-1)k_{+}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\widehat{{V^{\tilde{L}{h}_{\alpha\beta}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{{H}(|\tilde{L}|+1)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+Z^{vl}(t)

+\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(N_{0}-|\tilde{L}|-1)k_{+}}\|\widehat{{U^{\Gamma\tilde{L}{h}_{\alpha\beta}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0}}2^{-m/2}E_{\mathcal{L},\rho}^{vl}(t).

(3.2)

Proof.

For better presentation, we postpone the proof to subsection
3.7
.
∎

3.1.
Linear decay estimates for the density type function and the wave part

We first record the following
L^{\infty}
-decay estimate for the density type function of the Vlasov part.

Lemma 3.1
.

For
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
t\in\mathbb{R}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z},n\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},
, s.t.,
|t|\geq 1
, and any given zero order symbol
m(\xi,v)\in L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{0}
, the following decay estimate holds,

\sup_{\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)\widehat{g}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv\big|

\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n}2^{-nk}|t|^{-n}\|\nabla_{v}^{\beta}m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v,\xi}}\min\big\{\|(1+|v|)^{4n}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{g}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{1}_{x,v}},

\|(1+|v|)^{4n}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{g}(t,0,v)\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|t|^{-1}\|(1+|v|)^{4+4n}(1+|x|)\nabla_{v}^{\gamma}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}g(t,x,v)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{1}_{v}}\big\}.

(3.3)

Similarly, the following estimate holds for any fixed
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},a\in\mathbb{R}\cap(-3,\infty)
, and
h(x,v)\in H^{10+a}_{x,v}
,

\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)|\xi|^{a}\widehat{g}(t,\xi,v)h(x,v)dvd\xi\big|

\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 5+\lfloor a\rfloor}\big(\sum_{|\beta|\leq 5+\lfloor a\rfloor}\|\nabla_{v}^{\beta}m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}}\big)\big[|t|^{-3-a}\|(1+|v|)^{5+|a|}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\big(h(x,v)\widehat{g}(t,0,v)\big)\|_{L^{1}_{v}}

+|t|^{-4-a}\|(1+|v|)^{5+|a|}(1+|y|)\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\big(h(x,v)g(t,y,v)\big)\|_{L^{1}_{y}L^{1}_{v}}\big].

(3.4)

For any
n\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},p\in[1,\infty)
, the following estimate holds,

\big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)|\xi|^{n}\widehat{g}(t,\xi,v)h(x,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dvd\xi\big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|,|\beta|,|\gamma|\leq n}|t|^{-n}\|\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{v}^{\beta}h(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|(1+|v|)^{10+3n}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma}g(t,x,v)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

(3.5)

Proof.

The main ideas are singling out the zero frequency (or not) for different desired estimates and doing integration by parts in
v
. See
[
49
]
[The proof of Lemma 3.1] for more details.
∎

In the following Lemma, as in the same spirit of
[
29
]
[Lemma 2.11], we prove
L^{\infty}_{x}
decay estimate for the frequency super localized linear half-wave.

Lemma 3.2
.

Let
t\in\mathbb{R},x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\mu\in\{+,-\}
,
k,n,\tilde{k}\in\mathbb{Z}
, s.t.,
|t|\gg 1
,
2^{k}\geq|t|^{-1}
,
\tilde{k}\leq k
.
If
|x|\leq 2^{-100}|t|
, we have

\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}m(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)d\xi\big|

\lesssim|t|^{-1}\|m(\xi)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}2^{\tilde{k}/2}\big(2^{k}\|\nabla_{\xi}\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\big).

(3.6)

If
|x|\geq 2^{-100}|t|
, we have

\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}m(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)d\xi\big|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in\{\Omega_{ij}\},|\alpha|\leq 1,|\beta|\leq 2}|x|^{-1}2^{\tilde{k}/2}\|m(\xi)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}

\times\|\widehat{\Gamma^{\alpha}f}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{k}(|\xi|)\|_{L^{2}(\xi)}^{1-\delta_{1}^{2}}\|\widehat{\Gamma^{\beta}f}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{k}(|\xi|)\|_{L^{2}(\xi)}^{\delta_{1}^{2}},

(3.7)

\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}m(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)d\xi\big|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in\{\Omega_{ij}\},|\beta|\leq 2}|x|^{-1}2^{\tilde{k}/2}\|m(\xi)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}

\times\big[2^{k+\tilde{k}/2}\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+|x|^{-1/3}2^{-k/3}\|\widehat{\Gamma^{\beta}f}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{k}(|\xi|)\|_{L^{2}(\xi)}\big].

(3.8)

Proof.

Note that
\nabla_{\xi}(x\cdot\xi-\mu t|\xi|)=0
if and only if
{\xi}/{|\xi|}=\mu{x}/{t}=\mu{x}/{|x|}:=\xi_{0}
. Let
\bar{l}
be the least integer such that
2^{\bar{l}}\geq 2^{-k/2}|x|^{-1/2}
. From the volume of support of
\xi
and the Sobolev embedding in angular variables, we have

\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}\widehat{f}(\xi)m(\xi)\psi_{k}(|\xi|)\psi_{\leq\bar{l}}(\angle(\xi,\xi_{0}))\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)d\xi\Big|

\lesssim\|m(\xi)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}2^{(2k+\tilde{k})/2+2\bar{l}}\|\widehat{f}(r\theta)\psi_{k}(r)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{\infty}_{\theta}}.

(3.9)

For the case when the angle is localized around
2^{l}
where
l>\bar{l}
, we first do integration by parts in
\xi
in the rotational directions once. As a result, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}\widehat{f}(\xi)m(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\xi_{0}))d\xi=I_{l}^{1}+I_{l}^{2},\\
I_{l}^{1}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}\big(\frac{ix\times\xi}{|x\times\xi|^{2}}\cdot(\xi\times\nabla_{\xi})\widehat{f}(\xi)m(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\xi_{0}))\big)d\xi,\\
I_{l}^{2}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}\big[(\xi\times\nabla_{\xi})\cdot\big(\frac{ix\times\xi}{|x\times\xi|^{2}}m(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\xi_{0}))\big)\big]\widehat{f}(\xi)d\xi.

(3.10)

For
I_{l}^{2}
, we do integration by parts in
\xi
in the rotational directions one more time. As a result, we have

I_{l}^{2}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}(\xi\times\nabla_{\xi})\cdot\Big[\frac{ix\times\xi}{|x\times\xi|^{2}}\big[(\xi\times\nabla_{\xi})\cdot\big(\frac{ix\times\xi}{|x\times\xi|^{2}}m(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)\\
\times\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\xi_{0}))\big)\widehat{f}(\xi)\big]\Big]d\xi.

(3.11)

From the volume of support of “
\xi
”, we have

|I_{l}^{1}|+|I_{l}^{2}|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}\big(|x|^{-1}2^{-l}+|x|^{-2}2^{-2l}2^{-k-l}\big)

\times 2^{-k}2^{(2k+\tilde{k})/2+(1+\delta_{1}^{3}){l}}\|\widehat{\Gamma f}(r\theta)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\psi_{k}(r)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{2/(1-\delta_{1}^{3})}_{\theta}}

+|x|^{-2}2^{-2l}2^{-2k-2l}2^{(2k+\tilde{k})/2+2{l}}\|\widehat{f}(r\theta)\psi_{k}(r)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

(3.12)

From the above estimate (
3.12
), we have

\sum_{\bar{l}<l\leq 2}|I_{l}^{1}|+|I_{l}^{2}|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}|x|^{-1}2^{\tilde{k}/2}\|m(\xi)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}\big[\|\widehat{f}(r\theta)\psi_{k}(r)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

+\|\widehat{\Gamma f}(r\theta)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\psi_{k}(r)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{2/(1-\delta_{1}^{3})}_{\theta}}\big].

(3.13)

The desired estimate (
3.7
) holds from the estimates (
3.9
) and (
3.13
) and the Sobolev embedding on the sphere.

Similarly, if we rerun the above argument, do integration by parts in
\xi
in the rotational directions one more time for
I_{l}^{1}
. As a result, the following estimate holds,

\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi-i\mu t|\xi|}m(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}|\xi|-n)d\xi\big|\lesssim\sum_{l\geq\bar{l}}\sum_{\Gamma\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}|x|^{-2}2^{-2l}\big[2^{-k-l}2^{-k}2^{(2k+\tilde{k})/2+5{l}/3}

\times\|\widehat{\Gamma f}(r\theta)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\psi_{k}(r)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{6}_{\theta}}+2^{-2k-2l}2^{(2k+\tilde{k})/2+2{l}}\|\widehat{f}(r\theta)\psi_{k}(r)\psi(2^{-\tilde{k}}r-n)\|_{L^{2}(r^{2}dr)L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\big].

(3.14)

Therefore, our desired estimate (
3.8
) holds from the above estimate and the Sobolev embedding on the sphere.
∎

3.2.
Estimates of basic quantities

3.2.1.
Difference between the perturbed metric and the modified perturbed metric

In the following Lemma, we show that the growth rates of the modified perturbed metric and the perturbed metric are almost comparable in both
L^{2}
-type space and the
L^{\infty}
-type space. They are only different at very top order.

Lemma 3.3
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}],\tilde{L}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},{L}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}-5}P_{n}
,
\mu\in\{0,1,2,3\},
we have

\|\Lambda_{1}[\partial_{t}\widehat{{V^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}}}(t,\xi)]\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\|P_{k}\big(U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}(t)-\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}}(t)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{3k_{-}/2}\min\{2^{d(|\tilde{L}|,0)\delta_{0}m},2^{k+d(|\tilde{L}|,0)\delta_{0}m}+1+2^{m+k}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{L}|=N_{0}}\}\epsilon_{1},

(3.15)

\sum_{|L|\leq N_{0}-2}\sum_{l\in[0,N_{0}-|L|]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\|P_{k}\big(U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}(t)-\widetilde{U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}}(t)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1},

(3.16)

2^{-k_{-}+(N_{0}-3-|L|)k_{+}}\big(\|P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|P_{k}({U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)+\|Lh_{\alpha\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|+1)\delta_{1}m+1.1\gamma m}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.17)

Proof.

Note that the following decomposition holds for any differentiable symbol
m(\xi,v)
,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)m(\xi,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)\xi\cdot\nabla_{\xi}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,s\xi,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dvds\\
+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)\big(\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot\tilde{u}^{\mathcal{L}}_{corr}(t,v)\big)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\nabla_{v}m(\xi,v)\cdot\tilde{u}^{\mathcal{L}}_{corr}(t,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv\\
+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}m(\xi,v)t\nabla_{v}(i\hat{v}\cdot\xi)\cdot\tilde{u}^{\mathcal{L}}_{corr}(t,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv.

(3.18)

Recall (
2.54
) and the definition of the modified perturbed metric in (
2.64
). From the above decomposition and the volume of support of
\xi
if
k\leq 0
, we have

\|P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{1}[L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}]\big)(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{3k_{-}/2-lk_{+}}\min\{2^{d(|\tilde{L}|,l)\delta_{0}m},2^{k+d(|\tilde{L}|,l)\delta_{0}m}+1+2^{m+k}\mathbf{1}_{|{L}|=N_{0}}\}\epsilon_{1}

\|P_{k}\big(U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}(t)-\widetilde{U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}}(t)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{3k_{-}/2-k-lk_{+}}\min\{2^{d(|\tilde{L}|,l)\delta_{0}m},2^{k+d(|\tilde{L}|,l)\delta_{0}m}+1+2^{m+k}\mathbf{1}_{|{L}|=N_{0}}\}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.19)

Hence finishing the proof of the desired estimate (
3.15
).

From the decomposition (
3.18
), the estimate (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}
if
|L|\leq N_{0}-2
, and
l\in[0,N_{0}-|L|]\cap\mathbb{Z},

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\|P_{k}\big(U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}(t)-\widetilde{U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}}(t)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim\min\{2^{-m-k_{-}+d(|{L}|+1,(l-1)_{+})\delta_{0}m},2^{lk_{+}}(1+2^{k+d(|L|,0)\delta_{0}m})\}\epsilon_{1}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}.

Hence finishing the proof of the desired estimate (
3.16
).

From the linear decay estimates (
3.6
) and (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
and the obtained estimate (
3.16
), the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}-10}P_{n},

\|P_{k}({U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{k_{-}/2-\gamma k_{-}}\min\{2^{3k/2-(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}},2^{-(N_{0}-|L|-2)k_{+}}2^{-m+k/2+H(|{L}|+1)\delta_{1}m+\gamma m/100}\}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.20)

From the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds if
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
,

\|P_{k}(\tilde{\rho}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{L}(f))(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\sum_{\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\|P_{k}(\tilde{\rho}^{\tilde{h}}_{L}(f))(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\min\{2^{2k},2^{-3m-k}\}2^{-(N_{0}-|L|-3)k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.21)

Recall the definition of modified half-wave in (
2.64
). We know that the last estimate in (
3.17
) hold from the estimates (
3.20
) and (
3.21
).

∎

3.2.2.
General bilinear estimates for the wave-wave type interaction

Lemma 3.4
.

For any
L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0},|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n},
s.t.,
L_{1}\circ L_{2}=L\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0},|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
f,g\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn},h_{\alpha\beta}\}
, and Fourier multiplier operators
T_{1},T_{2}\in\mathcal{S}^{0}
, the following estimates hold under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
,

\|T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.22)

\|\partial_{\alpha}T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-2m/3+d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.23)

where
\|L\|,
and
|L|
are defined in Definition
2.1
.

Proof.

After doing dyadic decompositions for two inputs and using the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, we have

\|T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\|P_{k}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{\leq k-10}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

+\|P_{\leq k-10}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{k}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\big(\sum_{|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq 10}\|P_{k_{1}}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{k_{2}}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)^{2}.

(3.24)

Based on the relative size of
|L_{1}|
and
|L_{2}|
, we separate into two cases as follows.

\bullet
If
|L_{1}|\leq|L_{2}|
.   Note that we have
|L_{1}|\leq|L|/2
for this case. If
k_{1}\leq k_{2}+10
, then from the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type estimate and the
L^{\infty}
-estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, we have

\|P_{k_{1}}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{k_{2}}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}-k_{2}}\min\{\|U^{L_{1}f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}},2^{3k_{1}/2}\|U^{L_{1}f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\}\|U^{L_{2}g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.25)

If
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
, then the following estimate holds after putting
U^{L_{2}g}_{k_{2}}
in
L^{\infty}
,

\|P_{k_{1}}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{k_{2}}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}-k_{2}}\min\{\|U^{L_{2}f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|U^{L_{2}f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}\|U^{L_{1}g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.26)

If
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
and
|L|\geq N_{0}-5
, the following estimate holds after putting
U^{L_{1}f}_{k_{1}}
in
L^{\infty}
or putting
U^{L_{2}g}_{k_{2}}
in
L^{\infty}
and using
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding,

\|P_{k_{1}}(T_{1}(L_{1}f(t)))P_{k_{2}}(T_{2}(L_{2}g(t)))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}-k_{2}}\min\{2^{3k_{2}/2}\|U^{L_{1}f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}},\|U^{L_{1}f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\}\|U^{L_{2}g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.27)

After combining the estimates (
3.24
) and (
3.25
–
3.27
), we have

\begin{split}\|T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}&\lesssim 2^{d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\big(\sum_{k\leq-m}2^{k/2-2\gamma k_{-}}+\sum_{k\geq-m}2^{-m-k-2\gamma k_{-}}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m/2+d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.28)

Following the same strategy, with minor modifications, we have

\begin{split}\|\partial_{\alpha}T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}_{x}}&\lesssim 2^{d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\big(\sum_{k\leq-2m/3}2^{k-2\gamma k_{-}}\\
+\sum_{k\geq-2m/3}2^{-m-k/2-2\gamma k_{-}}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}&\lesssim 2^{-2m/3+d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.29)

\bullet
If
|L_{1}|\geq|L_{2}|
.   Since the estimate of
T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))
is symmetric with respect to
L_{1}
and
L_{2}
, we only need to estimate
\partial_{\alpha}T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))
.

Note that the following estimate holds from the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate,

\|\partial_{\alpha}T_{1}(L_{1}f(t))T_{2}(L_{2}g(t))\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|U^{L_{1}f}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|U^{L_{2}g}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m}.

(3.30)

To sum up, our desired estimates (
3.22
) and (
3.23
) hold from the estimates (
3.28
–
3.30
).
∎

3.2.3.
Estimates of basic quantities

Now, we apply basic linear and bilinear estimates obtained in previous subsubsections to control some basic quantities, which are mostly higher order terms. We estimate them in details here so that they can be treated like error terms in later energy estimates.

Lemma 3.5
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
), for any
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3\}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},

L_{1}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0},|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
and
L_{2}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0}-5,|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
, we have

2^{m/2-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\|{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{2}}+2^{2m/3-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\|\partial_{\alpha}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{2}}

+2^{2m-d(|L_{2}|+2,\|L_{2}\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\|{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{2\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.31)

Proof.

Note that

\textup{det}(g)=\sum_{\alpha_{i},\beta_{i}\in\{0,1,2,3\}}c^{\beta_{1}\cdots\beta_{4}}_{\alpha_{1}\cdots\alpha_{4}}\prod_{i=1,2,3,4}(1+h_{\alpha_{i}\beta_{i}}),

where
c^{\beta_{1}\cdots\beta_{4}}_{\alpha_{1}\cdots\alpha_{4}}\in\{0,-1,1\}
is some uniquely determined constants. Therefore, we have

{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{L}={L}_{1}\circ\cdots{L}_{4},{L}_{i}\in P^{b_{i}}_{a_{i}},\{j_{1},\cdots,j_{4}\}=\{1,\cdots,4\},\alpha_{i},\beta_{i}\in\{0,1,2,3\}\\
\end{subarray}}c^{\beta_{1}\cdots\beta_{4}}_{\alpha_{1}\cdots\alpha_{4}}\prod_{i=1,2,3,4}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{L}_{j_{i}}(1+h_{\alpha_{i}\beta_{i}})].

(3.32)

From the estimates (
3.22
) and (
3.23
) in Lemma
3.4
, we have

2^{m/2-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\|{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{2}}+2^{2m/3-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\|\partial_{\alpha}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{2}}

\lesssim 2^{2\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

From the
L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}
type mutlilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, and the estimate (
2.90
), we have

\|{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L_{2}}\|{L}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|{L}_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+d(|L_{2}|+2,\|L_{2}\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Hence finishing the proof of the desired estimate (
3.31
).
∎

Lemma 3.6
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
), for any
\alpha,\beta,\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3\}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},

L_{1}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0},|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
and
L_{2}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0}-5,|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
, we have

2^{m/2-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\big[\big(\|{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({-\textup{det}g})^{-1/2}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{\alpha\beta}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

+2^{m/6}\big(\|\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({-\textup{det}g})^{-1/2}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{\alpha\beta}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)\big]

+2^{2m-d(|L_{2}|+2,\|L_{2}\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\big(\|{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({-\textup{det}g})^{-1/2}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{\alpha\beta}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\lesssim 2^{2\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.33)

Proof.

Due to the small data regime, we will use a fixed point type argument to show that the size of “
{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({-\textup{det}g})^{-1/2}])
” is comparable with the size of “
{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\textup{det}g])
”. More precisely, let

y:=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[-\textup{det}(g)],\quad x:=\Lambda_{1}[-\textup{det}(g)]=h_{00}-h_{ii},

z:=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({-\textup{det}(g)})^{-1/2}],\quad w:=\Lambda_{1}[({-\textup{det}(g)})^{-1/2}]=-\frac{1}{2}x.

Note that

(1+z+w)^{2}=({1+x+y})^{-1}=1-x+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({1+x+y})^{-1}],\\
\Longrightarrow 2z+2zw+z^{2}+w^{2}=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({1+x+y})^{-1}],

(3.34)

\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\frac{1}{1+(x+y)}]=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\frac{-(x+y)}{1+(x+y)}]

=-\frac{\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(x+y)]}{1+(x+y)}-x\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\frac{1}{1+(x+y)}]=-y\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\frac{1}{1+(x+y)}]-y(1-x)

-x\big(-x+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\frac{1}{1+(x+y)}]\big)=-(x+y)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\frac{1}{1+x+y}]-y(1-x)+x^{2}.

(3.35)

Let

X_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{m/2-d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\big[\|{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({1+x+y})^{-1}])\|_{L^{2}}

+2^{m/6}\|\partial_{\gamma}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({1+x+y})^{-1}])\|_{L^{2}}\big],

Y_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{m/2-d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\big[\|{L}z\|_{L^{2}}+2^{m/6}\|\partial_{\gamma}{L}z\|_{L^{2}}\big],

\tilde{X}_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{2m-d(|L|+2,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\|{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({1+x+y})^{-1}])\|_{L^{\infty}},

\tilde{Y}_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{2m-d(|L|+2,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\|{L}z\|_{L^{\infty}}.

From the equality (
3.35
),
L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}
type estimate,
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type estimate, and the estimate (
3.31
) in Lemma
3.5
, we know that the following estimates hold,

\tilde{X}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\tilde{X}_{\beta,l}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\Longrightarrow\sum_{0\leq|\alpha|+N(n)\leq N_{0}-5}\tilde{X}_{n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.36)

X_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l\leq n/2+2,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\tilde{X}_{\beta,l}+\sum_{0\leq n^{\prime}\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}{X}_{\beta,n^{\prime}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2},\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\sum_{0\leq|\alpha|+N(n)\leq N_{0}}X_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.37)

Similarly, from the equality (
3.34
), the following estimates hold from the estimate (
3.36
),

\tilde{Y}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\tilde{Y}_{\beta,l}+\tilde{X}_{\alpha,n}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\big(\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\tilde{Y}_{\beta,l}\big)^{2},

\Longrightarrow\sum_{n\in[0,N_{0}-5]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\tilde{Y}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.38)

Y_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l^{\prime}\leq n/2+2,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\tilde{Y}_{\beta,l^{\prime}}+\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}{Y}_{\beta,l}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\big(\sum_{0\leq l^{\prime}\leq n/2+2,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\tilde{Y}_{\beta,l^{\prime}}\big)\big(\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}Y_{\beta,l}\big)\\
\Longrightarrow\quad\sum_{n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}Y_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.39)

Note that, for any fixed
\alpha,\beta\in\{0,1,2,3\}
, the following equality holds for some uniquely determined constants
c_{\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\cdots\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}}^{\alpha\beta}\in\{0,1,-1\}
,

\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{\alpha\beta}]=\sum_{\alpha_{i},\beta_{i}\in\{0,1,2,3\}}\prod_{i=1,2,3}c_{\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\cdots\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}}^{\alpha\beta}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(\textup{det}g)^{-1}(1+h_{\alpha_{i}\beta_{i}})].

(3.40)

Note that we have already derived the basic
L^{2}
-estimate and the basic
L^{\infty}
-estimate for
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(\det g)^{-1}]
, see estimates (
3.36
) and (
3.37
). After applying the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate and the
L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate for the equality (
3.40
), it is easy to see that our desired estimate (
3.33
) holds.
∎

Due to the on shell relation, see (
1.1
),
v_{0}
as well as
v^{0}
depend on the perturbed metric. For convenience, we identify the zero order and the first order of
v_{0}
(
v^{\alpha}
) as follows,

g^{\mu\nu}v_{\mu}v_{\nu}=-1,\quad\Longrightarrow\Lambda_{0}[v^{0}]=\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}},\quad\Lambda_{0}[v_{0}]=-\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}},\\
\Lambda_{0}[v^{i}]=v_{i},\quad\Lambda_{1}[v^{i}]=-h_{0i}\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}-h_{ij}v_{j},\quad\Lambda_{1}[v^{0}]=\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}\big(h_{00}-\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}h_{ij}\big),\\
\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]=\frac{1}{2}\big(\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}h_{00}+2v_{i}h_{0i}+\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}h_{ij}\big).

(3.41)

For the quadratic and higher order terms of
v_{0}
, their explicit formulas are not necessary. It would be sufficient to give their rough estimates, which are summarized in the following two lemmas.

Lemma 3.7
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
), for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},

for
L_{1}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0},|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
and
L_{2}\in\cup_{0\leq n\leq N_{0}-5,|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
, we have

2^{m/2-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\big[\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)+2^{m/6}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)\big]

+2^{2m-d(|L_{2}|+2,\|L_{2}\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}_{2}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\lesssim 2^{2\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.42)

Proof.

Note that

g^{00}\big(-\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\big)^{2}=g^{00}\big[\big(-\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]\big)^{2}

+2\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\big(-\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]\big)+\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\big)^{2}\big]=2\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+R,

(3.43)

where

R=g^{00}\big(-\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]\big)^{2}-2\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\big(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[g^{00}]\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+g^{00}\big(\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]\big)\big)+g^{00}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]\big)^{2}.

(3.44)

From the equality (
3.43
), we have

2\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]=-R-1-g^{ij}v_{i}v_{j}+2g^{0i}\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}-2g^{0i}v_{i}\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}]-2g^{0i}v_{i}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]

=-2g^{0i}v_{i}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[R]-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{ij}]v_{i}v_{j}+2\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{0i}]\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}-2\Lambda_{\geq 1}[g^{0i}]v_{i}\Lambda_{1}[v_{0}].

(3.45)

Moreover,

v_{0}=g_{\alpha 0}v^{\alpha},\quad v^{i}=g^{i\alpha}v_{\alpha},\quad\Longrightarrow\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]=-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}]+h_{0i}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}]+h_{0i}\Lambda_{1}[v^{i}],

(3.46)

\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}]=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{i0}v_{0}]+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{ij}]v_{j}=g^{i0}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{i0}\Lambda_{\leq 1}[v_{0}]]+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{ij}]v_{j}.

(3.47)

For any fixed
v
, we define the following two quantities

Z_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{m/2-d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\big[\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\langle t\rangle^{1/6}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big],

(3.48)

\tilde{Z}_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{2m-d(|L|+2,\|L\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(3.49)

W_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{m/2-d(|L|+1,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\big[\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)

+2^{m/6}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)\big],

(3.50)

\tilde{W}_{\alpha,n}:=\sum_{{L}\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}}2^{2m-d(|L|+2,\|L\|-|L|)\delta_{0}m-2\delta_{0}m}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sum_{i=1,2,3}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}{L}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{i}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big).

(3.51)

By using a similar argument as in the proof of Lemma
3.6
and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, the following estimate holds for any fixed
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\tilde{Z}_{\alpha,n}+\tilde{W}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,0\leq|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}(\tilde{Z}_{\beta,l}+\tilde{W}_{\beta,l})+\epsilon_{1}^{2},\Longrightarrow\sum_{n\in[0,N_{0}-5]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\tilde{Z}_{\alpha,n}+\tilde{W}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.52)

{Z}_{\alpha,n}+{W}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq l^{\prime}\leq n/2+2,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\big(\tilde{Z}_{\beta,l^{\prime}}+\tilde{W}_{\beta,l^{\prime}}\big)+\sum_{0\leq l\leq n,|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}\epsilon_{1}\big({Z}_{\beta,l}+{W}_{\beta,l}\big)+\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\Longrightarrow\sum_{n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}{Z}_{\alpha,n}+{W}_{\alpha,n}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.53)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate
(
3.42
).
∎

Lemma 3.8
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
),
the following estimate holds for any
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3\}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},
and any triple
\beta\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3}
, for any
i\in\{1,2,3\},\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3\}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},

2^{m/2-d(|L_{1}|+1,\|L_{1}\|-|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}\big[\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|+1}{L}_{1}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(v^{0})^{-1}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}{L}_{1}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v^{0}}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}\mathcal{L}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v_{0}}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)+2^{m/6}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|+1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(v^{0})^{-1}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{1}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v^{0}}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}\partial_{\gamma}\mathcal{L}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v_{0}}])\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)\big]+2^{2m-d(|L_{2}|+2,\|L_{2}\|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|+1}{L}_{2}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({v^{0}})^{-1}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}{L}_{2}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v^{0}}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{|\beta|-1}{L}_{2}\nabla_{v}^{\beta}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[{v_{0}}])\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\lesssim 2^{2\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.54)

Proof.

Recall (
3.41
). Note that

({v^{0}})^{-1}=({\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+\Lambda_{1}[v^{0}]+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}]})^{-1}.

Let

\quad x:=\Lambda_{1}[v^{0}],\quad y:=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}],\quad z:=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[({v^{0}})^{-1}],\quad w:=\Lambda_{1}[({v^{0}})^{-1}]=-\langle v\rangle^{-2}\Lambda_{1}[v^{0}]=-\langle v\rangle^{-2}x.

Hence, the following equality holds,

\big(\langle v\rangle^{-1}+z+w\big)\big(\langle v\rangle+x+y\big)=1,\quad\Longrightarrow\langle v\rangle z=-(x+y)z-\langle v\rangle^{-1}y-xw-yw.

(3.55)

From the fixed point type formulations for
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{0}]
,
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v^{0}]
, and
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\big(v^{0}\big)^{-1}]
in (
3.45
), (
3.46
), (
3.47
), and (
3.55
) and the
L^{2}
-type estimate and the
L^{\infty}
-type estimate (
3.42
) in Lemma
3.7
, our desired estimate (
3.54
) holds after using the same argument used in the proof of the estimates (
3.42
) Lemma
3.7
and doing induction on the size of “
|\beta|
”.
∎

3.3.
Estimates of the nonlinearities in Vlasov part

In the following lemma, we estimate the energy of the cubic and higher order terms of the Vlasov part.

Lemma 3.9
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{n},\rho\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}
,

\sum_{i=1,3,4}\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,i}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+3,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.56)

Moreover, for any
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{n},\rho,\iota\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\iota|=1
,
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-1
, we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\iota\circ\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\iota\circ\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+4,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m+\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.57)

Proof.

Recall (
2.27
–
2.30
). Note that, from the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
and the estimate (
3.42
) in Lemma
3.7
, we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim\big(\|v\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}(v^{0})^{-1}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)

\times\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+4,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m+\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.58)

By using the same strategy, our desired estimate (
3.57
) holds from the equality (
2.23
) and the estimate of corresponding coefficients (
2.24
)
in Lemma
2.2
.

It remains to estimate
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}
and
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,4}^{\mathcal{L}}
. We use different strategies based on the possible size of the number of vector fields acting on the Vlasov part.

\bullet
If
|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|>|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
Without further explanation, we let the vector fields be restricted in the range listed in (
2.30
). Since there are at most six derivatives act on the metric component, we use the decay of the perturbed metrics. As a result, from the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, the estimates of coefficients in (
2.31
), the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, the estimate (
3.42
) in Lemma
3.7
, and the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, the following estimate holds for the

\begin{split}\|c_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3};\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}&(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\\
\times\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot&\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\sum_{|\tilde{\rho}|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}|\leq|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|}\big(\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}_{1}&\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}]\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)](t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
\times\|\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&+\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(&({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)](t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\|\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\tilde{\rho}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\tilde{\rho}}u^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\qquad\qquad\lesssim&2^{-2m+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+3,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.\qquad\qquad\end{split}

(3.59)

\bullet
If
|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
.

For this case, we use the decay of the density of the Vlasov part. From the
L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type estimate, the decay estimate of density function type (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, and the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)c_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3};\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)](t,x+t\hat{v},v)

\times\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

+\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim\sum_{|\tilde{\rho}|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}|\leq N_{0}-5}\big(\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big](t,x,v)]\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

+\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\big)\|\langle v\rangle^{-20}\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\tilde{\rho}}(x-t\hat{v},v)\Lambda^{\tilde{\rho}}u^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+3,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.60)

By using a similar strategy, the following estimate holds from the decay estimate of density function type (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, the estimate (
3.42
) in Lemma
3.7
, the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, and the
L^{2}
-type bilinear estimate (
3.22
) in Lemma
3.4
,

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)c^{\tilde{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\cdot D_{v}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{m}\big(\|\nabla_{x}\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}g^{\alpha\beta}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-2}\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}g^{\alpha\beta}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)

\times\|\langle v\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\nabla_{x,v}^{\alpha}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+3,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.61)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
3.56
) holds from the estimates (
3.60
–
3.61
).

∎

Now, we estimate the quadratic terms of the Vlasov part.

Lemma 3.10
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,
\mathcal{L},\tilde{\mathcal{L}}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{n},\rho,\iota,\kappa\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\iota|=1
,
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}
,
|\kappa|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}|\leq|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-1,

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1,4}\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,i}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,i}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,2}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\\
&\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,3}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m/2+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.62)

Moreover, for the second part of non-linearity,
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
, the following improved estimate holds if we allow some loss of decay in
(x,v),

\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,2}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(4)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.63)

Proof.

Recall (
2.27
-
2.28
). From the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, the estimate of corresponding coefficients (
2.24
)
in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,1}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,2}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1},|\Gamma|+|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}}\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big(\|\langle v\rangle\Lambda_{1}[\Gamma(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}(v^{0})^{-1}\Lambda_{1}[v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}\Gamma g^{\alpha\beta}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\lesssim 2^{-m+\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+(H(1)\delta_{1}+2\gamma)m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.64)

Next, we estimate
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
. Recall (
2.29
). From the
L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type bilinear estimate, the decay estimate of the density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
and the estimate of coefficients in (
2.31
), we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,3}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-m/2+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.65)

Lastly, we estimate
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,4}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
. Recall (
2.30
). If
|\rho_{1}|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}|\geq 10
or
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-10
, then the following estimate holds from the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, the estimate of coefficients in (
2.31
) and the decay estimate in (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
,

\|c_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3};\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v},v)]\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

\times\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+(H(1)\delta_{1}+2\gamma)m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.66)

If
|\rho_{1}|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}|\leq 10
and
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq N_{0}-10
, we do dyadic decomposition for the metric component. As an example, we have

\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v},v)=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}H_{k}(t,x+t\hat{v},v),\\
H_{k}(t,x+t\hat{v},v)=\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v},v).

(3.67)

On one hand, the following estimate holds from the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate and the
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding,

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)H_{k}(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{(1-\gamma)k+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.68)

On the other hand, form the
L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type bilinear estimate, the decay estimate of the density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, the first estimate in (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
and the estimate of coefficients in (
2.31
), we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)H_{k}(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\sum_{\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\iota|\leq 4}2^{-k/2+\gamma k-3m/2+H(\mathcal{L}_{1},\iota\circ\rho_{1})\delta_{0}m}

\times\min\{2^{H(\mathcal{L}_{2},\rho_{2})\delta_{0}m},2^{H(|\mathcal{L}_{2}|+|\rho_{2}|)\delta_{1}m}+2^{k+H(\mathcal{L}_{2},\rho_{2})\delta_{0}m}+2^{m+k}\mathbf{1}_{|\rho_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|=N_{0}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.69)

Combining the above two estimates (
3.68
) and (
3.69
), we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+(H(1)\delta_{1}+2\gamma)m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.70)

Following the same strategy, we have

\|c_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3};\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\tilde{L}_{1}\Gamma_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(({2v^{0}})^{-1}{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v},v)]\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

\times\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+(H(1)\delta_{1}+2\gamma)m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.71)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
3.62
) holds from the estimates (
3.64
–
3.66
) and (
3.70
–
3.71
).

\bullet
Proof of the desired estimate (
3.63
).

Recall (
2.28
). The main idea is to exploit the decay rate of the distance with respect to the light cone. More precisely, the following identity holds,

(\partial_{t},\nabla_{x})=\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}a_{\Gamma}(t,x)\Gamma,\quad a_{\Gamma}(t,x)=c_{\Gamma}(t,x)(|t|^{2}-|x|^{2})^{-1}

(3.72)

where
c_{\Gamma}(t,x)
are some uniquely determined coefficients and satisfy the following estimate

|c_{\Gamma}(t,x)|\leq|t|+|x|,\quad|a_{\Gamma}(t,x)|\leq\big||t|-|x|\big|^{-1}.

(3.73)

Note that the following point-wise estimate holds,

\langle|t|-|x+t\hat{v}|\rangle^{-1}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\langle|v|\rangle^{-2}\lesssim 2^{-m}.

(3.74)

From the above estimate, the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, and the decay estimate in (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}_{\iota\circ\kappa}(x,v)\Lambda_{2}[\Lambda^{\iota}\mathfrak{N}_{\kappa,2}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in P_{1},|\Gamma_{1}|+|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}}2^{-m}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1}(v^{0})^{-1}\Lambda_{1}[v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\Gamma_{2}\Gamma_{1}g^{\alpha\beta}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(4)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.75)

Hence finishing the proof of desired estimate (
3.63
).
∎

3.4.
Estimates of cubic and higher order terms of the metric part

Lemma 3.11
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
), the following estimate holds for any
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}],

l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,

\|L(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}h_{\alpha\beta})-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}Lh_{\alpha\beta}\|_{E_{l}}+\|L\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{wa}]\|_{E_{l}}+\|R_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}(t)\|_{E_{l}}

\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+d(n+4,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.76)

Proof.

Recall (
2.35
), (
1.7
) and (
1.8
). We have

{L}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[(g^{00})^{-1}P_{\alpha\beta}]=\sum_{\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L}\tilde{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(g^{00})^{-1}])\tilde{L}_{2}P_{\alpha\beta}+\sum_{L_{1}\circ L_{2}\circ L_{3}\preceq L}c_{{L}_{2}{L}_{3}}^{{L}_{1}}\Lambda_{\geq 1}[{L}_{1}(g^{\gamma\mu}g^{\lambda\nu})]

\times\big[\big(-\partial_{\alpha}{L}_{2}h_{\mu\lambda}\partial_{\beta}{L}_{3}h_{\gamma\nu}\big)+\frac{1}{2}{L}_{2}\partial_{\alpha}h_{\mu\gamma}\partial_{\beta}{L}_{3}h_{\lambda\nu}\big],

{L}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[(g^{00})^{-1}Q_{\alpha\beta}]=\sum_{\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L}\tilde{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(g^{00})^{-1}])\tilde{L}_{2}Q_{\alpha\beta}-L(\Lambda_{\geq 3}[Q_{\alpha\beta}]),

L(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}h_{\alpha\beta})-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}Lh_{\alpha\beta}=\sum_{L_{1}\circ L_{2}\preceq L,L_{2}\prec L}c_{{L}_{1}{L}_{2}}^{{L}}{L}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}])\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}{L}_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}.

(3.77)

From the volume of support of the output frequency, the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type trilinear estimate, and the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}
type trilinear estimate and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, we know that the following estimate holds after putting the input with the highest order derivative in
L^{2}
,

\begin{split}&\|L(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}h_{\alpha\beta})-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}Lh_{\alpha\beta}\|_{E_{l}}+\|L\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{wa}]\|_{E_{l}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+d(n+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq 0}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+d(n+4,0)\delta_{0}m}\min\{2^{-3m/2},2^{3k/2}2^{-m/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+d(n+4,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.\\
\end{split}

(3.78)

Recall the formula of “
R_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}
” in (
2.46
). Our desired estimate (
3.76
) follows from the above estimate (
3.78
) and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
.
∎

Lemma 3.12
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}],

l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,

\|\Lambda_{\geq 3}[L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}]\|_{E_{l}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.79)

Proof.

Recall (
2.35
). We have

\Lambda_{\geq 3}[L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}]=\sum_{\tilde{L}\preceq L}c_{L\tilde{L}}\tilde{L}\big(-\Lambda_{\geq 3}[T_{\alpha\beta}(f)]+\frac{1}{2}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[g_{\alpha\beta}T(f)]\big),\quad T(f)=g^{\mu\nu}T_{\mu\nu}(f),

\Lambda_{\geq 3}[(g^{00})^{-1}g_{\alpha\beta}T(f)]=\Lambda_{\geq 2}[(g^{00})^{-1}g_{\alpha\beta}]T(f)+\Lambda_{1}[(g^{00})^{-1}g_{\alpha\beta}]\Lambda_{\geq 2}[T(f)]

+\Lambda_{0}[(g^{00})^{-1}g_{\alpha\beta}]\Lambda_{\geq 3}[T(f)].

(3.80)

Since
g_{\alpha\beta}T(f)
and
T_{\alpha\beta}(f)
can be estimated in the same way, we only estimate
T_{\alpha\beta}(f)
in details here. Recall (
1.9
). After doing dyadic decomposition for inputs, for any
\tilde{L}\preceq L
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}
, s.t.,
|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n
, we have

\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{L}T_{\alpha\beta}(f)]=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\mu|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|\\
|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}c^{\tilde{L},\mu}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f(t,x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)dv,

(3.81)

where
c^{\tilde{L},\mu}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}
are some uniquely determined absolute constants whose precise values are not pursued here.

Note that, from the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, and the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, we have

\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N_{0}-n}\|\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\tilde{L}T_{\alpha\beta}(f)]\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq N_{0}-n}\|\langle v\rangle^{-10}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

\times\|\langle v\rangle^{20}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+2e(|L|+4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.82)

Moreover, from the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, we have

\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f(t)\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{\leq k-10}\big(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)P_{k}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

+\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{\leq k-10}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t)P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

+\big(\sum_{|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq 10}\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t)P_{k_{2}}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)^{2}.

(3.83)

Based on the possible size of
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|
and
|L|
, we separate into three cases as follow.

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq|L|-5
From the estimate (
3.4
) in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds

\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)P_{k_{2}}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-3m+e(|L|+4)\delta_{0}m+\delta_{1}k_{1}-2k_{1,+}}\epsilon_{1}\|(1+|v|)^{-10}P_{k_{2}}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(3.84)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\geq|L|-5
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
.   From the estimate (
3.4
) in Lemma
3.1
, the obtained estimate (
3.84
) is also valid if
k_{2}\geq k_{1}-10.
It remains to consider the case

k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
. From the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, and the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, after putting the metric component in
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
, the following estimate holds if
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
,

\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)P_{k_{2}}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+e(|L|+4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{10}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.85)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\geq|L|-5
and
|L|\geq N_{0}-5
.   Note that we have
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq 5
for this case. From the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, and the estimate (
3.54
) in Lemma
3.8
, after putting the metric component in
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
, the following estimate holds if
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
,

\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)P_{k_{2}}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{2}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\mu}\big(\frac{v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}}{v^{0}\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\big)]\big)\big)dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-2m+d(|\mathcal{L}_{2}|+4,|\alpha_{2}|)\delta_{0}m-10k_{2,+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{10}P_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.86)

To sum up, after combining the estimates (
3.83
–
3.86
), we have

\|\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\tilde{L}T_{\alpha\beta}(f)]\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.87)

Combinging the estimates (
3.82
) and (
3.87
), we have

\|\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\tilde{L}T_{\alpha\beta}(f)]\|_{E_{n}}\lesssim\sum_{k\leq-m}2^{(1+\gamma)k}2^{-m/2+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\sum_{k\in[-m,0]\cap\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k/2+\gamma k}2^{-2m+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

+2^{-2m+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+2d(|L|+4,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.88)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
3.79
).
∎

3.5.
Estimates of wave-Vlasov type quadratic terms of the metric part

Recall (
2.58
). In this subsection, we mainly estimate the wave-Vlasov type interaction in
L^{2}
-type space, i.e.,
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}]
and
\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}
. To this end, we first prove some bilinear estimates of general bilinear operators defined as follows.

For any
\mu\in\{+,-\}
,
a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}
,
k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}
, any symbol
m\in\cup_{d\in\mathbb{R}}P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}
, we define the following two operators,

T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\widehat{h}(t,\xi-\eta,v)\widehat{u}(t,\eta,v)

\times m(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)d\eta dv.

(3.89)

Lemma 3.13
.

Assume that
|t|\geq 1,a,b,c,d\in\mathbb{R},k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}
,
m\in P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}
. The following estimate holds,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\|m\|_{P^{d}_{v}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}

\times\min\{\|(1+|x|+|v|)^{d+10}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}|t|^{-2}2^{-2k_{2}}\|(1+|x|+|v|)^{d+10}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\}.

(3.90)

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(U,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}

\times\min\{2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi,v}},\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\times\min\{\|(1+|v|)^{d}\widehat{u}(t,\xi,v)\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{\xi}},\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}|t|^{-2}2^{-2k_{2}}\|(1+|x|+|v|)^{d+10}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\}.

(3.91)

Proof.

Recall (
3.90
). After using the volume of support of
\xi
, the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate, Minkowski inequality, and the volume of support of
\eta
, the following estimates hold,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P^{d}_{v}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\min\{2^{3k/2+3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2},2^{3k_{2}}\}

\times\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}\|(1+|v|)^{d}\widehat{u}(t,\xi,v)\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

(3.92)

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|(1+|v|)^{d}\widehat{u}(t,\xi,v)\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\times\min\big\{2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi,v}},\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}\big\}.

(3.93)

On the other hand, we can do integration by parts in
v
twice. As a result, the following estimates hold,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\min\{2^{3k/2+3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2},2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}\}2^{-2k_{2}}|t|^{-2}\|m\|_{P^{d}_{v}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}

\times\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}\|(1+|x|+|v|)^{d+10}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

(3.94)

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2-2m-2k_{2}}\|(1+|x|+|v|)^{d+10}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\times\min\{2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi,v}},\|\widehat{h}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

(3.95)

Therefore, our desired estimate (
3.90
) holds from the estimates (
3.92
) and (
3.94
) and the desired estimate (
3.91
) holds from the estimates (
3.93
) and (
3.95
).

∎

By exploiting the distance with respect to the light cone, in the following Lemma, we show that we can trade one derivative of the wave part for the decay rate
2^{-m}
over time for the wave-Vlasov type interaction.

Lemma 3.14
.

Assume that
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},a,b,c,d\in\mathbb{R},k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}
,
m\in P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}
,
{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
, then the following estimate holds for the bilinear operator defined in
(
3.89
)
,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{-m}2^{3k_{2}}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\big(2^{-k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}\big(\Gamma h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

+2^{-2k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\partial_{t}h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.96)

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{-m}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}_{-}/2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\big(2^{-k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}\big(\Gamma h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

+2^{-2k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\partial_{t}h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.97)

Proof.

Note that, after applying the equality (
3.72
) for
\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\widehat{h}(t,\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)](t,x,v)
, we have

\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{\xi}[e^{-it|\xi|}T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)](x)=\sum_{\Gamma\in P}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K(x_{1},x_{2},v)u_{k_{2}}(t,x-x_{1}-x_{2}-t\hat{v},v)

\times a_{\Gamma}(t,x-x_{1})\cdot\Gamma\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[-i\xi|\xi|^{-2}\widehat{h}(t,\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)]\big)(t,x-x_{1},v)dx_{1}x_{2}dv,

(3.98)

where

K(x,y,v):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi+iy\cdot\eta}m(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\xi d\eta.

By doing integration by parts in
\xi
and
\eta
many times, we know that the following pointwise estimate holds for the kernel if
k_{2}\leq k_{1}+10
,

|K(x,y,v)|\lesssim\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}(1+|v|)^{d}2^{{3k}+3k_{2}}(1+2^{k}|x|)^{-20/\delta_{1}}(1+2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}|y|)^{-20/\delta_{1}}.

(3.99)

If
k_{2}\geq k_{1}+10
, then we first switch the role of
\xi-\eta
and
\eta
and then do integration by parts in
\xi
and
\eta
many times. As a result, the following estimate holds for the kernel,

|K(x,y,v)|\lesssim\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}(1+|v|)^{d}2^{{3k}+3k_{1}}(1+2^{k}|x+y|)^{-20/\delta_{1}}(1+2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}|y|)^{-20/\delta_{1}}.

(3.100)

Note that the following estimate holds if
{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
,

(1+|t-(x-x_{1})|)(1+|x-x_{1}-x_{2}-t\hat{v}|)(1+2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}(|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|))^{20/\delta_{1}}(1+|v|)^{5/(2\delta_{1})}\geq|t|.

(3.101)

Hence, recall (
3.98
), our desired estimate (
3.96
) follows directly from the above estimate, the estimate (
3.73
), and the estimate of kernel in (
3.99
).

Now, we proceed to the proof of the desired estimate (
3.97
). From the estimates of kernel in (
3.99
) and (
3.100
), we have

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{-m}\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|+|v|^{5/(2\delta_{1})})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\times\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|(1+2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}|z_{2}|)^{10/\delta_{1}}(1+|v|)^{-d}K(z_{1},z_{2},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{1}_{z_{1},z_{2}}}

\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{-m}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|+|v|^{5/(2\delta_{1})})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.102)

Note that, as a result of direct computation, for any
\Gamma\in\{S,\Omega_{ij},L_{i},\partial_{x_{i}}\}
, we have

\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}\big(\Gamma h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|[\Gamma,\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}]\big(h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}\big(\Gamma h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+2^{-2k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\partial_{t}h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(3.103)

Therefore, from the above two estimates, to prove (
3.97
), it would be sufficient to consider the case
\min\{k,k_{2}\}\leq 1
. Based on the relative size of
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
, we split into two cases as follow.

\bullet
If
k_{2}\leq k_{1}+10
.  From the pointwise estimate (
3.101
) and the estimate of kernel
K(z_{1},z_{2},v)
in (
3.99
), we have

|\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{\xi}[e^{-it|\xi|}T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)](x)|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{-m}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|u(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}2^{3k+3k_{2}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+2^{k}|x-y|)^{-10/\delta_{1}}\tilde{m}(t,y,z)dydz\big),

(3.104)

where

\tilde{m}(t,y,z):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}|y-z-t\hat{v}|)^{-10/\delta_{1}}(1+|v|^{10}+|z|^{4})^{-1}dv.

(3.105)

From the estimates (
3.104
) , the following estimate holds from the Minkowski inequality,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{-m}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.106)

\bullet
If
k_{2}\geq k_{1}+10
.  From the estimate (
3.100
) , we have

|\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{\xi}[e^{-it|\xi|}T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)](x)|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}2^{-m}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}2^{3k+3k_{1}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+2^{k}|x-z-t\hat{v}|)^{-10/\delta_{1}}\tilde{m}(t,y,z)dydz\big).

(3.107)

Recall (
3.105
). From the above estimate, the following estimate holds,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{3k/2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}2^{-m}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.108)

To sum up, from the estimate (
3.106
) and the estimate (
3.108
), the following estimate holds regardless the size of
k_{1}-k_{2}
,

\|T^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(h,u)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}2^{ak+bk_{1}+ck_{2}}2^{-m}\|m\|_{P_{v}^{d}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}}\|\Gamma\big(\nabla|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}h\big)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}(u)(t,x,v)(1+|x|^{5}+|v|^{3/\delta_{1}+d})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(3.109)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
3.97
) holds from the estimates (
3.102
), (
3.106
), (
3.103
), and (
3.109
).

∎

In the following Lemma, by using the bilinear estimates obtained in previous Lemmas, we obtain rough energy estimate for the wave-Vlasov type interaction in the Einstein equations.

Lemma 3.15
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}],

l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,

\begin{split}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\|P_{k}(\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}})(t)\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-7m/6+3d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},\\
\|\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}(t)\|_{E_{l}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.110)

Proof.

Recall (
2.58
) and the equation satisfied by the
u^{\mathcal{L}}
in (
2.13
) . Note that

\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)+\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{4}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)+D_{v}\cdot\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)+\mathfrak{Q}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v),

(3.111)

where

\begin{split}\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)&=\sum_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq{\mathcal{L}}}\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\mathcal{L}}(v)\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{L}_{1}(v^{0})^{-1}]\mathcal{L}_{2}f,\\
\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)&=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq{\mathcal{L}}\end{subarray}}{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{3}}^{{\mathcal{L}};j,l}(v)\partial_{j}\mathcal{L}_{1}g^{\alpha\beta}\mathcal{L}_{2}f\mathbf{e}_{l},\\
\mathfrak{Q}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)&=\sum_{\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{n},\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq{\mathcal{L}}}\tilde{c}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\mathcal{L}}(v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{L}_{1}(v^{0})^{-1}]\mathcal{L}_{2}f.\end{split}

(3.112)

By using the same strategy used in obtaining the estimates (
3.62
) and (
3.63
) in Lemma
3.10
, the following estimate holds for any
\rho\in\mathcal{S}
s.t.,
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0},

\begin{split}\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+|\mathcal{L}|+1,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},\\
\sum_{i=2,3}\|\langle v\rangle^{-3}\langle|x|\rangle^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}\mathfrak{Q}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}&\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+d(|\tilde{c}(\rho)|+|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|-|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.113)

From the equalities (
2.13
) and (
3.111
), we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)k^{\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\partial_{t}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\psi_{k}(\xi)dv=I_{k}+II_{k}+III_{k},

where

\begin{split}I_{k}&=\sum_{i=3,4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it|\xi|-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)k^{\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\frac{\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}}]}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|+\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\psi_{k}(\xi)dv,\\
II_{k}&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it|\xi|}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)k^{\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)i\xi\cdot\frac{\widehat{\mathfrak{N}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i\big(|\xi|+\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\psi_{k}(\xi)dv,\\
III_{k}:&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it|\xi|}\big(\widehat{\mathfrak{N}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\big(\frac{{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)k^{\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)}{i\big(|\xi|+\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\big)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv.\end{split}

(3.114)

From the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
, the estimate (
3.63
) in Lemma
3.10
, and the estimate (
3.113
), the following estimate holds from the volume of support of
\xi
if
k\leq 0
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}&\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\big(\big\|I_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\big\|III_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big)\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}2^{-k+3k_{-}/2}\big(2^{-7m/6+3d(|L|+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+2^{-2m+d(|L|+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\big)\\
&\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+3d(|L|+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.115)

Similarly, from the estimate (
3.62
) in Lemma
3.10
and the estimate (
3.113
), the following estimates hold from the volume of support of “
\xi
” if
k\leq 0
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\big\|II_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}2^{3k_{-}/2}\big(\sum_{\rho\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|\leq l,\tilde{c}(\rho)=0}\|\langle v\rangle^{10}\Lambda^{\rho}\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

+\|\langle v\rangle^{10}\mathfrak{R}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.116)

Our desired estimates in (
3.110
) hold from the decomposition (
3.114
) and the estimates (
3.115
) and (
3.116
).
∎

Lemma 3.16
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}],

l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,

\begin{split}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\|\Lambda_{2}[P_{k}(L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl})]\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2},\\
\|\Lambda_{2}[P_{k}(L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl})]\|_{E_{l}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.117)

Proof.

Recall (
2.35
). Since
g_{\alpha\beta}T(f)
can be estimated in the same way as the estimate of
T_{\alpha\beta}(f)
, we only handle
T_{\alpha\beta}(f)
in details here.

Recall the equality (
3.81
). For fixed
\mathcal{L}_{i}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{n},i\in\{1,\cdots,4\},
s.t.,
\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq{L},\mathcal{L}_{3}\circ\mathcal{L}_{4}\preceq\mathcal{L}_{2}
, we do dyadic decomposition for both inputs and have the decomposition as follows,
