P_{k}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{L}_{1}f(t,x,v)\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\mathcal{L}_{4}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\beta}\big(\frac{v_{\mu}v_{\nu}}{v^{0}}\big)\big)]dv\big)=\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\cup_{i=1,2,3}\chi_{k}^{i}}H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k},

where

H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}P_{k}\big[P_{k_{1}}(\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)P_{k_{2}}(\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{L}_{3}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{-\det g}}\big)\mathcal{L}_{4}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\beta}\big(\frac{v_{\mu}v_{\nu}}{v^{0}}\big)\big)])\big]dv.

(3.118)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq|L|-5
.   From the estimate (
3.90
) in Lemma
3.13
, the following estimate holds,

\|H^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\rho\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|\leq N_{0}-(|\mathcal{L}_{1}|+3),|L|\leq|\mathcal{L}_{2}|,|\gamma|\leq 3}2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2-k_{2}-(N_{0}-n+2)k_{1,+}}2^{3k_{1}/2}\min\{1,2^{-2m-2k_{1}}\}

\times\|P_{k_{2}}U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle|x|+|v|\rangle^{20}\Lambda^{\rho}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}f}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

(3.119)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|>|L|-5
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
.   Note that, the following estimate holds from the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate by putting the perturbed metric component in
L^{\infty}
and the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
if
k_{2}\leq k-10
,

\|H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\min\{2^{k_{2}},2^{-m}\}2^{-\gamma k_{2}+H(|\mathcal{L}_{2}|+1)\delta_{1}m+1.1\gamma m-4k_{2,+}}\epsilon_{1}

\times\min\{\|\langle|v|\rangle^{4}P_{k_{1}}(\mathcal{L}_{1}f)(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},2^{3k_{1}/2}\|\langle|v|\rangle^{4}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\}.

(3.120)

If
k_{2}\geq k-10
, then the following estimate holds from the estimate (
3.90
) in Lemma
3.13
,

\|H^{k}_{k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{3k_{1}-k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+H(N_{0})\delta_{0}m}\min\{1,2^{-2m-2k_{1}}\}\epsilon_{1}\|P_{k_{2}}U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.121)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|>|L|-5
and
|L|\geq N_{0}-5
.   For this case we have
l\leq 5
and
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq 5.
Note that the obtained estimate (
3.120
) is also valid if
k_{2}\leq k-10
.

It would be sufficient to consider the case
k_{2}\geq k-10
, i.e.,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}
.
From the volume of support of the output frequency and inputs frequency, the following estimate holds if
{k_{1}}\leq{-99m/100}
,

\|H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{3\min\{k,k_{1}\}/2+3k_{1}/2-k_{2}}\|\widehat{U^{{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k_{2}}(\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\langle v\rangle^{4}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}}(t,\xi,v)\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

(3.122)

If
{k_{1}}\geq{-99m/100}
, then the following estimate holds from the estimate (
3.97
) in Lemma
3.14
and the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
,

\|H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{3k/2-20k_{2,+}}2^{-2m-k_{2}+d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.123)

To sum up, in whichever case, from the above obtained estimates (
3.119
–
3.123
), for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z},
we have

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+lk_{+}}\|H_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.124)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
3.117
).

∎

3.6.
Estimates of wave-wave type quadratic terms of the metric part

In this subsection, we first prove several
bilinear estimates for the wave-wave type interaction and then give a fixed time energy estimate for the wave-wave type quadratic terms of the metric part.

We remark that there are two major differences between the desired bilinear estimates and the general
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type rough bilinear estimate. Firstly, we pay attention to the smallest frequency among the output frequency and the frequencies of the two inputs. Because of the delicate structure of energy spaces at the low frequency part, the gain of the factor
2^{\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}
is crucial. Secondly, we pay attention to the hierarchy of growth rates for different orders of vector fields applied, which is very subtle and also crucial to close the bootstrap argument.

3.6.1.
Set-up of bilinear estimates

For any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,
L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},k\in\mathbb{Z},i\in\{1,2\},
we assume that the following estimates hold,

\begin{split}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}(A_{i}(k,m,L_{1};L_{2}))^{-2}\|R_{\alpha}P_{k}(L_{2}f_{i}^{L_{1}})(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}&\lesssim 1,\quad A_{i}(k,m,L_{1}):=A_{i}(k,m,L_{1};Id),\\
\|\widehat{f_{i}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}&\lesssim C(k,m),\end{split}

(3.125)

where
f_{i}^{L_{1}}
is some input depends on the input
f_{i}
and the vector field
L_{1}
, e.g.,
L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}
,
\widetilde{U^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}}
, and
\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}}
. Moreover, the following hierarchy holds for
A_{i}(k,m,L_{1};L_{2})
,
i\in\{1,2\},

A_{i}(k,m,\tilde{L}_{1};\tilde{L}_{2})\leq A_{i}(k,m,L_{1};L_{2}),\quad\textup{if\,\,}\tilde{L}_{1}\preceq{L}_{1},\quad\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq{L}_{2}.

(3.126)

Given any fixed
m\gg 1,

t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
,
L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n}
, s.t.,
|L_{1}|+|L_{2}|\leq N_{0}
, and any
f,g
satisfy the estimates in (
3.125
),
we define the following bilinear operator,

\begin{split}T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}L_{1}f_{1},R_{\beta}L_{2}f_{2})(t,x)&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})\\
&\times P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(L_{1}f_{1})(x-x_{1})P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}(L_{2}f_{2})(x-x_{1}-x_{2})dx_{1}dx_{2},\end{split}

(3.127)

where the kernel
K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})
is defined as follows,

K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix_{1}\cdot\xi+ix_{2}\cdot\eta}k(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)d\xi d\eta,

(3.128)

and the following estimate holds for the above defined kernlel

\|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})\|_{L^{1}_{x_{1},x_{2}}}\leq C(K).

(3.129)

For any
i\in\{1,2\}
,
L\in\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
, we also define the following quantities for convenience in notation,

\begin{split}D_{i}(m,k)&:=\sum_{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}2^{3k/2}C(k,m)+2^{-m/3-k/3}A_{i}(k,m,Id;\Gamma_{1}\circ\Gamma_{2}\big),\\
C_{i}(k,m,L)&:=\sum_{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}(A_{i}(k,m,L;\Gamma_{1}))^{1-\delta_{1}^{2}}(A_{i}(k,m,L;\Gamma_{1}\circ\Gamma_{2}))^{\delta_{1}^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.130)

With the above notation, from the estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, the following estimate holds if
k\geq-m
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-2
,

\begin{split}\|R_{\alpha}P_{k}(f_{i}^{L})(t,x)\psi_{\geq m-10}(|x|)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+k/2}C_{i}(k,m,L),\\
\|R_{\alpha}P_{k}(f_{i})(t,x)\psi_{\geq m-10}(|x|)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+k/2}D_{i}(m,k).\end{split}

(3.131)

3.6.2.
Bilinear estimates

In the following Lemma, based on the possible room of allowing maximum number of vector fields, we provide several
L^{2}
-type bilinear estimates for the bilinear operator defined in previous subsubsection.

Lemma 3.17
.

The following estimate holds for the bilinear form defined in
(
3.127
)
,

\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.132)

Moreover, if
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
and the kernel
K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})
satisfies the following estimate,

\quad\|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})\psi_{\geq-m+\delta_{1}m}(|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|)\|_{L^{1}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\cap L^{\infty}_{x_{1},x_{2}}}\leq 2^{-10m}C(K).

(3.133)

Then then following estimate holds if
|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-2
,
\star\in\{\leq m-10,\geq m+10\}
,

\begin{split}&\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\psi_{\star}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\\
&\times\min\big\{\sum_{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in P_{1}}2^{-2m-2k_{1}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1};\Gamma_{1}\circ\Gamma_{2})+2^{-m-3k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&+2^{-2m-4k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square\Gamma_{1}f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}},\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}2^{-m-k_{1}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1};\Gamma)+2^{-m-3k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}}\big\}.\end{split}

(3.134)

If
|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-1
and
|L_{2}|\leq N_{0}-1
, then the following estimate holds for
\star\in\{\leq m-10,\geq m+10\}
,

\begin{split}&\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\psi_{\star}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim\prod_{i=1,2}2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)\big(\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}2^{-m-k_{1}}A_{i}(k_{i},m,L_{i};\Gamma)+2^{-m-3k_{i}}\|P_{k_{i}}(\square f_{i}^{L_{i}})\|_{L^{2}}\big).\end{split}

(3.135)

Moreover, for the close to the light cone case, the following rough estimate holds,

\begin{split}&\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m+\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)C_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).\end{split}

(3.136)

In particular, if
|L_{1}|=0
, then we have

\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-m+\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)D_{1}(m,k_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.137)

Proof.

Recall (
3.127
) and the estimate of kernel in (
3.129
). For fixed
x_{1},x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we use the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate and the
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding. As a result, we have

\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.138)

Alternatively, the following estimate holds from first using the volume of support of
\xi
and then using the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate,

\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{3k/2}C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.139)

Hence the desired estimate (
3.132
) holds from the estimates (
3.138
) and (
3.139
).

Now we proceed to prove our desired estimates (
3.134
) and (
3.135
), which are the the far away from the light cone cases.
From the estimate of kernel in (
3.133
), we can restrict ourself to the case
|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\leq 2^{m-\delta_{1}m}
. More precisely, by using the same strategy used in obtaining the estimate (
3.132
), we have

\big|\int_{|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\geq 2^{m-\delta_{1}m}}K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})P_{k_{1}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}})(x-x_{1})P_{k_{2}}(R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(x-x_{1}-x_{2})dx_{1}dx_{2}\big|

\lesssim 2^{-10m+3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.140)

Hence
x-x_{1}
and
x-x_{1}-x_{2}
are also localized away from the light cone if
|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\leq 2^{m-\delta_{1}m}
.
From the equality (
3.72
), the following equalities hold if
|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-1
,

\begin{split}P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(f_{1}^{L_{1}})(t,x)&=\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}-a_{\Gamma}(t,x)\cdot\Gamma\nabla_{x}|\nabla|^{-2}(P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(f_{1}^{L_{1}})),\quad\\
\Gamma\nabla_{x}|\nabla|^{-2}(P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(f_{1}^{L_{1}}))&=T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,1}\partial_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}+\nabla_{x}|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(\Gamma f_{1}^{L_{1}})+T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}\partial_{t}^{2}f_{1}^{L_{1}},\end{split}

(3.141)

where
T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,1}\in\mathcal{S}^{-2},T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}\in\mathcal{S}^{-3}
are some uniquely determined Fourier multiplier operator such that
T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}=0
if
\alpha\neq 0.

If
|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-2
, then we can do the process of trading derivatives for vector fields one more time in (
3.141
). As a result, we have

\begin{split}\nabla_{x}|\nabla|^{-2}P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}(\Gamma f_{1}^{L_{1}})&+T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,1}\partial_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}+T_{\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}\Delta f_{1}^{L_{1}}=\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in P}-a_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(t,x)\big[\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,1}\partial_{\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}\Gamma f_{1}^{L_{1}})\\
&+\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}\partial_{\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}f_{1}^{L_{1}})+\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,3}\partial_{t}^{2}(\Gamma f_{1}^{L_{1}})+\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,4}\partial_{t}^{2}(f_{1}^{L_{1}})\big],\end{split}

(3.142)

where
\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,1},\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,2}\in\mathcal{S}^{-3},\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,3},\tilde{T}_{\tilde{\Gamma},\Gamma,k_{1}}^{\alpha,4}\in\mathcal{S}^{-4}
.
Therefore, by using the strategy used in obtaining the estimate (
3.132
), our desired estimate (
3.134
) holds from the equalities in (
3.141
) and (
3.142
) and the estimates (
3.72
) and
(
3.125
).

If
|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-1
and
|L_{2}|\leq N_{0}-1
, we are allowed to trade the space regularity for the decay rate of the distance with respect to the light cone for both inputs
f_{1}^{L_{1}}
and
f_{2}^{L_{2}}
. As a result, our desired estimate (
3.134
) holds after using the equalities in (
3.135
) for both “
f_{1}^{L_{1}}
” and “
f_{2}^{L_{2}}
” and rerun the argument used in obtaining the estimate (
3.132
).

Now, we focus on proof of the desired estimates (
3.136
) and (
3.137
). Since they can be proved similalry, we first prove the more difficult estimate (
3.136
) and then briefly explain how to obtain the other desired estimate (
3.137
).

Note that the estimate (
3.140
) is still valid. Hence, it would be sufficient to restrict ourself to the case
x_{1},x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
are fixed such that
|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\leq 2^{m-\delta_{1}m}
.
Note that, the following estimate holds from the estimate (
3.131
) and the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate

\big|\int_{|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\leq 2^{m-\delta_{1}m}}K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})P_{k_{1}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}})(x-x_{1})P_{k_{2}}(R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(x-x_{1}-x_{2})\psi_{>m-10}(x)dx_{1}dx_{2}\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{1}/2}C(K)C_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(3.143)

Hence finishing the proof of desired estimate (
3.136
) if
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1},k\geq k_{1}-10
.

It remains to consider the case
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}
and the case
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1},k\leq k_{1}-10
. Due to the symmetric structure in
L^{2}
, the strategy used in the case
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},
i.e.,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
is also applicable for the case
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1},k\leq k_{1}-10
, it would be sufficient to consider the case when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}
, i.e.,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
.

To take advantage of the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, we localize further the frequency of
f_{1}^{L_{1}}

and have the following decomposition,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2})P_{k_{1}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}})(x-x_{1})P_{k_{2}}(R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(x-x_{1}-x_{2})\psi_{\geq m-20}(x-x_{1})dx_{1}dx_{2}

=\sum_{n\in[2^{k_{1}-k_{2}-10},2^{k_{1}-k_{2}+10}]\cap\mathbb{Z}}I_{n}^{1}+I_{n}^{2},

where

I_{n}^{1}:=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z},|l-n|\leq 10}T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(P_{k_{1},n}(R_{\alpha}P_{k_{1},l}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\psi_{\geq m-20}(\cdot)),R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x),

(3.144)

I_{n}^{2}:=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z},|l-n|>10}T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(P_{k_{1},n}(R_{\alpha}P_{k_{1},l}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\psi_{\geq m-20}(\cdot)),R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)

(3.145)

where the Fourier multiplier operator
P_{k_{1},n},n\in\mathbb{Z}
, are defined by the Fourier symbol
\psi_{\leq k_{2}}(|\xi|-2^{k_{2}}n)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)
.

From the estimate (
3.140
) and the decomposition in (
3.144
) and the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}},R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-20m+3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}}\big(C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\big)^{2}+\sum_{i,j\in\{1,2\}}I_{i,j},\end{split}

(3.146)

where

I_{i,j}:=\sum_{n,n^{\prime}\in[2^{k_{1}-k_{2}-10},2^{k_{1}-k_{2}+10}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|n-n^{\prime}|\leq 10}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{I_{n}^{i}}I_{n^{\prime}}^{j}dx.

(3.147)

From the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate and the super localized decay estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, the following rough estimate holds for
I_{n}^{1}
,

\begin{split}\big\|I_{n}^{1}\big\|_{L^{2}}&\lesssim\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z},|l-n|\leq 10}C(K)\|P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}(f_{2}^{L_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{1},l}R_{\alpha}(f_{1}^{L_{1}})\psi_{\geq m-20}(|x|)\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 2,|\kappa|\leq 1}2^{-m+k_{2}/2}C(K)A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\big[\|P_{k_{1},n}\big(R_{\alpha}\Omega^{\gamma}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\big)\|_{L^{2}}^{\delta_{1}^{2}}\|P_{k_{1},n}\big(R_{\alpha}\Omega^{\kappa}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\big)\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\delta_{1}^{2}}\big].\end{split}

(3.148)

From the orthogonality in
L^{2}
and the estimate (
3.148
) , we have

\begin{split}\big|I_{1,1}\big|&\lesssim\sum_{n,n^{\prime}\in[2^{k_{1}-k_{2}-10},2^{k_{1}-k_{2}+10}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|n-n^{\prime}|\leq 10}\|I_{n}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|I_{n^{\prime}}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim\Big(\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 2,|\kappa|\leq 1}C(K)2^{-m+k_{2}/2}A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\|P_{k_{1}}\big(R_{\alpha}\Omega^{\gamma}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\big)\|_{L^{2}}^{\delta_{1}^{2}}\|P_{k_{1}}\big(R_{\alpha}\Omega^{\kappa}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\big)\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\delta_{1}^{2}}\Big)^{2}\\
&\lesssim\big(C(K)2^{-m+k_{2}/2}A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})C_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})\big)^{2}.\end{split}

(3.149)

Now, we estimate the case
I_{i,j},i,j\neq 1
. Note that the symbol
\psi_{\leq k_{2}}(|\xi|-2^{k_{2}}l)\psi_{\leq k_{2}}(|\xi-\sigma|-2^{k_{2}}n)
vanishes if
|\sigma|\leq 2^{k_{2}}|l-n|/5
and
|l-n|\geq 10
. As a result, we have

P_{k_{1},n}(R_{\alpha}P_{k_{1},l}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\psi_{\geq m-20}(\cdot))(x)=2^{m}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\widehat{R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}}(\xi-\sigma)\psi_{k_{1}}(|\xi|)\psi_{\leq k_{2}}(|\xi|-2^{k_{2}}n)\\
\times\psi_{k_{1}}(|\xi-\sigma|)\psi_{\leq k_{2}}(|\xi-\sigma|-2^{k_{2}}l)\hat{\psi}(2^{m}|\sigma|)\psi_{\geq-1}(2^{-k_{2}}5|l-n|^{-1}\sigma)d\xi d\sigma.

(3.150)

Since
\hat{\psi}
is a Schwarz function and
k_{2}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m+10
, from the rapidly decay of Schwarz function, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\hat{\psi}(2^{m}|\sigma|)\big|\psi_{\geq-1}(2^{-k_{2}}5|l-n|^{-1}\sigma)d\sigma\lesssim 2^{-100m}|l-n|^{-2}.

(3.151)

From the above estimate (
3.151
), the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type estimate, and the
L^{\infty}-L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, the following estimate holds for any
n\in 2^{k_{1}-k_{2}}[2^{-10},2^{10}]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,

\big\|I_{n}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}(1+|l-n|)^{-2}2^{3k_{2}/2}2^{-90m}C(K)\|P_{k_{1},l}R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.152)

From the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type estimate and the Sobolev embedding, the following rough estimate holds of
I_{n}^{1}
,

\big\|I_{n}^{1}\big\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}(1+|l-n|)^{-2}2^{3k_{2}/2}C(K)\|P_{k_{1},l}R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.153)

Recall (
3.146
). From the above estimates (
3.152
) and (
3.153
) the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i,j\in\{1,2\},i,j\neq 1}\big|I_{i,j}\big|\lesssim\sum_{n,n^{\prime},l_{1},l_{2}\in[2^{k_{1}-k_{2}-10},2^{k_{1}-k_{2}+10}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|n-n^{\prime}|\leq 10}2^{3k_{2}}2^{-90m}(C(K))^{2}\\
&\times(1+|l_{2}-n|)^{-2}(1+|l_{1}-n|)^{-2}\|P_{k_{1},l_{1}}R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{1},l_{2}}R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim 2^{3k_{2}}(C(K))^{2}2^{-90m}\|P_{k_{1}}R_{\alpha}f_{1}^{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|P_{k_{2}}R_{\beta}f_{2}^{L_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\big(2^{-45m+3k_{2}/2}C(K)A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\big)^{2}.\end{split}

(3.154)

Hence our desired estimate (
3.136
) holds from the estimates (
3.146
), (
3.149
), and (
3.154
).

With minor modification, our desired estimate (
3.137
) holds after rerunning the above argument with the first decay estimate in (
3.131
) replaced with the second decay estimate in (
3.131
).
∎

3.6.3.
Estimate of the wave-wave type quadratic terms

In this subsection, we use the bilinear estimates obtained in previous subsubsection to estimate the wave-wave type quadratic terms of the metric part. More precisely, we have

Lemma 3.18
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
2.88
) and (
2.91
), for any
L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
k\in\mathbb{Z},

K\in\{S,Q,P\}
, we have

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}(\Lambda_{2}[{L}K_{\alpha\beta}])(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}(2^{H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}+2^{k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}),

(3.155)

\|\Lambda_{2}[{L}K_{\alpha\beta}](t)\|_{E_{l-1}}\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m-5\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.156)

Moreover, for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, we have

\begin{split}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}&\|P_{k}(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\square Lh_{\alpha\beta}])(t)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\\
\end{split}

(3.157)

Proof.

Recall (
2.72
). For any
L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,
t\subset[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
, we define

\begin{split}X_{L}^{k}(t)&:=\sum_{K\in\{S,Q,P\}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}(\Lambda_{2}[{L}K_{\alpha\beta}])(t)\|_{L^{2}},\\
X_{L}(t)&:=2^{-d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}X_{L}^{k}(t).\end{split}

(3.158)

Recall the decomposition of
L(K_{\alpha\beta}),K\in\{S,Q,P\}
, in (
2.72
), the bootstrap assumption (
2.88
) and the definition of bounds in (
3.125
) and (
3.130
). For any
L,L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
s.t.,
L_{1}\circ L_{2}\in\cup_{n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{n}
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
, from the estimate (
3.16
), the corresponding quantities for the perturbed metric
h_{\alpha\beta}
are defined as follows,

\begin{split}A_{i}(k,m,L_{1};L_{2})&:=2^{k_{-}/2-k-\gamma k_{-}-(\|L_{1}\|+\|L_{2}\|-|L_{1}|-|L_{2}|)k_{+}+d(|L_{1}|+|L_{2}|,\|L_{1}\|+\|L_{2}\|-|L_{1}|-|L_{2}|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1},\\
C_{i}(k,m,L)&:=2^{-k/2-\gamma k_{-}-(N_{0}-|L|-2)k_{+}}2^{-(1-\delta)k_{+}}2^{(1-\delta_{1}^{2})H(|L|+1)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}^{2}H(|L|+2)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1},\\
D_{i}(k,m)&:=\big(2^{k/2-ak}2^{\delta m}+2^{-m/3+k/6-\gamma k_{-}+H(2)\delta_{1}m-(N_{0}-4)k_{+}}\big)\epsilon_{1},\end{split}

(3.159)

where
\|L\|,
and
|L|
are defined in Definition
2.1
.

For
L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},\Gamma\in\{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\Gamma_{3}:\Gamma_{1}\in P_{1},\Gamma_{2},\Gamma_{3}\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}\}
, the following quantities will be associated with the modified profiles
\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t,x)
and
\widetilde{U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}}(t,x)
defined in (
2.64
),

\widetilde{A}_{i}(k,m,L;\Gamma):=2^{k_{-}/2-k-\gamma k_{-}-(N_{0}-|L|-|\Gamma|)k_{+}+H(|L|+|\Gamma|))\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.160)

Again, thanks to the estimate (
3.16
), the modified profiles are not different much with the original profiles if the total vector fields are not very close to the top order.

Recall (
2.36
). From the estimate (
3.76
) in Lemma
3.11
, the estimate (
3.79
) in Lemma
3.12
, and the estimate (
3.117
) in Lemma
3.16
, the following estimate holds for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,
L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n}
,

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\square Lh_{\alpha\beta}]\big)\|_{L^{2}}

\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+d(n+4,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+X_{L}^{k}(t)+2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.161)

With the above preparation, based on the possible size of
k,k_{1},k_{2}
, we separate into two cases as follow.

\bullet
If
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
, i.e.,
|k_{1}-k_{2}|\geq 10,|k-\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}|\leq 10
.

From the estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds for any
Q\in\{S,P,Q\}
if
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\delta_{1}m
,

\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2+|k_{1}-k_{2}|}\|U^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|U^{L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{2}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.162)

Now we consider the case
-m+\delta_{1}m\leq\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq k+10.
From the estimate (
3.134
) in Lemma
3.17
and the estimate (
3.161
), the following estimate holds for any
H\in\{S,P,Q\}
one of the following two scenarios happens: (i)
|L_{1}|\leq|L|-3
; (ii)
|L_{1}|\geq|L|-3,|L|\leq N_{0}-5
, and
k_{1}\leq k_{2}
,

\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{\leq m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}2^{2k-k_{2}}2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|U^{L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{2}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\big[2^{-m-k_{1}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,\Gamma\circ L_{1})

+2^{-m+k_{1,-}/2-\gamma k_{1,-}-(l-1)k_{1,+}}\big(2^{-3k_{1}}X_{L_{1}}^{k_{1}}(t)+2^{-3k_{1}/2}\epsilon_{1}+2^{-k_{1}/2}2^{H(|L_{1}|)\delta_{0}m}+2^{-m/2+k_{1,-}-3k_{1}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\big)\big].

(3.163)

Moreover, from the estimate (
3.136
) in Lemma
3.17
, if
|L_{1}|\leq|L|-3
, we have

\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{2k-k_{2}}2^{-m+\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})\|U^{L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{2}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.164)

Similarly, if either
|L_{1}|\geq|L|-3,|L|\leq N_{0}-5
,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}
or
|L_{1}|\geq|L|-3,|L|\geq N_{0}-5
, then the following two estimates hold analogously,

\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{\leq m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{1}}2^{2k-k_{1}}2^{3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|U^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\big[2^{-m-k_{2}}A_{1}(k_{2},m,\Gamma\circ L_{2})

+2^{-m+k_{2,-}/2-\gamma k_{2,-}-(l-1)k_{2,+}}\big(2^{-3k_{2}}X_{L_{2}}^{k_{2}}(t)+2^{-3k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}+2^{-k_{2}/2}2^{d(|L_{2}|,l)\delta_{0}m}+2^{-m/2+k_{2,-}-3k_{2}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\big)\big].

(3.165)

\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{2k-k_{1}}2^{-m+\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}C_{1}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\|U^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}_{k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}.

(3.166)

From the obtained estimates (
3.162
–
3.166
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{H\in\{S,P,Q\}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(l-1)k_{+}}\big(\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)^{2}

\lesssim\big(\sup_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-\delta_{1}m/2}2^{d(|L_{2}|,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}X^{k_{1}}_{L_{1}}(t)\big)^{2}+2^{-2m+2d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m-10\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(3.167)

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m-5\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sup_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-\delta_{1}m/2+k_{-}}2^{d(|L_{2}|,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}X^{k_{1}}_{L_{1}}(t).

(3.168)

\bullet
If
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}
, i.e.,
|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq 10
.

For the High
\times
High type interaction, due to symmetry, we assume that
|{L}_{1}|\leq|{L}_{2}|
. Note that
|{L}_{1}|\leq N_{0}/2+5.
Recall the decomposition of
QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}
in (
2.79
) and we use formulations in (
2.80
) and (
2.81
). Since the error type is zero in this formulation, it would be sufficient to estimate
QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)
and
QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{vl;L,L_{1},L_{2}},H\in\{S,P,Q\}.

\oplus
The estimate of
QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{vl;L,L_{1},L_{2}}
.

Recall (
2.81
). Note that, if
k_{1}\leq-m/10
, then we can gain extra
2^{-m/10}
from the symbol of
{THVl}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\alpha\beta;\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}
, which is more than sufficient to cover the gap of difference between
2^{H(|L|)\delta_{1}m}
and
2^{d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}
. After we redo the argument used in obtaining the estimates (
3.162
–
3.164
), we obtain the following estimate,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1},k_{1}\leq-m/10}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{vl;L,L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m/40}X_{L_{1}}(t)\epsilon_{1}+2^{-m-m/40}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.169)

It remains to consider the case
k_{1}\geq-m/10
. From the estimate (

(3.170)

and

3.8.
Estimates of the error terms in the double Hodge decomposition

For the commutator term
E_{\mu}
, which was defined in (
2.40
), the following basic
L^{\infty}
-type estimate and the
L^{2}
-type estimate hold .

Lemma 3.19
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
L\in P_{n},n\in[0,N_{0}]\cap\mathbb{Z},l\in[0,N_{0}-n]\cap\mathbb{Z}
,
\tilde{L}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}-5}P_{n}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\|\tilde{L}E_{\mu}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+H(|\tilde{L}|+4)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.185)

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}(\partial_{\nu}(LE_{\mu}))(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.186)

Proof.

Recall (
2.40
). From the harmonic gauge condition (
1.5
), we have

E_{\mu}=\Lambda_{1}[\Gamma_{\mu}]=-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\Gamma_{\mu}]=-\Lambda_{\geq 1}[g^{\alpha\beta}]\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\mu}h_{\alpha\beta}\big)=E_{\mu}^{1}+E_{\mu}^{2},\\
E_{\mu}^{1}=-\Lambda_{1}[g^{\alpha\beta}]\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\mu}h_{\alpha\beta}\big),\qquad E_{\mu}^{2}=-\Lambda_{\geq 2}[g^{\alpha\beta}]\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\mu}h_{\alpha\beta}\big).

(3.187)

From the above decomposition, our desired estimate (
3.185
) holds from the
L^{\infty}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate, the
L^{\infty}
-type estimate in Lemma
3.3
, and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
.

Note that
\partial_{\nu}(LE_{\mu}^{1})
is a linear combination of same type of quadratic terms
LH_{\alpha\beta},H\in\{S,P,Q\}
. Similar to the obtained estimate (
3.156
), by using the same argument, the following estimate holds for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}(\partial_{\nu}(LE_{\mu}^{1}))(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-m+k_{-}+d(|L|+1,l)\delta_{0}m-5\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.188)

Note that the following estimates from the
L^{\infty}-L^{2}
type estimate and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
,

\sum_{|\alpha|\leq l-1}\|\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{\nu}(LE_{\mu}^{2})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+2d(n+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

\sum_{|\alpha|\leq l-1}\|\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{\nu}(LE_{\mu}^{2})\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+2d(n+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

From the above estimate, we have

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+(l-1)k_{+}}\|P_{k}\big(LE_{\mu}^{2}\big)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sup_{k\leq-2m/3}2^{k+\gamma k}2^{-m/2+2d(n+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

+\sup_{k\geq-2m/3}2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}2^{-3m/2+2d(n+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+2d(n+3,l)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(3.189)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
3.186
) holds from (
3.188
) and (
3.189
).
∎

In the following Lemma, we show that commutators
E_{L,0}^{comm}
and
R_{k}E_{L,k}^{comm}
,
L\in P_{1}
, which appears in (
2.42
) and (
2.43
), decay sharply over time.

Lemma 3.20
.

Under the bootstrap assumption
(
2.88
)
, the following estimate holds for any
L\in P,|L|=1
,
k\in\mathbb{Z},t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],

\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}\|\nabla_{x_{\alpha}}^{\gamma}P_{k}(E^{comm}_{{L},0})(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x_{\alpha}}^{\gamma}P_{k}(R_{i}E^{comm}_{{L},i})(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+0.999k-5k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}+2^{-2m+d(4,0)\delta_{0}m-k}.

(3.190)

Proof.

We first show that the commutators
E_{L,0}^{comm}
and
R_{k}E_{L,k}^{comm}
,
L\in P_{1}
, don’t depend on the bad component
\underline{F}.
Recall (
2.40
). As a result of direct computations, we have

E^{comm}_{{S},0}=-[\partial_{t},S]h_{00}+[\partial_{i},S]h_{0i}+\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},S]h_{00}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},S]h_{ii}=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}h_{00}+\partial_{i}h_{0i}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}h_{ii}

=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}(F+\underline{F})+\partial_{i}(-R_{i}\rho+\epsilon_{ikl}R_{k}\omega_{l})-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}R_{i}R_{i}(F-\underline{F})

-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\big(-\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}+\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}\Omega_{m}+\varepsilon_{ipm}\varepsilon_{iqn}R_{p}R_{q}\vartheta_{mn}\big)

=\partial_{i}\epsilon_{ikl}R_{k}\omega_{l}+|\nabla|(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F})-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\big(-\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}+\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}\Omega_{m}+\varepsilon_{ipm}\varepsilon_{iqn}R_{p}R_{q}\vartheta_{mn}\big),

(3.191)

E^{comm}_{L_{j},0}=-[\partial_{t},L_{j}]h_{00}+[\partial_{i},L_{j}]h_{0i}+\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},L_{j}]h_{00}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},L_{j}]h_{ii}=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}h_{00}+\partial_{t}h_{0j}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}h_{ii}

=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}(F+\underline{F})+\partial_{t}(-R_{j}\rho+\varepsilon_{jkl}R_{k}\omega_{l})-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}R_{i}R_{i}(F-\underline{F})

-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}\big(-\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}+\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}\Omega_{m}+\varepsilon_{ipm}\varepsilon_{iqn}R_{p}R_{q}\vartheta_{mn}\big)

=-\partial_{j}\underline{F}-\partial_{t}R_{j}(R_{0}\underline{F})-\partial_{t}R_{j}(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F})+\varepsilon_{jkl}\partial_{t}R_{k}\omega_{l}

-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}\big(-\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}+\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}\Omega_{m}+\varepsilon_{ipm}\varepsilon_{iqn}R_{p}R_{q}\vartheta_{mn}\big)=-|\nabla|^{-1}R_{j}\square\underline{F}-\partial_{t}R_{j}(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F})

+\varepsilon_{jkl}\partial_{t}R_{k}\omega_{l}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}\big(-\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}+\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}\Omega_{m}+\varepsilon_{ipm}\varepsilon_{iqn}R_{p}R_{q}\vartheta_{mn}\big),

(3.192)

E^{comm}_{\Omega_{ij},0}=-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},\Omega_{ij}]h_{00}+[\partial_{k},\Omega_{ij}]h_{0k}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{t},\Omega_{ij}]h_{kk}=[\partial_{k},\Omega_{ij}]h_{0k}=\partial_{j}h_{0i}-\partial_{i}h_{0j}

=\partial_{j}(h_{0i}+R_{i}R_{0}\underline{F})-\partial_{i}(h_{0j}+R_{j}R_{0}\underline{F})

(3.193)

E^{comm}_{{S},k}=-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},S]h_{00}+[\partial_{i},S]h_{ki}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},S]h_{ii}=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}h_{00}+\partial_{x_{i}}h_{ki}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}h_{ii}

=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}(F+\underline{F})-\partial_{x_{i}}R_{k}R_{i}\underline{F}+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F}+\partial_{x_{i}}\big(h_{ki}+R_{k}R_{i}\underline{F}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}\big(h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F}\big)

=-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}F+\partial_{x_{i}}\big(h_{ki}+R_{k}R_{i}\underline{F}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{k}}\big(h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F}\big)

E^{comm}_{L_{j},k}=-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},L_{j}]h_{00}+[\partial_{i},L_{j}]h_{ki}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},L_{j}]h_{ii}=-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}h_{00}+\partial_{t}h_{kj}-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}h_{ii}

=-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}(F+\underline{F})-\partial_{t}R_{k}R_{j}\underline{F}+\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F}+\partial_{t}(h_{kj}+R_{k}R_{j}\underline{F})-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}(h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F})

Therefore,

R_{k}E^{comm}_{L_{j},k}=R_{k}\big(-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}F+\partial_{t}(h_{kj}+R_{k}R_{j}\underline{F})-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{kj}\partial_{t}(h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F})\big).

Similarly, we have

E^{comm}_{\Omega_{mn},k}=-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},\Omega_{mn}]h_{00}+[\partial_{i},\Omega_{mn}]h_{ki}-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{k}},\Omega_{mn}]h_{ii}

=-\frac{1}{2}\big(\delta_{mk}\partial_{x_{n}}-\delta_{nk}\partial_{x_{m}}\big)(h_{00}-h_{ii})+\partial_{x_{n}}h_{km}-\partial_{x_{m}}h_{kn}=-\big(\delta_{mk}\partial_{x_{n}}-\delta_{nk}\partial_{x_{m}}\big)\underline{F}

-\frac{1}{2}\big(\delta_{mk}\partial_{x_{n}}-\delta_{nk}\partial_{x_{m}}\big)(F-h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F})+\partial_{x_{n}}(h_{km}-R_{k}R_{m}\underline{F})-\partial_{x_{m}}(h_{kn}-R_{k}R_{n}\underline{F})).

Therefore,

R_{k}E^{comm}_{\Omega_{mn},k}=R_{k}\big(-\frac{1}{2}\big(\delta_{mk}\partial_{x_{n}}-\delta_{nk}\partial_{x_{m}}\big)(F-h_{ii}+R_{i}R_{i}\underline{F})+\partial_{x_{n}}(h_{km}-R_{k}R_{m}\underline{F})-\partial_{x_{m}}(h_{kn}-R_{k}R_{n}\underline{F}))\big).

Therefore, our desired estimate (
3.190
) follows from the decay estimate of the metric componet in (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
for the far away from the light cone case, the estimate (
3.185
) in Lemma
3.19
, the decay estimates (
3.7
) and (
3.8
) in Lemma
3.2
and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
.
∎

3.9.
An improved estimate for the modified profile at low frequency

In the following Lemma, we show relation between
P_{k}\big[\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}}}(t)\big]
and
\Gamma\big(P_{k}\big[\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big]\big)(t)
, which helps us to rule out the very low frequency case in the energy estimate of the metric part.

Lemma 3.21
.

For any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}],

\Gamma\in P,L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}-1}P_{n},k\in\mathbb{Z}
, the following equality holds for the modified perturbed metric defined in
(
2.64
)
,

P_{k}\big[\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}}}(t)\big]=\Gamma\big(P_{k}\big[\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big]\big)(t)+\sum_{L_{1}\preceq L}P_{k}R_{\Gamma;\alpha,\beta}^{\mu}\big({U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}}(t)\big)^{\mu}+Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)+Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t),

(3.194)

where
R_{\Gamma;\alpha,\beta}^{\mu}
are some zero order Fourier multiplier operator, “
Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
” represents the error terms depend only with respect to the vlasov part and
Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
is a quadratic and higher order terms involve the perturbed meteric, see
(
3.214
)
for their detailed formulas.
Moreover, the following general formulation holds for
Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
,

Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)=\sum_{\mathcal{L}\preceq L\circ\Gamma}\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)m_{\mathcal{L}}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t,\xi,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)dv\big](x),

(3.195)

where
m_{\mathcal{L}}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t,\xi,v)\in\langle v\rangle^{10}L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{-1}
.

Proof.

Recall the definitions of profiles in (
2.57
) and (
2.47
) and the double Hodge decompositions in (
2.37
). From (
2.64
), we have

\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}=R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}}(Lh_{\alpha\beta})+\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\mathcal{L}\preceq L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{\mu k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{2|\xi|\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu},

(3.196)

\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}=R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}}(\partial_{t}Lh_{\alpha\beta})+\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\mathcal{L}\preceq L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu},

(3.197)

where
R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}}\in\{R_{i},R_{i}R_{j}\}\times\mathbb{C}
. Therefore, from the equality (
3.196
), we have

\begin{split}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}})&=\Gamma\big(P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)+[P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}},\Gamma](Lh_{\alpha\beta})\\
&-\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\},\mathcal{L}\preceq L}\Gamma\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{\mu k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}\\
&+\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\},\tilde{\mathcal{L}}\preceq\Gamma\circ L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{\Gamma L\tilde{\mathcal{L}};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{\mu k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}}}(t,\xi,v)}{2|\xi|\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}.\end{split}

(3.198)

Note that the following equality holds for some uniquely determined operators
R_{\alpha\beta;0}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\in\mathcal{S}^{0}
and
R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\in\mathcal{S}^{-1}
,

[P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}},\Gamma]=P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;0}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\partial_{t},\quad[\partial_{t},\Gamma]=c_{\Gamma,\alpha}\partial_{\alpha},

(3.199)

where

R_{\alpha\beta;0}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\in\mathcal{S}^{0},\quad R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\in\mathcal{S}^{-1},\quad R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}=0,\quad\textup{if}\quad\Gamma\notin\{L_{i}\}.

(3.200)

Following a similar procedure, from (
3.197
), we have

\begin{split}P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}})&=\Gamma P_{k}\big(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}}[\partial_{t},\Gamma](Lh_{\alpha\beta})+[P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}},\Gamma](\partial_{t}Lh_{\alpha\beta})\\
&-\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\},\mathcal{L}_{1}\preceq L}\Gamma\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}_{1};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}\\
&+\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\},\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\Gamma\circ L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}.\end{split}

(3.201)

We now consider the case when the vector field hits the density type term. Recall (
3.201
).
Note that

\begin{split}\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(x-y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,y,v)dydv,\\
K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(x,v)&:={b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}d\xi.\end{split}

(3.202)

As a result of direct computations, we have

\Omega_{ij}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(y_{i}\partial_{j}-y_{j}\partial_{i})K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)

+K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\Omega_{ij}\mathcal{L})f(t,x-y,v)+\big(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}\big)K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)\big)dydv.

(3.203)

S\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)

=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)S\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)+y\cdot\nabla_{y}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv.

(3.204)

L_{i}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)

=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\mathcal{L}_{i}\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)+\partial_{v_{i}}\big(\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\big)\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)

-y_{i}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)+y_{i}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv.

(3.205)

To sum up, from the equalities (
3.203
), (
3.204
), and (
3.205
), we have

\Gamma\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]=\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}(y,v)

\times\tilde{\Gamma}\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv+C_{\Gamma}^{L_{i}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}y_{i}K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big),

(3.206)

where
\mathcal{F}_{x}[K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}](\xi,v)\in L^{\infty}_{\langle v\rangle^{-10}}\mathcal{S}^{-1}
and
C_{\Gamma}^{L_{i}}\in\mathbb{R}
,
i\in\{1,2,3\}
, are some uniquely determined constants. Similarly, we have

\Gamma\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2|\xi|\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]=\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{K}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}(y,v)\tilde{\Gamma}\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)

+C_{\Gamma}^{L_{i}}y_{i}\tilde{K}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv,

(3.207)

\tilde{K}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(x,v):={b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)}{i2|\xi|\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}d\xi,

where
\mathcal{F}_{x}[K^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}](\xi,v)\in L^{\infty}_{\langle v\rangle^{-10}}\mathcal{S}^{-2}
. Therefore, from (
3.198
), (
3.199
) and (
3.207
), we have

P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}})=\Gamma\big(P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;0}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}Lh_{\alpha\beta}+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\partial_{t}Lh_{\alpha\beta}+vl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)+hm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t),

(3.208)

where

vl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\preceq\Gamma\circ L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{\Gamma L\mathcal{L}_{1};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{\mu k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}}(t,\xi,v)}{2|\xi|\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}

-\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq L}\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{K}^{L\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}(y,v)\tilde{\Gamma}\mathcal{L}_{2}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)^{\mu},

(3.209)

hm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)=-\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\mathcal{L}\preceq L}\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\}}C_{\Gamma}^{L_{i}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}y_{i}\tilde{K}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)^{\mu}.

(3.210)

Similarly, from (
3.201
), (
3.199
), and (
3.206
) and the nonlinear wave equation satisfied by
Lh_{\alpha\beta}
in (
2.36
), we have

\begin{split}P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{\Gamma L}})&=\Gamma P_{k}\big(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta}^{\tilde{h}}c_{\Gamma,\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}Lh_{\alpha\beta}+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;0}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\partial_{t}Lh_{\alpha\beta}\\
&+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\Delta Lh_{\alpha\beta}+ptvl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)+pthm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t),\end{split}

(3.211)

where

ptvl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\},|\mathcal{L}_{1}\preceq\Gamma\circ L,\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq L}\big(\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}_{1};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}dv]\big)^{\mu}

-\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{K}^{L\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{h}}_{\Gamma\tilde{\Gamma}}(y,v)\tilde{\Gamma}\mathcal{L}_{2}f(t,x-y,v)dydv\big)^{\mu}+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\big(\sum_{\mathcal{L}\preceq L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\mathcal{L}f(t,x,v)dv\big),

(3.212)

pthm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\{+,-\},\mathcal{L}\preceq L\\
\tilde{\Gamma}\in\mathcal{P}\cup\{Id\}\\
\end{subarray}}C_{\Gamma}^{L_{i}}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}y_{i}{K}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\big((\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)\big)dydv\big)^{\mu}

+P_{k}R_{\alpha\beta;-1}^{\tilde{h};\Gamma}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\square Lh_{\alpha\beta}].

(3.213)

Hence, to sum up, from (
2.64
), (
3.208
) and (
3.211
) , we know that the following equalities hold,

\begin{split}Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)&=ptvl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)+i|\nabla|\big(vl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)\big),\\
Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)&=pthm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)+i|\nabla|\big(hm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)\big).\end{split}

(3.214)

Recall the detailed formulas of
vl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
in (
3.209
) and
ptvl_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(tg)
in (
3.212
).
We know that the representation formula for
Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
in (
3.195
) holds for some uniquely determined symbol
m_{\mathcal{L}}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t,\xi,v)
.
∎

With the decomposition in previous Lemma, now, we show that the error terms appeared in (
3.194
) is indeed small and the estimate of perturbed metric is improved at very low frequency, e.g., of size almost like
\langle t\rangle^{-1}.

Lemma 3.22
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
,
|L|\geq 1
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, s.t.,
k\leq-2H(N_{0})\delta_{0}m
, and zero order Fourier multiplier operator
M
with symbol in
\mathcal{S}^{0}
,

2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\big(\|MP_{k}(U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}})(t,x)\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}+\|MP_{k}(\widetilde{U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}})(t,x)\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\lesssim(1+2^{-k-m})2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}+2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+m+k}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.215)

Proof.

Recall (
2.64
). From the decomposition of density type function in (
3.18
), we have

2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\big(\|P_{k}\tilde{\rho}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{L}(f)(t)\|_{L^{2}}+\|P_{k}\tilde{\rho}^{\tilde{h}}_{L}(f)(t)\|_{L^{2}}\big)\lesssim\epsilon_{1}+2^{k+m}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.216)

Since
L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
,
|L|\geq 1
, we know that
L=\Gamma\circ\tilde{L}
holds for some
\Gamma\in P_{1},\tilde{L}\in P_{|L|-1}
.
From the equality (
3.194
) in Lemma
3.21
, we have

\begin{split}&2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\|MP_{k}(\widetilde{U^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}})(t,x)\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\big[\|\Gamma MP_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}+\|[\Gamma,M]P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&+2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\big(\sum_{L_{1}\prec L}\|P_{k}({U^{{L}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\|_{L^{2}}+\|Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}+\|Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\big)\big].\end{split}

(3.217)

From the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
and the fact that
|\tilde{L}|\leq N_{0}-1
, with minor modification in the obtained estimate (
3.19
), we have

2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\big(\|[\Gamma,M]P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{L_{1}\prec L}\|P_{k}({U^{{L}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\|_{L^{2}}\big)\lesssim(1+2^{-m-k})2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|\tilde{L}|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}

+2^{-m+{d}(|L|+1,0)\delta_{0}m-5\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}\lesssim(1+2^{-m-k})2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|\tilde{L}|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.218)

Recall the detailed formulation of
Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
in (
3.214
). We first estimate the imaginary part
hm_{\Gamma;k}^{L;\tilde{h}}(t)
. Recall (
3.210
), the equations (
2.9
) and (
2.13
) and the decomposition in (
3.111
). After doing integration by parts in
v
once, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}y_{i}\tilde{K}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\mathcal{L}f(t,x-y,v)dydv

=\sum_{i=3,4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}y_{i}\tilde{K}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\big(\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)+\mathfrak{Q}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathfrak{N}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)

+\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)\big)-\nabla_{v}\big(y_{i}\tilde{K}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}(y,v)\big)\cdot\big(\mathfrak{N}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)+\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x-y-t\hat{v},v)\big)dydv.

(3.219)

Recall (
3.202
). With the above equality, from the volume of support of
\xi
if
k\leq 0
,
the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
, the estimate (
3.63
) in Lemma
3.10
, the estimate (
3.113
), and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}\||\nabla|(hm_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t))\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}+k+3k_{-}/2}2^{-3k+d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\big(2^{-m+k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

+2^{-7m/6+2d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\big)\lesssim 2^{-m+d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-k-7m/6+3d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.220)

Now we estimate the real part
pthm_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)
. Recall (
3.213
). With minor modification in the estimate of
hm_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)
, the following estimate holds from the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
,

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}\|pthm_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k}2^{-m+{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-m/6+3d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.221)

Combining the estimates (
3.220
) and (
3.221
), we have

2^{-k_{-}/2+\gamma k_{-}}\|Highmetric_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-m/6+3d(|L|+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-k-m+{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.222)

Recall the general formula of
Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)
in (
3.195
). By using the same strategy used in obtaining the equality (
3.18
) and the estimate (
3.216
), we have

2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\|Vlasov_{\Gamma;k}^{\tilde{L};\tilde{h}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}+2^{k+m}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.223)

From the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, we have

2^{-k/2+\gamma k}\|\Gamma P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}h_{\alpha\beta}}})\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}+2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+m+k}\epsilon_{1}.

(3.224)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
3.215
) holds from the estimates (
3.216
–
3.218
) and (
3.222
–
3.224
).
∎

4.
Energy estimate of the perturbed metric part

Let
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}
,
L\in P_{n},n\leq N_{0}
. To get around the losing derivatives issue, we utilize the symmetric structure of the Einstein equation and define a modified energy for the modified perturbed metric as follows,

E_{modi}^{\tilde{h};L}(t):=\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}E_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t),

where

E_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t):=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|P_{k}({\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}})(t)\big|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx.

(4.1)

The first part of
E_{modi}^{L\tilde{h};1}(t)
denotes the energy of the modified profile
\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t)
, and the second part are cubic correction terms which aim to get around the losing derivatives issue caused from the quasilinear nature of the Einstein equation.

From (
2.58
) and the definition of the modified perturbed metric in (
2.64
), we have

\partial_{t}E_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t)=\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{t}P_{k}(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})dx\big]

+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\partial_{t}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{t}H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx

=\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big(H_{i0}\partial_{i}P_{k}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}+H_{ij}\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}P_{k}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)dx\big]+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx

+\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big(e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}P_{k}\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}-\big(H_{i0}\partial_{i}P_{k}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}+H_{ij}\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}P_{k}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)\big)dx\big]

+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{t}H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\partial_{t}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx.

(4.2)

Recall the definition of the modified half-wave in (
2.60
). The real part of
\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
is
\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}
. Note that, thanks to the symmetric structure, after doing integration by parts in
x
, we have

\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big(H_{i0}\partial_{i}P_{k}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}+H_{ij}\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}P_{k}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)dx\big]+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx\\
=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}H_{i0}P_{k}\big(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)-{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}H_{ij}P_{k}\big(\partial_{j}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx.

(4.3)

To sum up, from equalities (
4.2
) and (
4.3
), we have

\begin{split}\partial_{t}E_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t)&=\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big(e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}P_{k}\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}-\big(H_{i0}\partial_{i}P_{k}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}+H_{ij}\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}P_{k}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)\big)dx\big]\\
&-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}H_{i0}P_{k}\big(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}H_{ij}P_{k}\big(\partial_{j}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{t}H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\partial_{t}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx.\end{split}

(4.4)

Recall (
2.34
) and (
2.58
). We know that
\Lambda_{1}[H_{0i}]=-2h_{0i}
and
\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]=h_{00}\delta_{ij}+h_{ij}
. After using the double Hodge decompositions in (
2.37
), we have

\begin{split}&-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}\Lambda_{1}[H_{i0}]P_{k}\big(\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{j}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big[P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{1}[H_{i0}]\partial_{i}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}\\
&+\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)-\big(\Lambda_{1}[H_{i0}]\partial_{i}P_{k}\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}+\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]\partial_{i}{\partial_{j}P_{k}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\big)\big]dx\\
&=\sum_{h^{\prime}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\}}\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}((U^{h^{\prime}})^{\mu}(t),(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}})^{\nu}(t))(x)dx\big]+\mathfrak{R}_{k},\end{split}

(4.5)

where, very importantly, as a result of direct computations, the bilinear operators
\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}(\cdot,\cdot)\in\mathcal{M}_{\mu\nu}^{null},\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\},
see (
2.86
), have null structure.

The quartic and higher order term
\mathfrak{R}_{k}
reads as follows,

\mathfrak{R}_{k}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\big(P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}}))\big)^{2}\partial_{i}R_{i}\big(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\big[\partial_{t}\big(\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{j}+\varepsilon_{jlm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}(\Omega_{m}-R_{0}\omega_{m})\big)

+\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{j}+\varepsilon_{jlm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}|\nabla|^{-1}\square\omega_{m}\big]+P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})\partial_{j}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\partial_{i}\big(\big(\varepsilon_{ilm}R_{j}+\varepsilon_{jlm}R_{i}\big)R_{l}(\Omega_{m}-R_{0}\omega_{m})\big)dx.

(4.6)

Now, we study the first line in (
4.5
). Recall (
2.58
). From (
2.13
), and (
3.111
), we classify the nonlinearity
\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}
of
e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}P_{k}\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
based on the order of nonlinearity as follows,

\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}(t,x)dx\big]=-\sum_{\mathcal{L}\in\mathcal{P}_{a}^{b},|\mathcal{L}|\leq|L|}\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,\xi)}

\times(\psi_{k}(\xi))^{2}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}{b}^{L\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k^{\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\partial_{t}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)}{i2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}d\xi dv\big]=\sum_{i=1,\cdots,4}\textup{Re}\big[E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;i}(t)\big],

where

\begin{split}E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;1}(t)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{U^{L\tilde{h}}}}(t,\xi)}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\xi\cdot\big(\widehat{\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{N}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}]}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{D}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)\big)}{2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\big(\psi_{k}(\xi)\big)^{2}dvd\xi,\\
E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;2}(t)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{U^{L\tilde{h}}}}(t,\xi)}\big(\widehat{\mathfrak{N}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{D}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big(\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi){b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)}{2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\Big)\big(\psi_{k}(\xi)\big)^{2}dvd\xi,\\
E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;3}(t)&:=\sum_{i=3,4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{U^{L\tilde{h}}}}(t,\xi)}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\big(2\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}}]}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{D}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi,v)\big)}{i4\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\big(\psi_{k}(\xi)\big)^{2}dvd\xi,\\
E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;4}(t)&:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{U^{L\tilde{h}}}}(t,\xi)}{b}^{L\mathcal{L};\tilde{h}}_{\alpha\beta}(v)\frac{k_{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\xi\cdot\big(\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}}]}(t,\xi,v)\big)}{2\big(|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\big(\psi_{k}(\xi)\big)^{2}dvd\xi.\end{split}

(4.7)

From (
4.4
), (
4.5
), and (
4.7
), based on the types and the orders of inputs, we classify
\partial_{t}\tilde{E}_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t)
as follows,

2\partial_{t}\tilde{E}_{modi;k}^{\tilde{h};L}(t)=C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)+R_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\textup{Re}\big[C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)\big]dx+R_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t),

where
C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)
denotes wave-wave-Vlasov type trilinear interaction,
R_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)
denotes the quartic and higher order interaction that involves the Vlasov part,
C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
denotes wave-wave-wave type trilinear interaction, and
R_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)
denotes the quartic and higher order purely wave-type interaction. More precisely, the detailed formulas are given as follows,

C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)=E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;1}(t)+\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{2}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}]\big)dx\big]

+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big[\Lambda_{1}[H_{ij}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}({\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)+\Lambda_{1}[H_{i0}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{i}({\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)\big]dx,

(4.8)

R_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)=\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 3}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}]\big)dx\big]+\sum_{i=2,3,4}E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;i}(t),

(4.9)

C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)=\sum_{h^{\prime}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}((U^{h^{\prime}})^{\mu}(t),(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}})^{\nu}(t))(x)

+\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}(Q_{mt}^{\tilde{h};L})(t,x),

(4.10)

R_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)=\mathfrak{R}_{k}+\textup{Re}\big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}\big(R_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}\big)dx\big]-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big({P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big)^{2}\partial_{i}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i0}]\\
-{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\partial_{i}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{ij}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{j}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big(P_{k}(\Lambda_{1}[H_{i\gamma}]\partial_{i}{\partial_{\gamma}\tilde{h}^{L}})-\Lambda_{1}[H_{i\gamma}]P_{k}(\partial_{i}{\partial_{\gamma}\tilde{h}^{L}})dx\\
+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\partial_{t}H_{ij}]\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{P_{k}(\widetilde{\partial_{t}{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}\big[\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{i0}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{i}({\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)\\
+\Lambda_{\geq 2}[H_{ij}]P_{k}\big(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}({\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})\big)\big]dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\partial_{t}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx.

(4.11)

In this section, we aim to prove the following proposition.

Proposition 4.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
) and (
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
L\in P_{n},n\leq N_{0}
,
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\},
and any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],

\big|E_{modi}^{\tilde{h};L}(t_{2})-E_{modi}^{\tilde{h};L}(t_{1})\big|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.12)

By assuming the estimates of the wave-wave-wave type interaction and the wave-wave-Vlasov type interaction, which are postponed in next two subsections, we first give a proof of the above Proposition.

Proof of Proposition

4.1
.

Recall (
4.11
). From the estimates in Lemma
4.1
, the estimate (
4.44
) in Proposition
4.2
, and the estimate (
4.55
) in Proposition
4.3
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)dxdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)dxdt\big|\big]

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.13)

It remains to estimate
R_{vlasov}^{L\tilde{h};k}(t)
and
R_{metric}^{L\tilde{h};k}(t)
.

\bullet
The estimate of
R_{vlasov}^{L\tilde{h};k}(t)
.   Recall (
4.11
), (
2.46
), and (
2.35
). From the
L^{2}-L^{2}
estimate and the estimate (
3.79
) in Lemma
3.12
, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 3}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}]\big)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{H(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}\|\Lambda_{\geq 3}[L\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}]\|_{E_{n}}\lesssim 2^{-4m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(4.14)

Recall (
4.7
). From the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate, the volume of support of
\xi
, and the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
, and the estimate (
3.113
), the estimate (
3.63
) in Lemma
3.10
, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{i=2,3,4}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|E_{\tilde{h};L}^{k;i}(t)|\lesssim\sum_{i=1,3,4}\sum_{j=2,3}2^{H(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}\big(\|(1+|x|)^{2}(1+|v|)^{10}\mathfrak{N}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

+\|(1+|x|)^{2}(1+|v|)^{10}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{N}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|(1+|x|)^{2}(1+|v|)^{10}\mathfrak{D}_{j}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)

\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+3d(N_{0}+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(4.15)

Combining the estimates (
4.14
) and (
4.15
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|R_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t)|\lesssim 2^{-7m/6+3d(N_{0}+3,0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(4.16)

\bullet
The estimate of
R_{metric}^{L\tilde{h};k}(t)
.  We first estimate
\mathfrak{R}_{k}^{3}
. Recall (
4.6
). From the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, the following estiamte holds for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|\mathfrak{R}_{k}^{3}|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{2H(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\big(\|\partial_{i}P_{k_{1}}(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial_{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}(\Omega_{m}-R_{0}\omega_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-1}P_{k_{1}}(\square\omega_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big).

(4.17)

From the relations between double Hodge decomposition variable in (
2.41
) and the equalities (
2.42
) and (
2.43
), we have

\|\partial_{i}P_{k_{1}}(\rho-R_{0}\underline{F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial_{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}(\Omega_{m}-R_{0}\omega_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-1}P_{k_{1}}(\square\omega_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\lesssim\|\partial_{t}P_{k_{1}}\tau(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|E_{\mu}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-1}\partial_{t}E_{\mu}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\lesssim\min\{2^{-k_{1}}\big(\|\partial_{t}E_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\square h_{0i}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)+\|E_{0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{k_{1}/2}\big(\|\partial_{t}E_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\square h_{0i}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big)+2^{3k_{1}/2}\|E_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}.

(4.18)

From the estimates (
3.186
) and (
3.185
) in Lemma
3.19
, the linear decay estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, which is used in the close to the light cone case, the equality (
3.72
) in the far away from the light cone case, and the estimate (
3.1
) in Lemma
3.1
, we have

(\textup{\ref {aug25eqn10}})\lesssim\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}\min\{2^{-k_{1}-2m}+2^{-2m},2^{k_{1}/2-m}+2^{3k_{1}/2-m}\}2^{2H(1)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-4m/3+2H(1)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(4.19)

From the above estimate (
4.19
) and the estimate (
4.17
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|\mathfrak{R}_{k}^{3}|\lesssim 2^{-4m/3+2H(1)\delta_{0}m+2H(|L|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.20)

Recall (
2.66
) and (
2.58
). Note that, the imaginary part of
e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
contains only
\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}
, which is semilinear. From the
L^{2}
-estimate of
\mathcal{N}_{vl;m}^{{\tilde{h};L}}
(
3.110
) in Lemma
3.15
and the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{i}}P_{k}(\partial_{t}\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}}-\widetilde{\partial_{t}\tilde{h}^{L}})H_{ij}\partial_{x_{j}}P_{k}(\widetilde{\tilde{h}^{L}})dx\big|\lesssim 2^{-2m+2d(N_{0}+3,0)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.21)

Recall (
4.11
). From the above estimates (
4.20
) and (
4.21
), the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate, the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.76
) in Lemma
3.11
, and the estimate (
3.33
) in Lemma
3.6
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|R_{metric}^{L\tilde{h};k}(t)\big|\lesssim 2^{-m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(4.22)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
4.12
) holds from (
4.13
), (
4.16
) and (
4.22
).

∎

Now, the goal is reduced to estimate the contribution from the wave-wave-Vlasov type interaction, i.e., the estimate of
C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
and the wave-wave-wave type interaction, i.e., the estimate
C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
. We first do further reduction for the estimate of
C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
and then analyze these two types of interaction in the next two subsections in details.

We first rule out the very low output frequency case and the case when
x
is far away from the light cone. The main tool we will use is the Lemma
3.22
, which allows us to exploit the difference of hierarchy we set for the growth rate of energy of the different order of vector fields.

Let

\alpha_{out}(|L|):=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}(H(|L|)-H(|L|-1)-2\beta(|L|))/2-1/50&\textup{if\,}|L|\geq 1,\\
-10&\textup{if\,}|L|=0,\end{array}\right.

(4.23)

where
\beta(|L|)
is defined in (
2.89
).

In the following Lemma, we first rule out the very low output frequency case.

Lemma 4.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}
,
L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)dx\big|\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(4.24)

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)\big(\psi_{<m-4}(x)+\psi_{>m+1}(x)\big)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.25)

Proof.

Recall (
4.10
) and (
2.78
). We split into two cases as follows.

\bullet

Case
1
:  Proof of the desired estimate (
4.24
).

Similar to the obtained estimate (
3.155
), after rerunning the argument and using the volume of support of the output frequency instead of using the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate, the following estimate holds if
k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
,

\sum_{H\in\{S,P,Q\}}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k/2+\gamma k+(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{(1+\gamma)k_{-}+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(4.26)

From the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type estimate, the above obtained estimates (
4.26
)
and the estimate (
3.215
) in Lemma
3.22
for the case
|L|\geq 1
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}(Q_{mt}^{\tilde{h};L})(t,x)\psi_{\leq m+2}(|x|)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.27)

Hence finishing the proof of the desired estimate (
4.24
).

From the proof of the estimates (
3.163
), (
3.166
), and (
3.172
), the following improved estimate holds for the exterior of the light cone region if
k\leq-m/2
,

\sum_{H\in\{S,P,Q\}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m/2}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k/2+\gamma k+(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\|QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t)\psi_{>m+2}(|x|)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.28)

From the obtained estimates (
4.27
) and (
4.28
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}P_{k}(Q_{mt}^{\tilde{h};L})(t,x)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.29)

Recall again (
4.10
). It remains to estimate the
\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}(U^{h^{\prime}},(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\nu})
. Note that, from the support of frequency variables, the frequency of
U^{h^{\prime}}
is bounded from above by
2^{k+10}
, otherwise the integral vanishes. From the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type estimate and the
L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k+2\gamma k}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\mu}(t,x)\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}(U^{h^{\prime}},(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\nu})(t,x)dx\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-m+2H(|L|)\delta_{0}m+(1+\gamma)k+\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.30)

To sum up, our desired estimate (
4.24
) holds from the estimates (
4.27
–
4.30
).

\bullet

Case
2
:  Proof of the desired estimate (
4.25
).

For the semilinear quadratic terms
QK_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x),K\in\{Q,P\}
and
\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}(U^{h^{\prime}},(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\nu})(t,x)
, we use the bilinear estimate (
3.134
) in Lemma
3.17
and for the quasilinear quadratic term
QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)
, we use the the bilinear estimate (
3.134
) in Lemma
3.17
for the input
L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}
if
|L_{2}|\leq|L|/2+4
and use the bilinear estimate (
3.135
) in Lemma
3.17
if
|L_{2}|>|L|/2+4
.

Here, we exploit the fact that
|L_{2}|<N_{0}-1
for the quasilinear quadratic term
QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(t,x)
. As a result, the symbols of quadratic terms allow us to gain the smallness
2^{-m}
from the distance with respect to the light cone, which is more than sufficient to ensure the validity of our desired estimate (
4.25
).
∎

Now, we consider the case
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
. Recall (
4.11
). To distinguish the subtle growth rates of the perturbed profile and the modified perturbed profile for the metric component, from (
2.78
–
2.79
), based on the size of
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}
, we decompose
C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
into several parts as follows,

C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)=\sum_{H\in\{S,P,Q\}}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},}CH_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)+\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{j\in\{1,2\}}\tilde{C}_{metric;j}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x),

(4.31)

where
CH_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
