denotes term determined by
Q_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}(t,x)
and
\tilde{C}_{metric;j}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)
are given as follows,

\begin{split}\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)&=\sum_{{h}^{\prime}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{{h}^{\prime}\tilde{h}}(\widetilde{(U^{{h}^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}})^{\mu}},\widetilde{(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\nu}})(t,x),\\
\tilde{C}_{metric;2}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)&=\sum_{h^{\prime}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)\tilde{Q}^{\mu\nu}_{h^{\prime}\tilde{h}}({(\tilde{\rho}^{h^{\prime}}(P_{k^{\prime}}f))^{\mu}},\widetilde{(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k})^{\nu}})(t,x).\end{split}

(4.32)

CH_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
is more subtle. We use different formulations for the wave-wave-wave type interaction based on the size of the input frequency because, from (
3.15
), there is no need to decompose the profile into the modified profile and the density type function if the input frequency is very small.

More precisely, let

\alpha_{inp}(|L|):=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}(H(|L|)-H(|L|-1)-2\beta(|L|))/2-1/50&\textup{if\,}|L|\geq 1,\\
10&\textup{if\,}|L|=0,\end{array}\right.

(4.33)

We remark that,
\alpha_{inp}(|L|)
only differs from
\alpha_{out}(|L|)
when
|L|=0
, see (
4.23
).

If
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
, then

\forall H\in\{S,P,Q\},\qquad CH_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\star\in\{ess,err,vl\}}CH_{metric;\star}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),\\
CS_{metric;\star}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L,\tilde{L}_{2}\prec L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x),\\
CK_{metric;\star}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QK_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x),\quad K\in\{P,Q\},

(4.34)

where
QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}},H\in\{S,P,Q\},\star\in\{ess,err,vl\}
are defined in (
2.79
).

If
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
, then

CH_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=CH_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)+CH_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),\quad H\in\{S,P,Q\},

where, for
K\in\{P,Q\}
, we have

\begin{split}CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)&=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QK_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x),\\
CK_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)&=0,\end{split}

(4.35)

and, for the quaslinear terms, i.e.,
H=S
, as two inputs have different number of derivatives, we use different formulas based on sizes of
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
. More precisely, if
k_{2}=\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
, then

CS_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L,\tilde{L}_{2}\prec L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x),

(4.36)

CS_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=0.

(4.37)

If
k_{1}=\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
, then

CS_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L,\tilde{L}_{2}\prec L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big[c^{\mu\nu}_{i0}P_{k}\big(P_{k_{1}}(L_{1}h_{\mu\nu})P_{k_{2}}(\partial_{i}\widetilde{\partial_{t}L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}})\big)

+c^{\mu\nu}_{ij}P_{k}\big(P_{k_{1}}(L_{1}h_{\mu\nu})P_{k_{2}}(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\widetilde{L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}})\big)\big](t,x),

(4.38)

CS_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L\\
\tilde{L}_{2}\prec L\end{subarray}}c_{\tilde{L}_{1}\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big[c^{\mu\nu}_{i0}P_{k}\big(P_{k_{1}}(L_{1}h_{\mu\nu})P_{k_{2}}(\partial_{i}\big({\partial_{t}L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}})-\widetilde{\partial_{t}L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}})\big)\big)

+c^{\mu\nu}_{ij}P_{k}\big(P_{k_{1}}(L_{1}h_{\mu\nu})P_{k_{2}}(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\big({L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}}-\widetilde{L_{2}h_{\alpha\beta}})\big)\big](t,x).

(4.39)

With the above preparations, in the following Lemma, we first rule out some easy cases by using the obtained estimates in section
3
.

Lemma 4.2
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
, the following estimates hold,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k,k^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
j=1,2,k^{\prime}\leq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;j}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(4.40)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k,k^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;2}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.41)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
K\in\{S,P,Q\},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CK_{metric;err}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.42)

Proof.

Recall the detailed formula of
\tilde{C}_{metric;j}^{L\tilde{h};k,k^{\prime}}(t)
in (
4.31
) and the equality (
4.5
). From the bilinear estimate (
3.136
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds if
k^{\prime}\leq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
,

2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;j}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|\lesssim 2^{-m+k^{\prime}+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.43)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
4.40
).

Recall (
4.32
). Since
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
, the desired estimate (
4.41
) holds directly from the estimate (
3.97
) in Lemma
3.14
, the estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, and the decay estimate of the density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
.

Recall (
2.80
), (
2.83
), and (
2.70
). The desired estimate (
4.42
) follows directly from the estimate (
3.15
) in Lemma
3.3
and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
and the bilinear estimate (
3.136
) in Lemma
3.17
.
∎

To sum up, it remains to estimate two types of interactions:

For the wave-wave-Vlasov type interaction, it remains to estimate (i)
C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
in (
4.8
); (ii)
\tilde{C}_{metric;2}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)
in (
4.32
) for the case
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
; (iii)
CH_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),H\in\{S,P,Q\}
in (
4.34
) for the case
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m

and
CS_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
in (
4.39
) for the case
k_{1}=\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
. We analyze these three cases in details in subsection
4.1
.

For the wave-wave-wave type interaction, it remains to estimate (i)
\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)
in (
4.32
) for the case
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
; (ii)
CH_{metric;ess}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),H\in\{S,P,Q\}
in (
4.34
) for the case
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
; (iii)
CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),K\in\{P,Q\},
in (
4.35
) and
CS_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
in (
4.36
) and (
4.38
) for the case
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
. We analyze these three cases in details in subsection
4.2
.

With the above divided plan, for the rest of this section, we aim to prove the following Proposition.

Proposition 4.2
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k>-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{metric}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.44)

Proof.

Recall the decomposition in (
4.31
).
Our desired estimate holds from the estimates (
4.40
–
4.42
) in Lemma
4.2
, the estimates (
4.53
–
4.55
) in Lemma
4.3
, the estimate (
4.75
) in Lemma
4.4
, and the estimate (
4.80
) in Lemma
4.7
.

∎

4.1.
The estimate of wave-wave-Vlasov type interaction

Recall the detailed formula of
C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
in (
4.8
) and the the detailed formulas of corresponding terms in (
4.7
), (
2.14
), (
3.112
), and (
2.55
). We can write
C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)
as a linear combinations of the following trilinear form,

\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)dxdt=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},\mu\in\{+,-\}\\
L,L_{1}\in P_{n},\mathcal{L}_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{n},\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}\\
V^{L}\in\{\widetilde{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}},{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\}\\
V_{1}^{L_{1}}\in\{\widetilde{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}},{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}}\}\\
\end{subarray}}K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1}),

(4.45)

where

K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi|+it\mu|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\overline{\widehat{V^{L}}(t,\xi)}\widehat{(V^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)

\times\tilde{m}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta dv,

(4.46)

where the symbol
\tilde{m}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)
satisfies the following estimate,

\|(1+|v|)^{-100}\tilde{m}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}.

(4.47)

Similarly, we have

\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CK_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt

=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\{+,-\},L,L_{1}\in P_{n},\mathcal{L}_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{n},\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}\\
V^{L}\in\{\widetilde{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}},{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\}\\
V_{1}^{L_{1}}\in\{\widetilde{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}},{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}}\}\\
\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varphi(\sigma)T^{K,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)d\sigma,

(4.48)

\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CS_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt

=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu\in\{+,-\},L,\tilde{L}_{1},L_{1}\in P_{n},\mathcal{L}_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{n},\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},{L}_{1}\prec\mathcal{L}\\
V^{L}\in\{\widetilde{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}},{V^{Lh_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}\},V_{1}^{L_{1}}\in\{\widetilde{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}},{V^{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}},{V^{\tilde{h}^{L_{1}}}}\}\\
\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varphi(\sigma)\big[T^{S_{1},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)

+T^{S_{2},\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big]d\sigma

(4.49)

where

\varphi(\sigma):=2^{3m}\widehat{\psi}_{[-4,1]}(2^{m}\sigma),

(4.50)

T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi-\sigma|+it\mu|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\overline{\widehat{V^{L}}(t,\xi-\sigma)}\widehat{(V^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)

\times m_{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta dv,\quad\star\in\{P,Q,S_{1},S_{2}\},

(4.51)

and the symbols
m_{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v),\star\in\{P,Q,S_{1},S_{2}\},
satisfies the following estimate,

\begin{split}\sum_{\star\in\{P,Q\}}\|(1+|v|)^{-100}m_{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}&\lesssim 2^{-k_{2}},\\
\|(1+|v|)^{-100}m_{S_{1}}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}&\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}},\\
\|(1+|v|)^{-100}m_{S_{2}}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}&\lesssim 2^{k_{1}-2k_{2}}.\end{split}

(4.52)

Recall (
4.46
) and the estimates (
4.47
) and (
4.52
), we know that
K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})
is a special case of trilinear form
T^{S_{1},\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)
.
Recall (
4.31
). We know that the trilinear form of
\tilde{C}_{metric;2}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)
is of same type as
CK_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),K\in\{P,Q\}
with
k^{\prime}=k_{1}
and
k_{2}=k
.

With the above preparations, by assuming the validity of a technical Lemma, Lemma
4.3
, we state and prove the main result of this subsection as follows.

Proposition 4.3
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimates hold for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\},

L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
k_{1}=\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m-10\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CS_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|

\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(4.53)

\sum_{k\in[-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,\infty)\cap\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}H\in\{S,P,Q\},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m-10\end{subarray}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CH_{metric;vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|+\sum_{k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;2}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\Big]\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(4.54)

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C_{vlasov}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k}(t,x)dxdt\big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.55)

Proof.

As discussed at the beginning of this section, the proof of our desired estimates is reduced to the estimate of trilinear forms
T^{K,\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma),K\in\{S_{1},S_{2},P,Q\}
for different choices of
k,k_{1},k_{2}
in different settings.

Recall (
4.46
) and (
4.51
). From the estimates of symbols in (
4.52
), the bilinear estimate (
3.90
) in Lemma
3.13
for the case
|L_{1}|,|\tilde{L}_{1}|\geq|L|-5
, and the bilinear estimates (
3.96
) and (
3.97
) in Lemma
3.14
for the case
|L_{1}|,|\tilde{L}_{1}|\leq|L|-5
, the following estimate holds for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
,
L,\tilde{L}_{1},L_{1}\in P_{n},\mathcal{L}_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}
, s.t.,
\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},{L}_{1}\prec\mathcal{L}
,

\sum_{\star\in\{S_{1},P,Q\}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big(\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|+\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

+\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|\big)\lesssim 2^{2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m+\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.56)

The above estimate allows us to rule out the case
\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
.

Moreover, we rule out the case
|\sigma|
is not so small. Recall (
4.49
). From the rapidly decay rate of
\varphi(\sigma)
if
|\sigma|\geq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}
and the rough estimate, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{\star\in\{S_{1},P,Q\}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\int_{|\sigma|\geq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}}|\varphi(\sigma)|\big(|T^{\star,\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)|

+|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)|\big)d\sigma\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

It remains to consider the case
\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
and fixed
\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, s.t.,
|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}.
Based on the possible size of
\mathcal{L}_{2}
, we separate into two cases as follows.

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|-5
.

For this case, the total number of vector fields act on the Vlasov part is far away from the top order and we mainly use the decay estimate for the density type function. More precisely, from the estimate of symbol in (
4.52
) and the estimate (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, s.t.,
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
,

\sum_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|-5}\sum_{\star\in\{P,Q\}}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k_{-}/2+m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\min\{2^{2k_{2}}+2^{3k_{2}+d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m},2^{-4m-2k_{2}}

+2^{-4m-k_{2}+d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\}\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.57)

Similarly, from the estimate of symbol in (
4.47
), the following estimate holds for any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|-5\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|+\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{S_{1},\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|\big]

\lesssim\sup_{k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k_{1}\leq k_{2}-10}2^{m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\min\{2^{k_{1}},2^{-k_{1}/2}\min\{2^{3k_{2}}+2^{4k_{2}+d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m},2^{-4m-k_{2}}

+2^{-5m-k_{2}+d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.58)

It remains to consider
T^{S_{1},\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)
. Recall (
4.52
). Since the symbol
m_{S_{2}}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)
is worse when
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10
, this term is more delicate. By using the estimate (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, we first rule out the case
k_{2}\geq-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
as follows,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\prec\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|-5\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{2}\geq-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{S_{2};\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{2}\geq-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}2^{m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\big(2^{-3m-2k_{2}}+2^{-3m-k_{2}+d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}\big)

+2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.59)

It remains to estimate
T^{S_{2};\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)
for the case
k_{1}\geq-2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
and
k_{2}\in[-m+\alpha_{input}(|L|)\delta_{1}m-10,-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m]
. Recall (
4.49
). We have
|{L}_{1}|<|L|
for the estimate of
T^{S_{2};\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)
. From the bilinear estimate (
3.96
) in Lemma
3.14
and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\prec\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq N_{0}-5\\
k_{2}\leq-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m\\
k_{1}\geq-2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{2}\leq-m+2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}2^{m+2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m+k_{1}-2k_{2}}2^{-m-k_{1}+3k_{2}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.60)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|L|-5
and
|L|\geq N_{0}-5
.

For this case, there are at most five vector fields act on the metric component. From the bilinear estimate (
3.97
) in Lemma
3.14
, the
L^{\infty}_{x}
decay estimate in (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}\\
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|L|-5,\star\in\{P,Q\}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

\lesssim 2^{m+3d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}2^{-2m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.61)

Similarly, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|L|-5,k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{1}\geq-m+4d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m\end{subarray}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big(\big|T^{S_{1},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

+\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|+\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|\big)

\lesssim\sum_{k_{1}\geq-m+4d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}2^{m+3d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m}2^{-2m}2^{-k_{1}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.62)

From the estimate (
4.66
) in Lemma
4.3
, the following estimate holds if
k_{1}\leq-m+4d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
and
k_{2}\geq-2d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m
.

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|L|-5\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{1}\leq-m+4d(|L|+1,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m\end{subarray}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3},|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big(\big|T^{S_{1},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

+\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|+\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|\big)\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.63)

\bullet
If
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|L|-5
and
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
.

The main difference between this case and the previous case is that we pay attention to the possible issue of losing derivative.

If
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
, i.e.,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}+10
, then the argument used in the case
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|L|-5
is still applicable since
|L|\leq N_{0}-5
. As a result, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\star\in\{S_{1},P,Q\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big(\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|

+\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|+\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|\big)\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.64)

If
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}
, i.e.,
k_{1}\leq k_{2}-10,|k-k_{2}|\leq 10
, then the argument used in the case
|\mathcal{L}_{1}|>|L|-5,|L|\geq N_{0}-5
is still applicable. As a result, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\star\in\{S_{1},P,Q\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\\
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2N(|L|)k_{+}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3},|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}}\big(\big|K_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu;L,L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1})\big|

+\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;\tilde{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|+\big|T^{S_{2},\mu;L;{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|\big)\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.65)

Hence finishing the proof of desired estimates (
4.53
), (
4.54
) and (
4.55
).

∎

For the rest of this subsection, we prove the assumed Lemma
4.3
in the proof of the above Proposition.

Lemma 4.3
.

Given any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L,L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}{P}_{l},
s.t.,
\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}
. Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for the trilinear form defined in
(
4.51
)
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\star\in\{S_{1},S_{2}\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\\
k_{1}\leq-m/2,k\geq-4d(N_{0}+1,0)\delta_{0}m\end{subarray}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3},|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.66)

Proof.

Recall (
4.51
). Based on the size of
v
, we decompose
T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})
into two parts as follows,

T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2};V,V_{1},\sigma)=T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};\geq}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)+T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};<}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma),\\
T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};\diamondsuit}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{V^{L}}(t,\xi-\sigma)}e^{it|\xi-\sigma|+it\mu|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\widehat{(V^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\\
\times\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)m_{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)\psi_{\diamondsuit m/10}(v)d\eta dv,\quad\diamondsuit\in\{\geq,<\}.

(4.67)

For the case
|v|
is relatively large, we gain sufficient decay rates from the high moment of
v
. More precisely, from the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate and the estimate of symbol in (
4.52
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};\geq}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)\big|\lesssim 2^{-m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.68)

Now, we focus on the estimate of
T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};<}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)
. Note that the following estimate holds if
|v|\lesssim 2^{m/10}
and
|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}
,

|\xi-\sigma|+\mu|\xi-\eta|-\eta\cdot\hat{v}\gtrsim\frac{|\eta|}{1+|v|^{2}}-2|\xi-\eta|-|\sigma|\gtrsim\frac{|\eta|}{2(1+|v|^{2})}\gtrsim 2^{k_{2}}(1+|v|^{2})^{-1}.

(4.69)

With the above observation, to take the advantage of high oscillation in time, we do integration by parts in time once for
T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};<}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)
. As a result, we have

T^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};<}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2},\sigma)=End^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})+J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})+J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2}),

(4.70)

End^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\sum_{i=1,2}(-1)^{i-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{U^{L}}(t_{i},\xi-\sigma)}\widehat{(U^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t_{i},\xi-\eta)\widehat{{\mathcal{L}_{2}f}}(t_{i},\eta,v)\\
\times m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star,\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)d\eta dv,\\
J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi-\sigma|+it\mu|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\partial_{t}\big(\overline{\widehat{V^{L}}(t,\xi-\sigma)}\widehat{(V^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\big)\\
\times\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star,\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)d\eta dvdt,\\
J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi-\sigma|+it\mu|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\overline{\widehat{V^{L}}(t,\xi-\sigma)}\widehat{(V^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\\
\times\partial_{t}\big(\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)\big)m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star,\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)d\eta dvdt,

(4.71)

where the symbol
m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star,\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)
is defined as follows,

m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\star,\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v):=m_{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)\psi_{<m/10}(v)(i|\xi-\sigma|+\mu|\xi-\eta|-\eta\cdot\hat{v})^{-1}.

From the estimate of symbol in (
4.52
), the estimate (
4.69
), the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, and the
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, we have

\sum_{\star\in\{S_{1},S_{2}\}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2},k_{1}\leq-m/2,k\geq-4d(N_{0}+1,0)\delta_{0}m}2^{-k+2\gamma k+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|End^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{1}\leq-m/2,k\geq-4d(N_{0}+1,0)\delta_{0}m}2^{2d(|L|,N_{0}-|L|)\delta_{0}m-k_{2}}2^{k_{1}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.72)

Recall the estimate of phase in (
4.69
). Note that we have
|k-k_{2}|\leq 10
for the case we are considering. After doing integration by parts in time, the symbol is one derivative smoother. Hence, there is no losing derivatives issue for the estimates of
J^{L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2}),i\in\{1,2\}
. By using the same strategy used in the estimate of endpoint case, the following estimate holds from the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
,

\sum_{\star\in\{S_{1},S_{2}\}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2},k_{1}\leq-m/2,k\geq-4d(N_{0}+1,0)\delta_{0}m}2^{-k+2\gamma k+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|

\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.73)

Lastly, we estimate
J^{L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2}).
Recall the equation satisfied by
{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
in (
2.13
) and the decomposition in (
3.111
). The following equality holds after doing integration by parts in
v
for the nonlinearity
D_{v}\cdot\mathfrak{R}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)
and
D_{v}\cdot\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)
,

J^{\star,\mu;L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\overline{\widehat{U^{L}}(t,\xi)}\widehat{(U^{L_{1}}_{1})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)

\times\big[\big(\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{3}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)+\sum_{i=3,4}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\widehat{\mathfrak{R}_{i}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}](t,\eta,v)+i\eta\cdot(\widehat{\mathfrak{R}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{1}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)\big)m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)

+\big(\widehat{\mathfrak{Q}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)+\widehat{\mathfrak{R}_{2}^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\eta,v)\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}m_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\big]d\eta dvdt.

From the estimate of symbol in (
4.52
), the estimate (
4.69
), the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, the
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
, and the estimate (
3.62
) in Lemma
3.10
, and the obtained estimate (
3.113
), we have

\sum_{\star\in\{S_{1},S_{2}\}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2},k_{1}\leq-m/2,k\geq-4d(N_{0}+1,0)\delta_{0}m}2^{-k+2\gamma k+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|J^{L;L_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2};2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.74)

Recall the decompositions (
4.67
) and (
4.70
). To sum up, our desired estimate (
4.66
) holds from the estimates (
4.68
) and (
4.72
–
4.74
).

∎

4.2.
The estimate of wave-wave-wave type interaction

In the following Lemma, we first rule out the very small input frequency case for the wave-wave-wave type interaction.

Lemma 4.4
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L\in\cup_{n\in[0,N_{0}]}P_{n},k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},H\in\{S,P,Q\}\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CH_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.75)

Proof.

Recall the detailed formula of
CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
in (
4.35
–
4.39
) and the formula of
QK_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{L;L_{1},L_{2}},

K\in\{S,P,Q\}
in (
2.74
–
2.76
).
From the estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma
3.17
and the estimate (
3.215
) in Lemma
3.22
, the following estimate holds for
K\in\{S,P,Q\}
if
|L|\geq 1
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|

\lesssim\sum_{l\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{(1-\gamma)l+e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m}2^{\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\min\{(1+2^{-l-m})2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}

+2^{{H}(|L|-1)\delta_{1}m+m+l},2^{H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}\}\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

It remains to consider the case
|L|=0
. Note that
CS_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
vanishes for the case we are considering and the symbol of
CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x),

K\in\{P,Q\}
contributes the smallness of
2^{\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}
. Hence, the following estimate holds from the volume of support and the
Z
-norm estimates of profiles,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CK_{metric;wa}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|

\lesssim\sum_{l\leq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{(2-2a)l+2H(0)\delta_{1}m+2\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.76)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
4.75
).
∎

Now, we restrict ourselves to the case
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
and
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
for the estimate of
CK_{metric;ess}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
in (
4.34
) and the case
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
and
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
for the estimate of
\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t)
in (
4.31
).

Recall (
4.34
). In the following Lemma, we first rule out the case
L_{1}\circ L_{2}\prec L
.

Lemma 4.5
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l},k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,H\in\{S,P,Q\},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
L_{1}\circ L_{2}\prec L,\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}

\times\big[\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QK_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|+\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)

\times R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QH_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{{L}_{1},{L}_{2};\gamma_{1}\kappa_{1},\gamma_{2}\kappa_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{{L}_{1}h_{\gamma_{1}\kappa_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{{L}_{2}h_{\gamma_{2}\kappa_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)dx\big|\big]\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.77)

Proof.

Note that for the case we are considering, we have
|L|\geq 1
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
|L_{1}|\leq|L_{2}|
. Note that, from the bilinear estimates (
3.136
) and (
3.137
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds,

\textup{L.H.S. of (\ref {2020june19eqn1})}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},L_{1}\circ L_{2}\prec L\\
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2N(|L|)k_{+}}\|P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\times\big[\|\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)P_{k}(R_{\alpha}(\widetilde{U_{k_{1}}^{L_{1}h_{\iota\tau}}})^{\mu}R_{\beta}(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{L_{2}h_{\gamma\kappa}}})^{\nu})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

+\|P_{k}(|\nabla|^{-1}R_{\alpha}(\widetilde{U_{k_{1}}^{L_{1}h_{\iota\tau}}})^{\mu}(t,x)R_{\beta}|\nabla|(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{L_{2}h_{\gamma\kappa}}})^{\nu})(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

+\|P_{k}(|\nabla|R_{\alpha}(\widetilde{U_{k_{1}}^{L_{1}h_{\iota\tau}}})^{\mu}R_{\beta}|\nabla|^{-1}(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{L_{2}h_{\gamma\kappa}}})^{\nu})\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\big]

\lesssim 2^{-m+e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m+H(|L_{2}|)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}(2^{H(|L_{1}|+1)\delta_{1}m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{1}|\geq 1}+2^{\delta_{1}m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{1}|=0})\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{-m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
4.77
).
∎

Now, it remains to consider the case
L_{1}\circ L_{2}=L
, in which the null structure play essential roles. We summarize the effect of null structure in the following Lemma.

Lemma 4.6
.

Given
\mu,\nu\in\{+,-\}
,
\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{F,\underline{F},\rho,\Omega_{i},\omega_{i},\vartheta_{ij}\},

t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]
,
L,L_{1},L_{2},\tilde{L}_{1}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n}
, s.t.,
|L_{1}|+|L_{2}|=|L|
,
|\tilde{L}_{1}|+|\tilde{L}_{2}|=|L|
,
|\tilde{L}_{1}|<|L|
. Let

A:=\{(\textup{quasi},\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}),(\textup{semi},{L}_{1},{L}_{2}):\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq\tilde{L},L_{1}\circ L_{2}\preceq L,|\tilde{L}_{1}|<|L|\}.

(4.78)

For any given bilinear operators
T_{\mu\nu}^{\textup{quas}}
and
T_{\mu\nu}^{\textup{semi}}
with symbols
m_{\mu\nu}^{\textup{quas}}(\xi-\eta,\eta)|\xi-\eta|^{-1}|\eta|\in\mathcal{M}^{null}_{\mu\nu}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{M}}^{null}_{-\nu}
and
m_{\mu\nu}^{\textup{semi}}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\in\mathcal{M}^{null}_{\mu\nu}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{M}}^{null}_{-\nu}
respectively, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},(\star,L_{1},L_{2})\in A\\
\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{\mu\nu}^{{\star}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)

\times\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\big]\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.79)

Proof.

Postponed to subsection
4.3
for better presentation.
∎

By using the above Lemma, in the following Lemma, we finish the remaining cases of estimating
CK_{metric;ess}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)
in (
4.34
) for the case
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
and
\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t)
in (
4.31
) for the case
k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m
.

Lemma 4.7
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\},

L\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},

\sum_{k\in[-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,\infty)}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}H\in\{S,P,Q\},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m-10\end{subarray}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}CH_{metric;ess}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k_{1},k_{2}}(t,x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|+\sum_{k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\Big]

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.80)

Proof.

Recall (
4.31
) and (
4.34
). From the estimate (
4.77
) in Lemma
4.5
, to prove our desired estimate (
4.80
), it would be sufficient to the case
|L_{1}|+|L_{2}|=|\tilde{L}_{1}|+|\tilde{L}_{2}|=|L|,|\tilde{L}_{2}|<|L|
. Based on the possible sizes of
|L|
and
k
, we separate into two cases as follow.

\bullet
If
k\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m
, i.e., the assumptions in Lemma
4.6
are valid.

Recall (
2.82
) and the statement about null structure in Lemma
2.4
. Due to the presence of null structure in all the quadratic terms of wave-wave type interaction in the equation satisfied by
\tilde{h}^{L},\tilde{h}\in\{F,\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\},
the following estimate follows directly from the estimate (
4.79
) in Lemma
4.6
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}H\in\{P,Q\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
k\geq-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,{L}_{1},{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x)\\
\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\\
+\sum_{k^{\prime}\geq-2|\alpha_{out}(|L|)|\delta_{1}m}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{C}_{metric;1}^{\tilde{h}^{L};k,k^{\prime}}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx\big|\big]\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.81)

It remains to consider the case when
\tilde{h}=\underline{F}
. Recall (
2.82
). We first consider the quadratic terms without null structure, i.e., the interaction of
\vartheta_{mn}
and
\vartheta_{pq}
. From the bilinear estimates (
3.136
) and (
3.137
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds for any
\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{\vartheta_{ij}\}
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)

\times R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QP_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|

\lesssim 2^{e(L;\underline{F})\delta_{1}m+e(L;\vartheta_{mn})\delta_{1}m}\big[2^{\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/200}\mathbf{1}_{|L|\geq 2}+\mathbf{1}_{|L|\leq 1}\big]\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\underline{F})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/200}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.82)

Now, we consider the cases with null structure, i.e., all other quadratic terms except the terms considered in the above integral. From the estimate (
4.79
) in Lemma
4.6
, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\},(\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2})\notin\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\times\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3},\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QP_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)dxdt\big|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\underline{F})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.83)

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)

\times R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QQ_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)dxdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)

\times R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QS_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{\tilde{L}_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{\tilde{L}_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\big]

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\underline{F})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.84)

To sum up, the following estimate holds from the estimates (
4.82
), (
4.83
), and (
4.84
),

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}H\in\{P,Q\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}\\
\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big[\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)

\times R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QH_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,{L}_{1},{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x)dxdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\underline{F}^{L}}})}(t,x)R_{\underline{F}}^{\alpha\beta}\big(QS_{\alpha\beta;k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{ess;L,\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}\big)(t,x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\big]\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\underline{F})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/300}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.85)

\bullet
If
k\in[-m+\alpha_{out}(|L|)\delta_{1}m,-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m)
.

Recall (
4.23
) and (
4.33
). For this case, it would be sufficient to consider the case
|L|=0.

Note that, from the rough bilinear estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma
3.17
, due to the smallness of at least
2^{\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}}
from symbols, we can rule out the case when
\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\leq-2\alpha_{inp}(0)\delta_{1}m
. It remains to consider the case
\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}\geq-2\alpha_{inp}(0)\delta_{1}m
, i.e.,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}.

From the bilinear estimate (
3.137
) in Lemma
3.17
and the
Z
-norm estimate of the good component, the following estimate holds for the quadratic term without null structure,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{\vartheta_{ij}\},H\in\{S,P,Q\}\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2N_{0}k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QH_{\alpha\beta;\mu\nu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)\\
\times\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}}})}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\lesssim 2^{m+e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{m+2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

(4.86)

It remains to consider the case
(\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2})\notin\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\times\{\vartheta_{ij}\}
. We first out the non-time-resonance case
\mu=\nu
. Note that the phase is relatively large for the case we are considering. More precisely, we have

\big||\xi|+\mu|\xi-\eta|+\nu|\eta|\big|\gtrsim 2^{k_{1}}\gtrsim 2^{-2\alpha_{inp}(0)\delta_{1}m}.

To take advantage of high oscillation in time, we do integration by parts in time once. Note that, due to the fact that the output frequency is very small, there is no losing derivative
issue. After using the bilinear estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds after doing integration by parts in time once,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\},(\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2})\notin\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\times\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\\
H\in\{S,P,Q\},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1},\mu\in\{+,-\}\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2N_{0}k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}}})}(t,x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)\\
\times R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QH_{\alpha\beta;\mu\mu}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}}}(t))^{\mu}))(x)dxdt\big|\lesssim 2^{-m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.87)

Lastly, we consider the case
\nu=-\mu,\mu\in\{+,-\}
. Since the symbol of quadratic terms with null structure belongs to
\mathcal{M}^{null}_{\mu(-\mu)}=\mathcal{M}^{null}_{-}
, see Lemma
2.4
, we gain the smallness of
(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|+\eta/|\eta|
from the symbol, which is less than
2^{k-k_{1}}
. Therefore, from the rough estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma
3.17
, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}\in\{\vartheta_{ij}\}\\
H\in\{S,P,Q\}\\
k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2N_{0}k_{+}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{P_{k}(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}}})}(t,x)R_{\tilde{h}}^{\alpha\beta}P_{k}QH_{\alpha\beta;\mu(-\mu)}^{\tilde{h}_{1},\tilde{h}_{2}}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}}}(t))^{-\mu}))(x)

\times\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt\big|\lesssim 2^{m+2k+4\alpha_{inp}(0)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.88)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
4.80
).
∎

4.3.
Proof of Lemma
4.6

In this subsection, we provide detailed proof of the Lemma
4.6
.

Firstly, we prove several
L^{2}
-type bilinear estimates in the following Lemma, which are analogue of bilinear estimates in Lemma
3.17
.

Lemma 4.8
.

Assume that
f_{1},f_{2}
satisfy assumptions defined in
(
3.125
)
. Given
L_{1},L_{2}\in\cup_{n\leq N_{0}}P_{n},

|L_{1}|\leq N_{0}-3
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\alpha\in\mathbb{R},\mu,\nu\in\{0,1,2,3\},l,k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}
, s.t.,
l\leq 2
,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}
, we define

K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,\xi):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)\psi_{l}(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}+\nu\frac{\eta}{|\eta|}))\big(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\times\frac{\eta}{|\eta|}\big)^{\alpha}

\times m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\widehat{R_{\mu}f_{1}^{L_{1}}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{R_{\nu}f_{2}^{L_{2}}}(\eta)d\eta,

(4.89)

where the symbol
m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\in\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.
Then the following bilinear estimate holds,

\|K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\alpha l+3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(4.90)

If
l+k_{1},l+k_{2}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
, then the following estimate holds for
\star\in\{\leq m-10,\geq m+10\}
,

\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})](t,x)\psi_{\star}(x)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\alpha l+3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})

\times\min\big\{\sum_{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in P_{0}^{1}\cup P_{1}^{0}}2^{-2m-2k_{1}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1};\Gamma_{1}\circ\Gamma_{2})+2^{-m-3k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}}

+2^{-2m-4k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square\Gamma_{1}f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}},\sum_{\Gamma\in P_{0}^{1}\cup P_{1}^{0}}2^{-m-k_{1}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1};\Gamma)+2^{-m-3k_{1}}\|P_{k_{1}}(\square f_{1}^{L_{1}})\|_{L^{2}},\big\},

(4.91)

\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})](t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}2^{\alpha l}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}C_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(4.92)

If
l+k_{1},l+k_{2}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
and
|L_{1}|=0
, then the following estimate holds,

\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})](t,x)\psi_{>m-10}(x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\lesssim 2^{-m+\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}2^{\alpha l}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}D_{1}(m,k_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2}).

(4.93)

Proof.

To prove our desired estimates, it would be sufficient to consider the case
l<-10
, otherwise, the angular localization function plays little role and desired estimates follow from the estimates in Lemma
3.17
.

Let
\{\theta_{1},\cdots,\theta_{K}\}\subset\mathbb{S}^{2}
be the centers of the ordered sectors s.t.,

1=\sum_{i=1,\cdots,K}\psi_{\leq l}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{i}),\quad\forall i,j\in\{1,\cdots,K\},\quad|\theta_{i}-\theta_{j}|\geq|i-j|2^{l-2}.

We do partition of unity on sphere for the angular variables
\xi/|\xi|
and
\eta/|\eta|
with the sector of size
2^{l}
and have the following decomposition,

\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})](t,x):=\sum_{i,j\in\{1,\cdots,K\},|i-j|\leq 3}K_{i}^{j}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}}),

where

K_{i}^{j}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\widehat{R_{\mu}f_{1}^{L_{1}}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{R_{\nu}f_{2}^{L_{2}}}(\eta)m(\xi,\eta)\big(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\times\frac{\eta}{|\eta|}\big)^{\alpha}\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)

\times\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\nu\eta))\psi_{\leq l}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{i})\psi_{\leq l}(\nu\eta/|\eta|-\theta_{j})d\eta.

From the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, we have

\|K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\sim\sum_{i,j\in\{1,\cdots,K\},|i-j|\leq 3}\|K_{i}^{j}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.94)

Note that, in terms of kernel, we have

K_{i}^{j}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{K}(x_{1},x_{2})P_{k_{1}}^{l,i}(R_{\mu}f_{1}^{L_{1}})(t,x-x_{1})P_{k_{1}}^{l,j}(R_{\nu}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x-x_{1}-x_{2})dx_{1}dx_{2},

\tilde{K}(x_{1},x_{2}):=\mathcal{F}_{\xi,\eta}^{-1}[m_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{k}(\xi,\eta)\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,\nu\eta))\psi_{\leq l+2}(\nu\eta/|\eta|-\theta_{j})\psi_{\leq l+2}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{i})](x_{1},x_{2}),

m_{k_{1}k_{2}}^{k}(\xi,\eta):=m(\xi,\eta)\psi_{[k-3,k+3]}(\xi)\psi_{[k_{2}-4,k_{2}+4]}(\eta)\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi-\eta)\big(\frac{\xi}{|\xi|}\times\frac{\eta}{|\eta|}\big)^{\alpha},

P_{k_{1}}^{l,i}(f)(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\hat{f}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\psi_{\leq l}(\angle(\xi,\theta_{i}))d\xi

=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi)\psi_{\leq l}(\angle(\xi,\theta_{i}))](y)P_{k_{1}}(x-y)dy.

(4.95)

Note that, the following estimate for the kernel after doing integration by parts in
\theta_{i}\cdot\xi
and
\theta_{i}\times\xi
directions and
\theta_{j}\cdot\eta
and
\theta_{i}\times\eta
,

|\tilde{K}(x_{1},x_{2})|\lesssim 2^{\alpha l}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}2^{3k+2l+3k_{2}+2l}\big(1+2^{k}|x_{1}\cdot\theta_{i}|\big)^{-1000}\big(1+2^{k+l}|x_{1}\times\theta_{i}|\big)^{-1000}

\times\big(1+2^{k_{2}}|x_{2}\cdot\theta_{j}|\big)^{-1000}\big(1+2^{k_{2}+l}|x_{2}\times\theta_{j}|\big)^{-1000}.

(4.96)

From the above estimate of kernel, the volume of support, the estimate (
4.94
), and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\|K_{\mu,\nu}(f_{1}^{L_{1}},f_{2}^{L_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{i,j\in\{1,\cdots,K\},|i-j|\leq 3}\big(2^{\alpha l+3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}

\times\|P_{k_{1}}^{l,i}(R_{\mu}f_{1}^{L_{1}})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}}\|P_{k_{2}}^{l,j}(R_{\nu}f_{2}^{L_{2}})(t,x)\|_{L^{2}}\big)^{2}

\lesssim\big(2^{\alpha l}2^{3\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}/2}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}A_{1}(k_{1},m,L_{1})A_{2}(k_{2},m,L_{2})\big)^{2}.

Hence finishing the proof of our
desired estimate (
4.90
) holds.

If we restrict ourself to the case
k,k_{1},k_{2}\in[-m+\delta_{1}m,\infty),l\in\mathbb{Z},
s.t.,
l+k_{1},l+k_{2}\geq-m+\delta_{1}m
, from the estimate of kenel in (
4.96
), we have

\|\tilde{K}(x_{1},x_{2})\|_{L^{1}_{x_{1},x_{2}}}+2^{20m}\|\tilde{K}(x_{1},x_{2})\psi_{\geq m-100}(|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|)\|_{L^{1}_{x_{1},x_{2}}}\leq 2^{\alpha l}\|m(\xi-\eta,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}}.

(4.97)

Moreover,

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi)\psi_{\leq l}(\angle(\xi,\theta_{i}))](y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}&\lesssim 1,\\
\|\mathcal{F}_{\xi}^{-1}[\psi_{[k_{1}-4,k_{1}+4]}(\xi)\psi_{\leq l}(\angle(\xi,\theta_{i}))](y)\psi_{\geq m-100}(y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}&\lesssim 2^{-20m}.\end{split}

(4.98)

Hence, our desired estimates (
4.91
–
4.93
) hold from the estimate (
3.132
) in Lemma

3.17
and the estimate of kernels in (
4.97
–
4.98
) for the case
|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\geq 2^{m-100}
or
|y|\geq 2^{m-100}
and the same argument used in the proof of the estimates (
3.134
–
3.137
) in Lemma
3.17
for the case
|x_{1}|+|x_{2}|\leq 2^{m-100}
and
|y|\leq 2^{m-100}.

∎

With the bilinear estimates in above Lemma, now we prove the postponed Lemma
4.6
.

Proof of Lemma

4.6
.

Note that, in the sense of potentially losing derivatives in the process of doing normal form transformation, the High
\times
High type interaction is similar and also much easier than the Low
\times
High and the High
\times
High type interaction, we restrict ourselves to the Low
\times
High type interaction and the High
\times
Low type interaction. Moreover, due to the symmetry between two inputs, without loss of generality, we assume that
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}
, i.e.,
k_{2}\leq k_{1}-10.

Based on the size of angle between
\xi-\eta
and
\eta
, for
\star\in\{\textup{semi},\textup{quas}\}
, we decompose the integrals in (
4.79
) into two parts as follows,

\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k_{1}}}(t,x)T_{\mu\nu}^{\star}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt

=J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})+J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;>}(t_{1},t_{2}),

(4.99)

where for
\diamondsuit\in\{\leq,>\},

J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\diamondsuit}(t_{1},t_{2})
are defined as follows,

J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\diamondsuit}(t_{1},t_{2})(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)

\times\psi_{\diamondsuit\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))\widehat{\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}(t))^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}}(t,\eta)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,

(4.100)

where the phase
\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)
and the threshold of angular cutoff function are given as follows,

\begin{split}\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)&:=|\xi+\sigma|+\mu|\xi-\eta|+\nu|\eta|,\quad\bar{l}_{+;+}=\bar{l}_{+;-}:=2,\\
\bar{l}_{-;+}=\bar{l}_{-;-}&:=-2\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100.\end{split}

(4.101)

We remark that, for the non-time-resonance case, i.e.,
\mu=+
, there is no need to do angular cutoff and we did this anyway merely for consistence in notation. For this case, we have
J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})=0
. Note that, on the physical space, we have

J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)T_{\mu\nu}^{\star;\leq}((\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},(\widetilde{U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dxdt,

where the bilinear operator
T_{\mu\nu}^{\star;\leq}
is defined by the symbol
m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{\leq\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))
.

Since the null structure of symbol contributes at least one degree of smallness of the angle between
\xi
and
-\nu\eta
, from the bilinear estimates (
4.92
) and (
4.93
) in Lemma
4.8
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L}2^{e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m+\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}+H(|\tilde{L}_{1}|)\delta_{1}m}

\times\min\{2^{H(|\tilde{L}_{2}|+1)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m/100}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{L}_{2}|\geq 1}+2^{\delta_{1}m}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{L}_{2}|=0}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.102)

Now, we proceed to estimate
J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;>}(t_{1},t_{2})
. Note that, for
l\in[2,\bar{l}_{\mu\nu})\cap\mathbb{Z}
, the following estimate holds for the phase
\Phi^{\mu\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)
when the angle is relatively large,

\textup{if\,\,}\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta)\sim 2^{l},|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m/10}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad|\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)|\sim 2^{k_{2}+2l}.

(4.103)

To exploit the oscillation of phase in time, we want to do integration by parts in time once for the case
|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m/10}
. However, due to the quasilinear nature of the Einstein’s equations, there is an issue of losing derivative after doing integration by parts in time. This issue has been resolved by Ionescu-Pausader in
[
29
]
by using the paralinearization and symmetrization method.

For the sake of completeness, we redo this process here. As in the modified profile of the perturbed metric, see (
2.57
) and (
2.59
), we modify the quasilinear variable
\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}
introduced in
[
29
]
as well. More precisely, we let

\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t):={\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t)-\widetilde{\rho}^{\tilde{h}}_{L}(f)(t)=\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}-iT_{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\sigma_{wa}]}\tilde{h}^{L},\quad\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}:=e^{it|\nabla|}\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t).

(4.104)

\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}:=(\partial_{t}-iT_{\sigma_{wa}})\tilde{h}^{L},\quad\sigma_{wa}:=\sqrt{(\tilde{g}^{0j}\zeta_{j})^{2}+(\tilde{g}^{jk}\zeta_{j}\zeta_{k})}+\tilde{g}^{0j}\zeta_{j},\quad\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}=-(g^{00})^{-1}g^{\mu\nu}.

(4.105)

From
[
29
]
[Proposition 4.5], the following paralinearization holds,

\begin{split}(\partial_{t}+iT_{\Sigma_{wa}})\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}&=-\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\beta}\tilde{h}^{L}+\sum_{h\in\{h_{\alpha\beta\}},\iota_{1},\iota_{2}\in\{+,-\}}I_{m_{\iota_{1},\iota_{2}}^{h}}((U^{h})^{\iota_{1}},(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}})^{\iota_{2}}),\\
\Sigma_{wa}&=\sqrt{(\tilde{g}^{0j}\zeta_{j})^{2}+(\tilde{g}^{jk}\zeta_{j}\zeta_{k})}-\tilde{g}^{0j}\zeta_{j},\end{split}

(4.106)

where the Weyl quantization operator
T_{a}
is defined in (
1.18
) and the symbols
m_{\iota_{1},\iota_{2}}^{h}\in\mathcal{M}
,
h\in\{h_{\alpha\beta\}},\iota_{1},\iota_{2}\in\{+,-\}
, satisfy the following estimate,

\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[m_{\iota_{1},\iota_{2}}^{h}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)]\|_{L^{1}_{x,y}}\lesssim\min\{1,2^{k_{2}-k_{1}}\},\quad\forall k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}.

(4.107)

Note that
\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t)
only differs from
\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
at the quadratic and higher order. As a result of
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type bilinear estimate, we have

\begin{split}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}&2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|J_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})-\mathcal{J}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.\\
&\mathcal{J}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}(t,x)T_{-\nu}^{\star;>}((\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}}(t))^{\mu},({U_{k_{2}}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}(t))^{\nu}))(x)dxdt.\end{split}

(4.108)

Now the goal is reduced to the estimate of
\mathcal{J}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2}).
After doing integration by parts in time once for
\mathcal{J}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})
for the case
|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m/10}
, we have

\mathcal{J}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;\leq}(t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{i=1,2}(-1)^{i}End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i})+\widetilde{Err}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{1},t_{2})-\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;i}(t_{1},t_{2})(t_{1},t_{2}),

(4.109)

End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{i}\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)\frac{m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)}{(i\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma))}

\times\psi_{>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t_{i},-\xi-\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}})^{\mu}}(t_{i},\xi-\eta)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t_{i},\eta)\psi_{\leq-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi.

(4.110)

\widetilde{Err}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)

\times\psi_{>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\psi_{>-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt.

(4.111)

\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;1}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)\frac{m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)}{(i\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma))}

\times\psi_{>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\psi_{\leq-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,

(4.112)

\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;2}(t_{1},t_{2}):=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)\frac{m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)}{(i\Phi^{\mu;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma))}

\times\psi_{>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu}}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))\partial_{t}\big(\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\big)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\psi_{\leq-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt.

(4.113)

\bullet
We first rule out the error type term
\widetilde{Err}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{1},t_{2})
.

From the rapidly decay rate of
\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)
when
|\sigma|\geq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m/10-1}
, from the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate and the volume of support of
\xi
, the following rough estimate holds,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\widetilde{Err}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{-m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.114)

\bullet
Next, we estimate the endpoint terms
End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i}),i\in\{1,2\}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;1}(t_{1},t_{2}).

Due to the presence of singular symbol because of the normal form transformation, we need to carefully localize the directions of frequencies.

Let
l\in[\bar{l}_{\mu\nu},2]\cap\mathbb{Z}
be fixed and
\{\theta_{1},\cdots,\theta_{K_{l}}\}\subset\mathbb{S}^{2}
be a corresponding ordered selection of centers on sphere such that
|\theta_{i}-\theta_{j}|\gtrsim|p-q|2^{l-2}
,
\forall p,q\in\{1,\cdots,K_{l}\}
. Based on the possible size of
|L_{2}|
, we split into two cases as follows.

\oplus
If
|L_{2}|\geq N_{0}-10
.

For this case, we have
|L|\geq N_{0}-10
and
|L_{1}|\leq 10.
After doing dyadic decomposition for the angle between
\xi
and
\mu\eta
, super localizing
U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}
,
and doing angular decomposition for
\xi/|\xi|
,
(\xi-\eta)/|\xi-\eta|
and
\eta/|\eta|
with the sector of size
2^{l}
in (
4.110
), we have

End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i})=\sum_{l>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu},p,q\in\{1,\cdots,K_{l}\},|p-q|\leq 3,e,f\in 2^{k_{1}-k}[2^{-10},2^{10}],|e-f|\leq 10}End^{e,f}_{l;p,q}(t_{i}),

End^{e,f}_{l;p,q}(t_{i}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k,e;p}(t)}(x-x_{1}-x_{3})(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}(t_{i}))^{\mu}(x-x_{3})

\times(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}_{k_{2};q}}(t_{i}))^{\nu}(x-x_{1}-x_{2}-x_{3})\tilde{K}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;l,p,q}(x_{1},x_{2},x_{3})\psi_{[m-4,m+1]}(x)dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx,

(4.115)

where

\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k,e;p}}(t,x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\widehat{\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi(2^{-k_{2}}|\xi|-e)\psi_{\leq l+3}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{p})d\xi,

\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}(t)(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\widehat{\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi)\psi(2^{-k_{2}}|\xi|-f)d\xi,

\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}_{k_{2};q}}(t)(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\widehat{\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k_{2}}(\xi)\psi_{\leq l+3}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{q})d\xi,

\tilde{K}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;l,p,q}(x_{1},x_{2},x_{3}):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix_{1}\cdot\xi+ix_{2}\cdot\eta+ix_{3}\cdot\sigma}\psi_{\leq-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma)\frac{m_{\mu\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{[k-2,k+2]}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)}{(\Phi^{\mu\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma))}

\times\psi_{[k_{2}-2,k_{2}+2]}(\eta)\psi_{\leq l+3}(\xi/|\xi|-\theta_{p})\psi_{\leq l+3}(\mu\eta/|\eta|-\theta_{q})\psi_{l}(\angle(\xi,-\nu\eta))d\sigma d\eta d\xi,

(4.116)

Note that, by doing integration by parts in
\xi,\eta,\sigma
, from the estimate (
4.103
), the following rough estimate holds for the kernel
\tilde{K}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;l,p,q}(x_{1},x_{2},x_{3})
,

|\tilde{K}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;l,p,q}(x_{1},x_{2},x_{3})|\lesssim 2^{-k_{2}-l}2^{-3m+3\delta_{1}m/10}2^{3k+2l}2^{3k_{2}+2l}(1+2^{-m+\delta_{1}m/10}|x_{3}|)^{-N_{0}^{3}}(1+2^{k+l}|x_{1}\times\theta_{p}|)^{-N_{0}^{3}}

\times(1+2^{k}|x_{1}\cdot\theta_{p}|)^{-N_{0}^{3}}(1+2^{k_{2}}|x_{2}\cdot\theta_{q}|)^{-N_{0}^{3}}(1+2^{k_{2}+l}|x_{2}\times\theta_{q}|)^{-N_{0}^{3}}.

(4.117)

From the super localized decay estimates (
3.7
) and (
3.8
) in Lemma
3.2
and the
L^{2}-L^{2}-L^{\infty}
type multilinear estimate, if
|L_{2}|\leq N_{0}-10
, then we have

2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|End^{e,f}_{l;p,q}(t_{i})\big|\lesssim\sum_{\Gamma,\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\in\{\Omega_{ij}\}}2^{-k_{2}-l}2^{k_{1}-k_{2}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\|\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k,e;p}(t)}(x)\|_{L^{2}}

\times\|\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{2};q}(t)}(x)\|_{L^{2}}2^{-m+k_{2}/2}\big(\min\{2^{k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\|\widehat{\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}},\|\Gamma\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\delta_{1}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{1}\Gamma_{2}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\delta_{1}^{2}}\}

+2^{-m/3-k_{1}/3}\|\Gamma_{1}\Gamma_{2}\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1},f}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\big)+2^{-20m-10k_{1,+}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

From the orthogonality in
L^{2}
, Hölder inequalties, and the above estimate, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l>\bar{l}_{\mu;\nu},k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\\
p,q\in\{1,\cdots,K_{l}\},,|p-q|\leq 3\\
e,f\in 2^{k_{1}-k_{2}}[2^{-10},2^{10}],|e-f|\leq 10\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|End^{e,f}_{l;p,q}(t_{i})\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-m-k_{2}-\bar{l}_{\mu\nu}+e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m+H(|L_{2}|)\delta_{1}m}\big(2^{H(|L_{1}|+1)\delta_{1}m+2\gamma m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{1}|\geq 1}

+2^{\delta_{1}m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{1}|=0}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.118)

\oplus
If
|L_{2}|\leq N_{0}-10
.

As in the previous caase, we also doing dyadic decomposition for the angle between
\xi
and
\mu\eta
. However, we don’t super-localize
U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}
and
U^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}_{k_{1}}
, and just put
U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}_{k_{2}}
in
L^{\infty}
. With minor modification in the previous case, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i})\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-m-k_{2}-\bar{l}_{\mu\nu}+e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m+H(|L_{1}|)\delta_{1}m}\big(2^{H(|L_{2}|+1)\delta_{1}m+2\gamma m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{2}|\geq 1}+2^{\delta_{1}m}\mathbf{1}_{|L_{2}|=0}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.119)

With minor modifications in the estimate of endpoint terms, by using the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
for
\partial_{t}{\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}}(t,\cdot)
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;1}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|

\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.120)

\bullet
Lastly, we estimate
\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star;2}(t_{1},t_{2})
, see (
4.113
).

We first rule out the non-resonance case,
\mu=+
. Note that,
\forall\sigma\in\mathbb{R}^{3},|\sigma|\leq 2^{-m+\delta_{1}m}
, we have
|\Phi^{+;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)|\sim 2^{k_{1}}
. Hence, we gain one derivative during the normal form transformation, which implies that it allows the one derivative loss for the rough estimate of
\partial_{t}{\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}}}(t,\cdot)
. By using the rough estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
and the same argument used in the estimate of the endpoint case, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3},k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{+\nu;\star;2}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.121)

Now, we focus on the resonance case
\mu=-.
Note that, from the equality (
4.106
) and the equation satisfied by
\tilde{h}^{L}
in (
2.46
), we have

\begin{split}e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}&=(\partial_{t}+i|\nabla|)\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}=(\partial_{t}+iT_{\Sigma_{wa}})\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}-iT_{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}}]\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}\\
&=-iT_{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}}]\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}+R^{\tilde{h}^{L}}+L^{\tilde{h}^{L}},\quad\quad L^{\tilde{h}^{L}}=-\Lambda_{1}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}],\\
R^{\tilde{h}^{L}}&=\sum_{h\in\{h_{\alpha\beta\}},\iota_{1},\iota_{2}\in\{+,-\}}I_{m_{\iota_{1},\iota_{2}}^{h}}((U^{h})^{\iota_{1}},(U^{\tilde{h}^{L}})^{\iota_{2}})-\big(\Lambda_{\geq 2}[N_{vl}^{{\tilde{h};L}}]+R_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}+Q_{mt;s}^{\tilde{h};L}\big).\end{split}

(4.122)

Recall (
4.104
). Since
\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t)
only differs from
\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
at the quadratic and higher order and the linear part vanishes in the equation satisfied by
\widetilde{V^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}
, see (
2.58
), we know that
e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}
consists only quadratic and higher order terms.

From (
4.122
