), by using the decomposition of metric into modified perturbed metric and the density type function in (
2.64
) if the total number of vector fields is more than “
|L|-5
”, otherwise the energy estimate of the perturbed metric is improved, see (
3.16
), we decompose
e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}
into three parts as follows,

e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{V}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}_{k}}=(\partial_{t}+iT_{\Lambda_{0}[\Sigma_{wa}}])\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}=-iT_{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}}]\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}+Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}+Good^{\tilde{h}^{L}},

(4.123)

where “
Good^{\tilde{h}^{L}}
” denotes all good type nonlinearities such that the following estimate holds,

\|Good^{\tilde{h}^{L}}\|_{E_{|L|}}\lesssim 2^{-m+H(|L|)\delta_{1}m+\beta(|L|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(4.124)

and
Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}
denotes the wave-Vlasov type quadratic terms in which the total number of vector fields act on the Vlasov part is more than “
|L|-5
” because otherwise it’s good type.
Roughly speaking,
Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}
has the following general formulation,

\begin{split}\mathcal{F}[Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}](t,\xi):=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}L_{1}\circ L_{2}\preceq L,|L_{2}|\geq|L|-5\\
h\in\{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta},\widetilde{L_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}\}\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}&e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot(\xi-\kappa)}m_{L_{1},L_{2}}^{L}(\xi,v)\\
&\times\widehat{h}(t,\kappa)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}}(t,\xi-\kappa,v)\psi_{\leq-3m/4}(\kappa)d\kappa dv,\end{split}

(4.125)

where the zero order symbol symbol
m_{L_{1},L_{2}}^{L}(\xi,v)\in\langle v\rangle^{10}L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{0}
, see (
1.19
). We remark that, thanks to the bilinear estimate (
3.96
) in Lemma
3.14
, the energy estimate of the wave-Vlasov type interaction can be improved (hence, it’s good type) if the frequency of the metric part is not so small, e.g., greater than
2^{-4m/5}.

Based on the decomposition (
4.123
), we decompose
\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{-\nu;\star;2}(t_{1},t_{2})
into three parts as follows,

\widetilde{\mathcal{J}}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{-\nu;\star;2}(t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{i=1,2,3,4}\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;i}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2}),

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;1}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})&=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\big[\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)\widehat{Good^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\\
&+\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\overline{\widehat{Good^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)}\big]m^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,\end{split}

(4.126)

\begin{split}\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})&=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}m^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\big[\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)\widehat{Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,\\
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})&=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}m^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\overline{\widehat{Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)}\big]d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,\end{split}

(4.127)

\begin{split}&\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;4}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}im^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\big[\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)\\
&\times\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma-\kappa)\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}]}(t,\kappa,-\xi-\sigma-\kappa)\psi_{\leq-10}(|\kappa|/|\xi+\sigma+\kappa|)-\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)\\
&\times\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi-\kappa)\overline{\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}]}}(t,\kappa,\eta-\xi-\kappa)\psi_{\leq-10}(|\kappa|/|\eta-\xi+\kappa|)\big]d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt,\end{split}

(4.128)

where

m^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)=2^{3m}\widehat{\psi_{[-4,1]}}(2^{m}\sigma)(i\Phi^{-;\nu}(\xi,\eta,\sigma))^{-1}m_{-\nu}^{\star}(\xi-\eta,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)

\times\psi_{>\bar{l}_{+;-}}(\angle(\xi,\eta))\psi_{\leq-m+\delta_{1}m/10}(\sigma).

(4.129)

\oplus
The estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;1}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
.

Recall (
4.126
). Similar to the estimate of endpoint case
End_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu\nu;\star}(t_{i})
in (
4.118
) and (
4.119
), by using the same strategy, the following estimate holds from the estimates (
4.124
),

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|\mathcal{H}^{\nu;1}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.130)

\oplus
The estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
and
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
.

Recall (
4.127
). Since there is little difference between the estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
and
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
, we only provide the estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
in details here.

From the multi-linear estimates used in the endpoint case and the rough estimate for
{Q_{vl}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}
, we first rule out the case
k_{2}\geq-m+5H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m
or
k\leq-5H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m
. It would be sufficient to consider the case
k_{2}\leq-m+5H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m
and
k\geq-5H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m
. Moreover, the rough estimate is only off from the desired estimate by at most
2^{3H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m}
.

Recall (
4.125
). On the Fourier side, in terms of the profiles, we have

\big|\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})\big|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1}\circ\tilde{L}_{2}\preceq L,|\tilde{L}_{2}|\geq|L|-5\\
h\in\{\tilde{L}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta},\widetilde{\tilde{L}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}\},\mu\in\{+,-\}\end{subarray}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{5}}e^{-it|\xi-\eta|+i\nu|\eta|+it\hat{v}\cdot(\xi+\sigma+\kappa)}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi)

\times\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)\widehat{V^{h}}(t,\kappa)\widehat{u^{\mathcal{\tilde{L}}_{2}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma-\kappa,v)m^{\star}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)m_{\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}}^{L}(-\xi-\sigma,v)\psi_{\leq-3m/4}(\kappa)d\kappa d\eta d\sigma d\xi dv\big|.

Note that, for the range of frequencies we are considering, we have

\forall v\in B(0,2^{m/10})\subset\mathbb{R}^{3},\quad\big||\xi-\eta|-\nu|\eta|-\hat{v}\cdot(\xi+\sigma+\kappa)\big|\gtrsim 2^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{-2}.

Therefore, either the corresponding phases are all relatively large or the size of
v
is large. As a result, after doing normal form
transformation once for the case
|v|\leq 2^{m/10}
and using the rapidly decay rate of
|v|
for the case
|v|\geq 2^{m/10}
, we have

2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}\big|\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;2}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})\big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.131)

\oplus
The estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
.

Recall (
4.128
). After changing coordinates
(\xi,\kappa)\longrightarrow(\xi+\kappa,-\kappa)
for the first integral and using the fact that
\Sigma_{wa}(x,\xi)
is a real valued symbol, we have

|\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})|\lesssim\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}_{1}^{L_{1}}}}}}(t,\eta-\xi-\kappa)\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}^{\tilde{h}^{L}}}}(t,-\xi-\sigma)

\times\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{h}_{2}^{L_{2}}}})^{\nu}}(t,\eta)M^{\nu;quasi}_{k;k_{1},k_{2}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma,\kappa)d\kappa d\sigma d\eta d\xi dt\big|,

(4.132)

where

M^{\nu;quasi}_{k;k_{1},k_{2}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma,\kappa):=im(\xi+\kappa,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}]}(t,-\kappa,-\xi-\sigma)\psi_{\leq-10}(|\kappa|/|\xi+\sigma|)

-im(\xi,\eta,\sigma){\widehat{\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\Sigma_{wa}]}}(t,-\kappa,\eta-\xi-\kappa)\psi_{\leq-10}(|\kappa|/|\eta-\xi+\kappa|).

(4.133)

From the above formula of symbol, we know that, after symmetrization, the leading order symbol vanishes and it is a zero order symbol, i.e.,
the above integral doesn’t lose derivatives, see also
[
29
]
[Lemma 4.9] for this fact and more detailed computations.

Hence, the estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
is reduced to the estimate of semilinear type quadratic term with modified perturbed metric as inputs, which are good type. Therefore, similar to the estimate of
\mathcal{H}^{\nu;1}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})
, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}\\
k_{2}\geq-m+\alpha_{inp}(|L|)\delta_{1}m\end{subarray}}2^{-k_{-}+2\gamma k_{-}+2(N_{0}-|L|)k_{+}}|\mathcal{H}^{\nu;\star;3}_{L;L_{1},L_{2}}(k;k_{1},k_{2})|\lesssim 2^{2e(L;\tilde{h})\delta_{1}m-\delta_{1}m/100}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(4.134)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
4.79
).

∎

5.
Z-norm estimate of the perturbed metric component

In this section, we will show that, same as in the Einstein-Klein-Gordon system, the perturbed metric components enjoy the modified scattering property. We first briefly introduce the correction of the phase function for the profile of the wave part, which is exactly same as in
[
29
]
and then show that the Vlasov part only plays a minor role in the
Z
-norm analysis, which is the main goal of this section. Due to the almost same setting of the profile and the bootstrap assumption in
[
29
]
is satisfied, for simplicity, the increment of
Z
-norm over time contributes from the Einstein part will be taken as granted. The difference of the
Z
-normed space at the high frequency doesn’t play much role in the analysis, only minor modifications are required. Readers are refer to
[
29
]
[Chapter 5, section 3] for more details.

For
h\in\{h_{\alpha\beta}\}
, we define the low frequency component of
h
, the phase correction function
\Theta(t,\xi)
, and the phase corrected profile
\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)
as follows,

h^{low}(t,x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\widehat{h}(t,\xi)\psi_{\leq 0}(\langle t\rangle^{0.68}\xi)d\xi,

where

\Theta(t,\xi)=\int_{0}^{t}h_{00}^{low}(s,s\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\frac{|\xi|}{2}+h_{0j}^{low}(s,s\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\xi_{j}+h_{jk}^{low}(s,s\frac{\xi}{|\xi|})\frac{\xi_{j}\xi_{k}}{2|\xi|}ds,

(5.1)

\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi):=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}\widehat{V}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi),\quad\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h}(t,\xi)=R_{h}^{\alpha\beta}(\xi)\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi),\quad h\in\{F,\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}.

(5.2)

In particular, from
[
29
]
[equality (5.3.8)], the following equality holds,

\partial_{t}\Theta(t,\xi)=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\xi\cdot\eta/|\xi|}\big(\widehat{h_{00}^{low}}(t,\eta)\frac{|\xi|}{2}+\widehat{h_{0j}^{low}}(t,\eta)\xi_{j}+\widehat{h_{jk}^{low}}(t,\eta)\frac{\xi_{j}\xi_{k}}{2|\xi|}\big)d\eta

As a result, from the equation satisfied by
h_{\alpha\beta}
in (
2.33
), we have

\partial_{t}\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}\big(\partial_{t}\widehat{V}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)-i\partial_{t}\Theta(t,\xi)\widehat{V}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)\big)=\sum_{i=1,2,3,4}\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{i}(t,\xi),

where

\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{1}(t,\xi):=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}e^{it|\xi|}\big(\mathcal{F}[h_{00}\Delta h_{\alpha\beta}-2h_{0i}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}h_{\alpha\beta}+h_{ij}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}h_{\alpha\beta}](t,\xi)-i\partial_{t}\Theta(t,\xi)\widehat{V}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)\big),

\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{2}(t,\xi):=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}e^{it|\xi|}\mathcal{F}[\Lambda_{2}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{wa}](t,\xi),

\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{3}(t,\xi):=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}e^{it|\xi|}\mathcal{F}[\Lambda_{\geq 3}[H_{i\gamma}\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma}h_{\alpha\beta}+\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{wa}](t,\xi),

\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{4}(t,\xi):=e^{-i\Theta(t,\xi)}e^{it|\xi|}\mathcal{F}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}](t,\xi).

(5.3)

Due to the phase correction, the low frequency part
{h_{\mu\nu}^{low}}
of
h_{\mu\nu}
is removed in the quasilinear type quadratic term
h_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}h_{\alpha\beta}
in
\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{1}(t,\xi)
.

Proposition 5.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}

\sum_{h\in\{F,\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\hat{V}^{h}_{\ast}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0},\quad 2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\hat{V}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{\ast}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{0}.

(5.4)

Proof.

Recall (
5.2
) and (
5.3
). For any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{n-1},2^{n}]\subset[0,T],n\leq m
, as obtained in
[
29
]
[estimate 5.3.17], the following estimate holds,

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\big(\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(s,\xi)ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{3}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(s,\xi)ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\big)\lesssim 2^{-\delta_{1}n/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(5.5)

As obtained in
[
29
]
[Lemma 5.20], the following estimates hold,

\begin{split}2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(s,\xi)ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}&\lesssim 2^{\delta_{1}n/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2},\\
\sum_{h\in\{F,\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}}2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{h}(s,\xi)ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}&\lesssim 2^{-\delta_{1}n/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(5.6)

It remains to estimate
\mathcal{R}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}_{4}(t,\xi)
. Recall (
2.35
). From the decay estimate of density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, and the improved bootstrap assumption for the Vlasov part obtained in section
6
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{n-1},2^{n}],

\big|\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{F}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}]](t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\big|\lesssim\min\{2^{-190k_{+}}2^{{d}(0,190)\delta_{0}n},2^{-3k-3n+{d}(3,0)\delta_{0}n}\}\epsilon_{0}.

From the above estimate, for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{n-1},2^{n}],
we have

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\Lambda_{1}[\mathcal{R}_{4}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(s,\xi)]ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-1.001n+{d}(3,0)\delta_{0}n}\epsilon_{0}.

(5.7)

From the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{1}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate and the
L^{\infty}_{x}
-type estimates of basic quantities in (
3.33
), (
3.42
), the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{n-1},2^{n}],

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathcal{R}_{4}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3},|\alpha|\leq 42}\|\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathcal{N}_{\alpha\beta}^{vl}](t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-3n/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(5.8)

From the bilinear estimate (
5.12
) in Lemma
5.1
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{n-1},2^{n}],

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\Lambda_{2}[\mathcal{R}_{4}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-3n/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(5.9)

After combining the estimates (
5.7
–
5.9
), we have

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{4}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(s,\xi)ds\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-1.001n+{d}(3,0)\delta_{0}n}\epsilon_{0}.

(5.10)

To sum up, recall the decomposition of
\partial_{t}\widehat{V}_{\ast}^{h_{\alpha\beta}}(t,\xi)
in (
5.3
), our desired estimate (
5.7
) holds from the estimates (
5.5
), (
5.6
), and (
5.10
).
∎

Let
\mu\in\{+,-\},

h\in\{U^{h_{\alpha\beta}},U^{\tilde{h}},\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{ij}\}\}
and symbol
m(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\in P^{100}_{v}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}
,
(a,b,c)\in\{(-1,0,0),(0,-1,0),(0,0,-1)\}
, see (
2.87
) for the definition of the symbol class
P^{d}_{v}\mathcal{M}^{a;b,c}
, we consider the following bilinear form,

T^{\mu}(h,u)(t,\xi):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi|-i\mu t|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}m(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\widehat{h^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\widehat{u}(t,\eta,v)d\eta dv.

(5.11)

For the above defined wave-Vlasov type interaction, in the following Lemma, we prove a
L^{\infty}_{\xi}
-type bilinear estimate.

Lemma 5.1
.

Let
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
be fixed. Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for the bilinear operator defined in
(
5.11
)
,

\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|T^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(5.12)

Proof.

After doing dyadic decompositions for two inputs, we have

T_{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)=\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi),

where

T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it|\xi|-i\mu t|\xi-\eta|-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\widehat{h^{\mu}}(t,\xi-\eta)\widehat{u}(t,\eta,v)

\times m(\xi-\eta,\eta,v)\psi_{k}(\xi)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{2}}(\eta)d\eta dv.

(5.13)

Note that, from the volume of support of
\eta
, the following estimate holds

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\lesssim 2^{1.001k_{-}+3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}-\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}-1.001k_{1,-}+2H(0)\delta_{0}m}2^{40(k_{+}-k_{1,+})-40k_{2,+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(5.14)

Alternatively, the following estimate holds if we do integration by parts in
v
five times,

2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\lesssim 2^{1.001k_{-}+3\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}-\min\{k,k_{1},k_{2}\}-1.001k_{1,-}+2{d}(5,160)\delta_{0}m}2^{-5m-5k_{2}}2^{40(k_{+}-k_{1,+})-150k_{2,+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(5.15)

From the above two estimates and the
L^{2}-L^{2}
type bilinear estimate at high frequency, we have

\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{1.001k_{-}+40k_{+}}\|T_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{\mu}(h,f)(t,\xi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\lesssim\sum_{k\leq-m}2^{2k+2H(0)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq-m}2^{-5m-3k+2{d}(5,160)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(5.16)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
5.12
).
∎

6.
Energy estimate for the Vlasov part

Recall (
2.91
) and (
2.26
). Due to the small data regime, it would be sufficient to consider the Cauchy problem at time
t=1
. Note that, for any
T^{\ast}\in[1,T)
, we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(T^{\ast},x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(T^{\ast},x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}-\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(1,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(1,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

=\int_{1}^{T^{\ast}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}2\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\partial_{t}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

+2\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\partial_{t}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)dxdvdt.

(6.1)

From the estimate (
2.95
), we have

\int_{1}^{T^{\ast}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}2\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\partial_{t}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)dxdvdt\leq 0.

(6.2)

Therefore, to control the weighted energy at time
T^{\ast}
, it would be sufficient to control the first integral in (
6.1
).

For any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
, from the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
and the equation satisfied by
\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
in (
2.26
), after doing dyadic decomposition for the metric component and classifying the nonlinearities based on the distribution of vector fields, we have

\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\partial_{t}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)dxdvdt\big|

\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\sum_{i=1,\cdots,5}\big|\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}I_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|,

(6.3)

where

I_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)v\cdot\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)

\times P_{k}\big(\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}]\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,

(6.4)

I_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\langle v\rangle\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}

\times\nabla_{x}P_{k}(h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})\cdot D_{v}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)dxdvdt,

(6.5)

I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l+|\alpha|\leq N_{0}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\\
|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\\
\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)

\times\nabla_{x}P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})\cdot D_{v}u(t,x,v)\mathbf{1}_{|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq 2}dxdvdt,

(6.6)

I_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\cup\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}\\
(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2})\in S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

\times\big[P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v}){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

+C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}{\mathcal{L}}_{2})h_{\mu\nu}(t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\big]dxdvdt,

(6.7)

I_{\rho;5}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\cup\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}\\
(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2})\in S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{2}\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

\times\big[P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v}){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

+C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}{\mathcal{L}}_{2})h_{\mu\nu}(t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\big]dxdvdt,

(6.8)

where
\hat{v}_{0}:=1
and the sets
S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{i},i\in\{1,2\},
are defined as follows,

S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}:=\{(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}):\rho_{1}\circ\rho_{2}\preceq\rho,\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|<|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|-1,\\
|\rho_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|<|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|,\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l\in[0,\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,|\rho_{2}|-\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\},

(6.9)

S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{2}:=\{(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}):\rho_{1}\circ\rho_{2}\preceq\rho,\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L},|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|=|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|-1,\\
|\rho_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq 1,\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l\in[0,\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})]\cap\mathbb{Z},|\alpha|\in[0,|\rho_{2}|-\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\}.

(6.10)

The following estimate holds for the coefficients,

\begin{split}|C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)|&\lesssim(1+|x|)^{|\rho|-|\tilde{L}|}(1+|v|)^{|\rho|-|\tilde{L}|-c(\rho)},\\
|{C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)|&\lesssim(1+|x|)^{|\rho_{2}|-|\tilde{L}|}(1+|v|)^{1+|\rho_{2}|-|\tilde{L}|-c(\rho_{2})},\\
|{C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)|+|C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)|&\lesssim(1+|x|)^{|\rho_{2}|-|\tilde{L}|}(1+|v|)^{|\rho_{2}|-|\tilde{L}|-c(\rho_{2})}.\end{split}

(6.11)

The main goal of this section is devoted to prove the following proposition.

Proposition 6.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l}
,
\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{0}.

(6.12)

Proof.

Recall the equality (
6.1
), the estimates (
6.2
) and (
6.3
). From the estimate (
6.13
) in Lemma
6.1
, the estimate (
6.43
) in Lemma
6.3
, the estimate (
6.46
) in Lemma
6.4
, the estimate (
6.75
) in Lemma
6.5
, the estimate (
6.17
) in Lemma
6.2
, we have

\|\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{n\in[1,m]\cap\mathbb{Z}}2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}n}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
6.12
).
∎

Lemma 6.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l}
,
\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\big|\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}I_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.13)

Proof.

Recall (
6.4
) and the choice of weight functions in (
2.93
). As a result of direct computations, we have

\big|\frac{\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|x|\geq 2^{4H(4)\delta_{1}m-3}}\big[\frac{\phi(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})+2^{-4H(4)\delta_{1}m}|x|\phi^{\prime}(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})}{1+|v|^{1/\delta_{1}}+|x|\phi(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})}\big].

(6.14)

Recall (
2.95
). Note that the following estimate holds if
||x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}}-1|\leq 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m},

|\phi^{\prime}(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})|\lesssim 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m},\quad\\
\Longrightarrow\big|\frac{\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}\big|\lesssim\frac{1}{|x|}\mathbf{1}_{|x|\geq 2^{4H(4)\delta_{1}m-3}}+|\phi^{\prime}(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})|\lesssim 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m}.

(6.15)

If
||x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}}-1|\geq 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m},
then we have

\big|\frac{\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|x|\geq 2^{4H(4)\delta_{1}m-3}}\frac{1}{|x|}+2^{-4H(4)\delta_{1}m}|\phi(|x|/(1+|t|)^{4H(4)\delta_{1}})|^{-1}\lesssim 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m}.

Therefore, in whichever case, we have

\big|\frac{\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}\big|\lesssim 2^{-2H(4)\delta_{1}m}.

(6.16)

The above smallness of
{\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)}{(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v))^{-1}}
fills the gap of the sharp decay rate for the perturbed metric. As a result, our desired estimate (
6.13
) holds from the above estimate, the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, and the decay estimates (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
.
∎

Lemma 6.2
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l},\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\big|\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}I_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.17)

Proof.

Recall (
6.5
). Let
\alpha:=H(4)\delta_{1}-2\delta_{1}.
From the obtained estimate (
6.16
), the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, and the
L^{\infty}\longrightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq-m+\alpha m}\big|I_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{m+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{2k+2\delta_{1}m}\big(1+2^{m-2H(4)\delta_{1}m}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.18)

Now we restrict ourselves to the case
k\geq-m+\alpha m,k\in\mathbb{Z}
.
Recall the definition of the weight function
\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)
in (
2.93
).
Motivated from the estimate (
6.14
), we decompose
D_{v}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)
into three parts as follows,

D_{v}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)=\sum_{i=0,1,2}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;i}}(t,x,v),

(6.19)

where

\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v):=\nabla_{v}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)-\psi_{\geq m/10}(|v|)t\nabla_{v}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v),

(6.20)

\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v):=-\psi_{<m/10}(|v|)\psi_{\geq 3m/5}(|x|)t\nabla_{v}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v),

(6.21)

\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;2}}(t,x,v):=-\psi_{<m/10}(|v|)\psi_{<3m/5}(|x|)t\nabla_{v}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v).

(6.22)

As a result of direct computations and the estimate (
6.14
), we have

|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v)|\lesssim(1+|v|)^{-1}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v),\qquad|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v)|\lesssim 2^{m/2}(1+|v|)^{-5}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v),

(6.23)

|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;2}}(t,x,v)|(1+|t-|x+t\hat{v}||)^{-2}\lesssim 2^{-m}(1+|v|)^{-5}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v).

(6.24)

Correspondingly, we have

I_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{i=0,1,2}\tilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),

(6.25)

\tilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\langle v\rangle\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}\nabla_{x}P_{k}(h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})\cdot\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;i}}(t,x,v)dxdvdt.

(6.26)

\bullet
The estimate of
\tilde{I}_{\rho;0}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
.

Recall the estimate (
6.23
). Due to the extra derivative
\nabla_{x}
of the metric part
\nabla_{x}P_{k}(h_{\alpha\beta})(\cdot,\cdot)
in (
6.5
), we first rule out the case
k\notin[-2H(4)\delta_{1}m,H(4)\delta_{1}m]
by using the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate. More precisely, the following estimate holds,

\sum_{k\notin[-2H(4)\delta_{1}m,H(4)\delta_{1}m]}|\tilde{I}_{\rho;0}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{k-4k_{+}+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(2)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.27)

It remains to consider the case
k\in[-2H(4)\delta_{1}m,H(4)\delta_{1}m]
. For this case,
we utilize the hidden null structure of
\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}P_{k}(Lh_{\alpha\beta})(\cdot),L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}.

To better see the hidden null structure, we use the double null decomposition variables. Recall (
3.41
). From the equalities in (
2.38
) and (
2.41
),
\forall L\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}P_{l}
, we have

\hat{v}_{\mu}\hat{v}_{\nu}P_{k}(Lh_{\mu\nu})=\sum_{\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{l},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\big(T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)^{\mu}+\mathcal{R}_{k},

(6.28)

where the symbol
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)(\xi),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{l},\vartheta_{mn}\},
of the Fourier multiplier operator
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)
and the quadratic and higher order error term “
\mathcal{R}_{k}
” are given as follows,

\begin{split}T_{k;\mu}^{F}(v)(\xi)&=c_{\mu}|\xi|^{-1}(1-(\hat{v}\cdot\xi/|\xi|)^{2})\psi_{k}(\xi),\\
T_{k;\mu}^{\underline{F}}(v)(\xi)&=|\xi|^{-1}(1-2\mu\hat{v}\cdot\xi/|\xi|+(\hat{v}\cdot\xi/|\xi|)^{2})\psi_{k}(\xi),\\
T_{k;\mu}^{\omega_{l}}(v)(\xi)&=c_{\mu}\epsilon_{ikl}(1-\mu\hat{v}\cdot\xi/|\xi|)\psi_{k}(\xi)(\hat{v}_{i}\xi_{k}-\hat{v}_{k}\xi_{i})/|\xi|^{2},\\
T_{k;\mu}^{\vartheta_{ab}}(v)(\xi)&=\frac{\epsilon_{mpa}\epsilon_{nqb}}{4|\xi|^{3}}(\hat{v}_{m}\xi_{p}-\hat{v}_{p}\xi_{m})(\hat{v}_{n}\xi_{q}-\hat{v}_{q}\xi_{n})\psi_{k}(\xi),\end{split}

(6.29)

\mathcal{R}_{k}=-2\hat{v}_{i}P_{k}R_{i}\big(\rho^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}-R_{0}\underline{F}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}\big)-\hat{v}_{m}\hat{v}_{n}\big(\varepsilon_{mpq}R_{n}+\varepsilon_{npq}R_{m}\big)R_{p}P_{k}\big(\Omega_{q}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}-R_{0}\omega_{q}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}\big).

(6.30)

Moreover, from (
6.28
), we have

\hat{v}_{\mu}\hat{v}_{\nu}P_{k}(\partial_{t}Lh_{\mu\nu})=\sum_{\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{k},\vartheta_{mn}\},\mu\in\{+,-\}}-\big(iT_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)|\nabla|U^{\tilde{h}^{L}}\big)^{\mu}+\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{k}+\big(T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)\square Lh_{\mu\nu}\big)^{\mu}.

(6.31)

After using the decomposition (
6.28
) for
\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}\nabla_{x}P_{k}(h_{\alpha\beta})
and have

|\tilde{I}_{\rho;0}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{k},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}J_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})+\tilde{R}_{k}(t_{1},t_{2}),

(6.32)

where

J_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\langle v\rangle\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)U^{\tilde{h}}\big)^{\mu}(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,

(6.33)

\tilde{R}_{k}(t_{1},t_{2})=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\langle v\rangle\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathcal{R}_{k}(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt.

(6.34)

Recall (
6.30
) and (
2.41
). Since there is no vector field act on the perturbed metric for the case we are considering, the low order commutator
E_{L,\alpha}^{comm}
vanishes, the following improved decay estimate holds for
\mathcal{R}_{k}^{m}(t,x+t\hat{v})
from the estimate (
3.185
) in Lemma
3.19
, and the estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
,

\|\nabla_{x}\mathcal{R}_{k}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+H(4)\delta_{0}m-2k_{-}}\epsilon_{1}\lesssim 2^{-2m+5H(4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1},\Longrightarrow|\tilde{R}_{k}(t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{-m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.35)

For
J_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})
,
\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\}
, we do integration by parts in time to utilize the null structure. As a result, on the Fourier side, we have

\big|J_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim\big|EndJ_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|+\big|\widetilde{J}_{k;1}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|+\big|\widetilde{J}_{k;2}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})\big|,

where

\begin{split}EndJ_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})&=\sum_{i=1,2}(-1)^{i}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}(t_{i},x,v)\cdot\big(\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)U^{\tilde{h}}\big)^{\mu}(t_{i},x+t_{i}\hat{v})dxdv,\\
\widetilde{J}_{k;1}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})&=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\cdot\big(\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{h}}\big)^{\mu}(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,\\
\widetilde{J}_{k;2}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})&=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\cdot\big(\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)U^{\tilde{h}}\big)^{\mu}(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,\end{split}

where

\mathcal{H}_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v):=\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}\langle v\rangle\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v),

and the operators
\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\},
are defined as follows,

\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)f(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\frac{i\xi T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)(\xi)}{\hat{v}\cdot\xi-\mu|\xi|}\widehat{f}(\xi)d\xi,

where the symbols
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)(\xi),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\},
are defined in (
6.29
). Thanks to the null structure of
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)(\xi)
, the symbols of the operators
\widetilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v),\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{j},\vartheta_{mn}\},
are regular.

From the decay estimates in Lemma
3.1
for the estimate of
e^{-it|\nabla|}\Lambda_{1}[\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{h}}]
and Lemma
3.2
for the estimate of wave part, the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
for the estimate of
e^{-it|\nabla|}\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{h}}]
, we have

|EndJ_{k}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})|+|\widetilde{J}_{k;1}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.36)

Recall the equation satisfied by
\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
in (
2.26
) and the detailed formulas of
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
and
\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)
in (
2.27
) and (
2.28
). We have

\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\partial_{t}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)=\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big(\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)+\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,4}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)

+\nabla_{x}\cdot\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)+D_{v}\cdot\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)+\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v),

where

\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,1}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)=-\frac{1}{2}\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v})v\big(\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2},

\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,2}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)=-\frac{1}{4}\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})\big(\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2},

\begin{split}\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,3}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)&=-\big[\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v})\big)\\
&+\frac{1}{4}D_{v}\cdot\big(\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})\big)\big]\big(\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}.\end{split}

From the above equalities, after doing integration by parts in
x,v
for
\widetilde{\mathfrak{N}}_{\rho,i}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v),i\in\{1,2\}
, the following estimate holds from the estimate of the weight function
\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;0}}(t,x,v)
in (
6.23
), the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9

, and the estimate (
3.62
) in Lemma
3.10
,

|\widetilde{J}_{k;2}^{\tilde{h},\mu}(t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{-m/4}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.37)

To sum up, from the estimates (
6.27
), (
6.35
), (
6.36
), and (
6.37
), the following estimate holds,

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha m}|\tilde{I}_{\rho;0}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.38)

\bullet
The estimate of
\tilde{I}_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
.

From the estimate of the weight function
\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v)
in (
6.23
) and the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, we have

\sum_{k\notin[-m/2-2\delta_{1}m,m/10]}|\tilde{I}_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\notin[-m/2-2\delta_{1}m,m/10]}2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+2\delta_{1}m+m/2+k-10k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.39)

It remains to consider the case
k\in[-m/2-2\delta_{1}m,m/10]
. For this case, we do integration by parts in time once. As a result, we have

\tilde{I}_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{l=1,2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t_{l},x,v)\big)^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t_{l},x,v)\langle v\rangle\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k}}(t_{l},x+t_{l}\hat{v},v)\cdot\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t_{l},x,v)dxdv

+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k}}(t_{l},x+t\hat{v},v)\cdot\partial_{t}\big[(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v))^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v)\big]dxdvdt,

+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle\hat{v}_{\alpha}\hat{v}_{\beta}\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k;h}}(t_{l},x+t\hat{v},v)\cdot(\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v))^{2}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v)dxdvdt

where

\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k}}(t,x+t\hat{v},v):=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{i(x+t\hat{v})\cdot\xi-it\mu|\xi|}\frac{c_{\mu}\xi}{|\xi|(\mu|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\widehat{(V^{h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\xi,\quad c_{\mu}:=i\mu/2,

\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k;h}}(t,x+t\hat{v},v):=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{i(x+t\hat{v})\cdot\xi-it\mu|\xi|}\frac{c_{\mu}\xi}{|\xi|(\mu|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\widehat{(\partial_{t}V^{h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\xi.

From the proof of the linear decay estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, the estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, and the estimate (
3.16
) in Lemma
3.3
, we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{-2}\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k}}(t_{l},x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-m+H(2)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1},\quad\|\langle v\rangle^{-2}\widetilde{h_{\alpha\beta}^{k;h}}(t_{l},x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-2m+H(2)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}.

From the above estimate, the estimate of the weight function
\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;1}}(t,x,v)
in (
6.23
) and the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, and the integration by parts in
v
process used in (
6.65
), we have

|\tilde{I}_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{-m+H(2)\delta_{1}m+m/2+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.40)

After combining the above estimate and the estimate (
6.39
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha m}|\tilde{I}_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.41)

\bullet
The estimate of
\tilde{I}_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
.

Recall (
6.26
), the equalities in (
3.72
) and (
3.141
), and the estimate (
3.74
). For this case, we trade spatial derivative for the vector fields and gain the decay with respect to the light cone twice. As a result, the following estimate holds from the decay estimate of the perturbed metric (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
,

(1+|t-|x+t\hat{v}||)^{2}|\nabla_{x}P_{k}(h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})|\lesssim 2^{-m-k+H(3)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}+2^{-2m-k+H(4)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}.

Recall that
k\geq-m+(H(4)\delta_{1}-2\delta_{1})m
. From the above estimate,
the estimate of the weight function
\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;2}}(t,x,v)
in (
6.24
), and the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha m}|\tilde{I}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+\alpha m}2^{-m-k+H(3)\delta_{1}m+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

+2^{-2m-k+H(4)\delta_{0}m+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.42)

Recall the decomposition (
6.25
). To sum up, our desired estimate (
6.17
) holds from the estimates (
6.18
), (
6.38
), (
6.41
), and (
6.42
).

∎

Lemma 6.3
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l}
,
\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\sum_{k\leq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{i=3,4}\big|I_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(6.43)

where
c(\rho,\mathcal{L}):=\min\{(H(\mathcal{L},\rho)-H(1))/3,\big(H(|\mathcal{L}+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)-H(|\mathcal{L}+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|-1))/2\}
.

Proof.

We first estimate
I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
.
Recall (
6.6
). Note that we have
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq 2
for this case. From the decomposition of profile in (
2.64
) and the decomposition of the density type function in (
3.18
). From the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate and the
L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
type Sobolev embedding, we have

\sum_{k\leq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,k\in\mathbb{Z}}|I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\leq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\iota_{1},\iota_{2}\in\mathcal{S},|\iota_{1}|=|\iota_{2}|=1,\tilde{c}(\iota_{1})=0,\tilde{c}(\iota_{2})=1}2^{m+2k}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}

\times(2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|)\delta_{1}m}+2^{m+k})\big(2^{H(Id,\iota_{2})\delta_{0}m}+2^{m+H(Id,\iota_{1})\delta_{0}m}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.44)

Now, we estimate
I_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
. Recall (
6.7
) and (
6.10
). Note that, we have
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq 2
. By using the same strategy used in the above estimate, we have

\sum_{k\leq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,k\in\mathbb{Z}}|I_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\leq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m\\
\rho_{1}\circ\rho_{2}\preceq\rho,\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\Gamma\in P_{1},\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\iota|=1,\tilde{c}(\iota)=0\\
|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|<|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|-1,\\
|\rho_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|<|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\end{subarray}}2^{m+k}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}

\times(2^{m+k}+2^{H(1+|\rho_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|)\delta_{1}m})\big(2^{H(\mathcal{L}_{1},\iota\circ\rho_{1})\delta_{0}m}+2^{k+H(\Gamma\circ\mathcal{L}_{1},\rho_{1})\delta_{0}m}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.45)

Hence our desired estimate (
6.43
) holds from the estimates (
6.44
–
6.45
).
∎

Lemma 6.4
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l}
,
\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t., s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}\big|I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|+\big|I_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

(6.46)

where
c(\rho,\mathcal{L}):=\min\{(H(\mathcal{L},\rho)-H(1))/3,\big(H(|\mathcal{L}+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)-H(|\mathcal{L}+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|-1))/2\}
.

Proof.

Recall the detailed formulas of
I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
and
I_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
in (
6.6
) and (
6.7
), the definition of index set
S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}
in (
6.9
), and the profile of perturbed metric in (
2.48
). We have
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\geq 2.

Due to the fact that
|\rho_{1}|+|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}|<|{\mathcal{L}}|+|\rho|-1
, by taking the advantage of the different growth rates of the Vlasov part and the metric part, see the estimate (
3.16
) in Lemma
3.3
, we can first rule out the case
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-5
and
k\leq 0
by using the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, i.e., using the decay estimate of the metric component. From now on, we restrict ourselves to the case
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq N_{0}-5
and the case
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-5
and
k\geq 0
.

After using the decomposition in (
2.63
) and doing integration by parts in time on the Fourier side for the modified perturbed metric part, we have

I_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\kappa\in\{+,-\}}\widetilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})+\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})+\widetilde{III}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),\quad i=3,4,

(6.47)

where

\widetilde{I}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l+|\alpha|\leq N_{0}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\\
|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)\cdot\nabla|\nabla|^{-1}\big(P_{k}((\tilde{\rho}^{h_{\mu\nu}}_{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}})^{\kappa})\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,

(6.48)

\widetilde{II}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l+|\alpha|\leq N_{0}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\\
|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{i}\kappa|\xi|+it_{i}\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{(-1)^{i}\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t_{i},-\xi,v)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t_{i},\xi)d\xi dv

+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}e^{it\kappa|\xi|+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t,-\xi,v)\partial_{t}\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)d\xi dvdt,

(6.49)

\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l+|\alpha|\leq N_{0}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\\
|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{it\kappa|\xi|+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}

\times\partial_{t}\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t,-\xi,v)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)d\xi dvdt,

(6.50)

\widetilde{I}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\cup\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}\\
(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2})\in S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(t,x,v)P_{k}\big((\tilde{\rho}^{h_{\mu\nu}}_{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}})^{\kappa}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})

+\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{\rho\rho_{1}\rho_{2};\mu\nu}(t,x,v)|\nabla|^{-1}P_{k}\big((\tilde{\rho}^{h_{\mu\nu}}_{\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}})^{\kappa}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt,

(6.51)

\widetilde{II}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\cup\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}\\
(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2})\in S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}\end{subarray}}(-1)^{i}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{i}\kappa|\xi|+it_{i}\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\big[\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{\rho\rho_{1}\rho_{2};\mu\nu}}(t_{i},-\xi,v)

\times\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t_{i},\xi)+\widehat{\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}}(t_{i},-\xi,v)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t_{i},\xi)\big]d\xi dv

+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\kappa|\xi|+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\big(\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{\rho\rho_{1}\rho_{2};\mu\nu}}(t,-\xi,v)\partial_{t}\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)

+\widehat{\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}}(t,-\xi,v)\partial_{t}\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)\big)d\xi dvdt,

(6.52)

\widetilde{III}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\cup\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}\\
(\tilde{L},\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2})\in S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{1}\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\kappa|\xi|+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\big(\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)

\times\partial_{t}\widehat{\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{\rho\rho_{1}\rho_{2};\mu\nu}}(t,-\xi,v)+\partial_{t}\widehat{\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}}(t,-\xi,v)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)\big)d\xi dvdt,

(6.53)

where

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v):=\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)D_{v}u(t,x,v),

(6.54)

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2}}_{\rho\rho_{1}\rho_{2};\mu\nu}(t,x,v):=\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu,\nu}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v),

(6.55)

\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(t,x,v):=\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v).

(6.56)

\bullet
The estimate of
\widetilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),i\in\{3,4\}.

Recall (
6.48
) and (
6.51
). Note that, these two terms are Vlasov-density type interaction. We first introduce two bilinear estimates which have been studied in
[
49
]
and will be used later.

For any fixed sign
\mu\in\{+,-\}
, any two distribution functions
f_{1}(t,x,v)
and
f_{2}(t,x,v)
, any fixed
k\in\mathbb{Z}
, any symbol
m(\xi,v)\in L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}
, and any differentiable coefficient
c(v)
, we define a bilinear operator as follows,

B_{k}(f_{1},f_{2})(t,x,v):=f_{1}(t,x,v)E(P_{k}[f_{2}(t)])(x+\hat{v}t),

(6.57)

where

E(P_{k}[f])(t,x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}e^{-i\mu t\hat{u}\cdot\xi}{c(u)m(\xi,u)\psi_{k}(\xi)}\widehat{f}(t,\xi,u)d\xi du.

For the above defined bilinear operator, as stated in
[
49
]
[Lemma 2.4],
\forall k\in\mathbb{Z}
, we have

\|B_{k}(f_{1},f_{2})(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 5}\min\{|t|^{-3}2^{k_{+}/2},2^{3k}\}\|m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}

\times\|(1+|v|+|x|)^{20}c(v)f_{2}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|(1+|v|+|x|)^{20}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}f_{1}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

(6.58)

Moreover, if
k\in\mathbb{Z},|t|^{-1}\lesssim 2^{k}\leq 1,
we have

\|B_{k}(f_{1},f_{2})(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 5}\big(\|m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}+\|m(\xi,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}\mathcal{S}^{\infty}_{k}}\big)

\times\big[|t|^{-2}2^{k}\|\big(|c(v)|+|\nabla_{v}c(v)|\big)f_{3}(t,v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+|t|^{-3}2^{k}\|(1+|v|+|x|)^{20}c(v)f_{2}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

+|t|^{-3}\|c(v)\big(\widehat{f_{2}}(t,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot f_{3}(t,v)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\big]\|(1+|v|+|x|)^{20}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}f_{1}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}},

(6.59)

where
f_{3}(t,v):\mathbb{R}_{t}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}\longrightarrow\mathbb{C}
, is
any localized differentiable function.

Based on the possible distribution of vector fields and spatial derivatives, we separate into three subcases as follows.

Subcase

1
.  If
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
.

For this case, we use the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate. As a result, from the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}\sum_{i=3,4}|\widetilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}2^{-2m-2k}(1+2^{k+H(5)\delta_{0}m})2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Subcase

2
.  If
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
and
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-5
.

For this subcase, as the case
k\leq 0
has been ruled out, it remains to consider the case
k\geq 0
. From the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the bilinear estimate (
6.58
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq 0}\sum_{i=3,4}|\widetilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{-m-k/2+H(10)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.60)

Subcase

3
.
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
and
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|>N_{0}-5
.

For this case, we have
|\tilde{c}(\rho_{1})|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq 5
. From the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the bilinear estimate (
6.58
) and (
6.59
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}\sum_{i=3,4}|\widetilde{I}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{-m-k/2+H(10)\delta_{0}m}

+\sum_{k\in[-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,0]\cap\mathbb{Z}}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(10)\delta_{0}m}\big(1+2^{-m-k}+2^{-m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

\bullet
The estimate of
\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),i\in\{3,4\}.
Recall (
6.49
) and (
6.52
).

Subcase

1
.  If
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
.

From the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}
type bilinear estimate, the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
and the estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, we conclude that

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\in[-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,-d(N_{0},0)\delta_{0}m],i=3,4}|\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|

\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in\mathbb{Z},k\in[-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,-d(N_{0},0)\delta_{0}m]\\
0\leq|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|-1\end{subarray}}2^{-k}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(\mathcal{L}_{1},\rho_{1})\delta_{0}m}\big[2^{k/2+k_{-}/2}2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|+1-|\rho_{1}|-|\mathcal{L}_{1}|)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}

+2^{k/2+k_{-}/2}2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|+1-|\rho_{1}|-|\mathcal{L}_{1}|)\delta_{0}m+k_{-}}\big]\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.61)

For
k
relatively large, we first use the equality (
3.141
) and the pointwise estimate (
3.74
) and then rerun the above argument. As a result, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-d(N_{0},0)\delta_{0}m,i=3,4}|\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-H(N_{0},0)\delta_{0}m}2^{-m-2k+3H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{k/2+k_{-}/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.62)

Subcase

2
.  If
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
,
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\leq N_{0}-5
.

For this case, we have
k\geq 0
. From the the
L^{2}_{x}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type bilinear estimate, the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq 0,i=3,4}|\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|

\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq 0}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|+1,0)\delta_{0}m+H(10)\delta_{0}m}(2^{-k/2}2^{-m/2}+2^{-3k/2-3m/2})\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.63)

Subcase

3
.  If
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|>|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-5
,
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|>N_{0}-5
.

For this case, we use the
L^{2}_{x}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type bilinear estimate. As a result, from the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,i=3,4}|\widetilde{II}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{H(10)\delta_{0}m}

\times(2^{-k/2}2^{-m/2}+2^{-3k/2-3m/2})\big(2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|+1)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}+2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|+1,0)\delta_{0}m+k_{-}}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.64)

\bullet
The estimate of
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),i\in\{3,4\}.

Recall the detailed formulas of
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;i}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2}),i\in\{3,4\},
in (
6.50
) and (
6.53
) and the nonlinearities in
(
6.54
–
6.56
).

We first show how to handle
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
in details as follows. Recall the decomposition of “
D_{v}
” in (
2.20
), and the equation satisfied by
\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}
in (
2.26
), we classify
\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)
as follows,

\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};1}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)+D_{v}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};2}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)+\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};3}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)+\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};4}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v),

(6.65)

where

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};1}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v):=-vc_{\iota}(t,x,v)\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v),

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};2}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v):=-\frac{1}{2}\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}

\times c_{\iota}(t,x,v)C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v),

(6.66)

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};3}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)=\sum_{\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\iota|=1}2\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\partial_{t}\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)c_{\iota}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v)

-\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)\big(\partial_{t}c_{\iota}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v)+c_{\iota}(t,x,v)({\mathfrak{N}}_{\iota,3}(t,x,v)+{\mathfrak{N}}_{\iota,4}(t,x,v))\big)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

+\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)\mathfrak{N}_{\rho,4}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)c_{\iota}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\big(vc_{\iota}(t,x,v)\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)

\times\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}](t,x+t\hat{v})\big)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v)+\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\iota}u(t,x,v)

\times D_{v}\cdot\big[\frac{1}{2}\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})c_{\iota}(t,x,v)\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)\big],

(6.67)

\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};4}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)=\sum_{\tilde{L}\in\cup_{\alpha\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3},l\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l},|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)}\mathfrak{N}_{\tilde{L}}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x,v)\nabla_{x}\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}g^{\alpha\beta}(t,x+t\hat{v}),

(6.68)

where

\mathfrak{N}_{\tilde{L}}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x,v):=c_{\tilde{L}}^{\rho}(t,x,v)(v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}u(t,x,v)\cdot D_{v}\big(\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(t,x,v)\big)^{2}C^{\tilde{L}}_{\mu\nu;\rho}(x,v)D_{v}u(t,x,v)\big).

(6.69)

From (
6.50
) and (
6.65
), after doing integration by parts in “
v
” for
D_{v}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};2}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)
and doing dyadic decomposition for the metric component in
\mathcal{N}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};4}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x,v)
, we have

\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=H_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})+\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}H_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,k_{1};t_{1},t_{2}),

H_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}\in\cup_{l+|\alpha|\leq N_{0}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l},|\tilde{L}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\kappa|\xi|+it\hat{v}\cdot\xi}\frac{\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{(\widetilde{V^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}

\times\big(i\xi\cdot\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};1}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t,\xi,v)+\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};3}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t,\xi,v)\big)-\nabla_{v}\big(\frac{\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}\big)\cdot\widehat{\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};2}_{\rho;\mu\nu}}(t,\xi,v)d\xi dvdt,

H_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,k_{1};t_{1},t_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tilde{L}_{1},\tilde{L}_{2}\in\cup_{\alpha\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3},l\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}}\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}P_{l}\\
|\tilde{L}_{1}|,|\tilde{L}_{2}|\leq\tilde{c}(\rho),|\alpha|\leq|\rho|-\tilde{c}(\rho)\end{subarray}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-it\hat{v}\cdot\eta}\frac{\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)\widehat{(\widetilde{U^{\tilde{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}h_{\mu\nu}}})^{\kappa}}(t,\xi)}{2|\xi|(|\xi|+\kappa\hat{v}\cdot\xi)}

\times i(-\xi-\eta)\widehat{\mathfrak{N}_{\tilde{L}_{2}}^{\alpha\beta}}(t,\eta,v)\widehat{\tilde{L}_{2}\mathcal{L}g^{\alpha\beta}}(t,-\xi-\eta)\psi_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)d\eta d\xi dvdt.

(6.70)

Based on the possible size of
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|
, we separate into two subcases as follows.

Subcase

1
. If
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|\geq 10
.

From the estimate (
3.56
) in Lemma
3.9
, the estimate (
3.62
) in Lemma
3.10
, the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
and the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds ,

\sum_{i=1,2,3}\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|v|)^{4}|{\mathcal{N}}^{\tilde{L}\mathcal{L};i}_{\rho;\mu\nu}(t,x-t\hat{v},v)|dv\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-3m/2+H(5)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

From the above estimate, we have

\sum_{k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}|H_{\rho;1}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\lesssim\big[\big(\sum_{k\in[-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m,0]\cap\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k/2-\gamma k}\big)+1\big]2^{-m/2+H(5)\delta_{0}m+H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}

\times 2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.71)

Recall (
6.69
). From the decay estimate (
3.3
) of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
and the
L^{2}_{x}-L^{2}_{x}-L^{\infty}_{x}
type multilinear estimate, we have

\sum_{k,k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})\delta_{0}m}|H_{\rho;2}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,k_{1};t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k,k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z},k\geq-m+c(\rho,\mathcal{L})m}2^{-k+(1/2-\gamma)k_{-}}2^{H(|\mathcal{L}|+|\tilde{c}(\rho)|)\delta_{1}m+H(5)\delta_{0}m}

\times 2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}2^{(1/2-\gamma)k_{1,-}}\big(\mathbf{1}_{|k-k_{1}|\leq 10}+2^{-m-\max\{k,k_{1}\}}\mathbf{1}_{|k-k_{1}|\geq 10}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{4}\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(6.72)

Subcase

2
. If
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|<10
.

Note that for this case we have
k\geq 0
. Since the total number of vector fields is far less than
N_{0}
, we first use the equality (
3.141
) for the metric component to trade one spatial derivative for one vector field.

Thanks to the pointwise estimate (
3.74
), which provides the smallness of
2^{-m}
, after rerunning the above argument, we have

\sum_{k\geq 0,k\in\mathbb{Z}}|\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim\sum_{k\geq 0,k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-k}2^{-m/2+H(5)\delta_{0}m+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.73)

Due to an obvious loss of “
t
” comes from the coefficient of
D_{v}
in
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
, which is not an issue for
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
, the estimate of
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
is similar and also easier than
\widetilde{III}_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
. With minor modifications in the above argument, we have

\sum_{k\geq 0,k\in\mathbb{Z}}|\widetilde{III}_{\rho;4}^{\mathcal{L};\kappa}(k,t_{1},t_{2})|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

(6.74)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
6.46
).

∎

Lemma 6.5
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T]
,
\mathcal{L}\in\cup_{l\leq N_{0}}\mathcal{P}_{l},\rho\in\mathcal{S},
s.t.,
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0},

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\big|I_{\rho;5}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

(6.75)

Proof.

Recall (
6.8
) and the index set
S_{\rho,\mathcal{L}}^{2}
defined in (
6.10
). We have
|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\geq 1
,
|\rho_{1}|+|\mathcal{L}_{1}|=|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}|-1
and
|\tilde{L}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq 1
. From the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate and the estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, the following estimate holds if
(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in\{\partial_{\alpha}\}\times\mathcal{P}
and
\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|=1

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times{C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)dxdvdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

\times C_{{\mathcal{L}}_{1}{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt\big|

\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\min\{2^{m+k+H(1)\delta_{1}m},2^{H(3)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m-10k_{+}}\}2^{H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m+H(\Gamma_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{1},\rho_{1})\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m-\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Now we consider the case
(\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2})\in P\times\{\partial_{\alpha}\}
or
\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|=0
. For this case we have
|\rho_{2}|=1,i.e.,\tilde{L}\in\{\nabla_{x}\}
. In whichever case, the metric component is improved at the low frequency. More precisely, from the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate and the decay estimate (
3.20
), we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\notin[-2H(4)\delta_{0}m,H(4)\delta_{0}m]}\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times{C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)dxdvdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

\times C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})dxdvdt\big|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.76)

From now on, we restrict ourself further to the case
k\in[-2H(4)\delta_{0}m,H(4)\delta_{0}m]
. Moreover, from the pointwise estimate (
3.74
), we can rule out further the small
(x,v)
case and the far away from the light cone case. More precisely the following estimate holds from the estimates (
3.72
) and (
3.74
),

\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v}){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

\times\tilde{\varphi}(t,x,v)\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}dxdvdt\big|+\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)

\times\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})\tilde{\varphi}(t,x,v)dxdvdt\big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.77)

where

\tilde{\varphi}(t,x,v):=\psi_{\leq m/10}(|x|+|v|)+\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{\geq m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|).

(6.78)

Now, we focus on the case when
(x,v)
is large and close to the light cone case.
From the estimate (
2.12
) and the estimate of the coefficient (
2.24
) in Lemma
2.2
, we identify the leading coefficients as follows,

|\tilde{C}(t,x,v)|\lesssim(1+|v|)^{-1-c(\rho_{2})},\quad\tilde{C}(t,x,v):=C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)-c_{\rho_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}(t,x,v)\hat{v}_{\mu}\hat{v}_{\nu},

(6.79)

where
\hat{v}_{0}:=1.

Motivated from the above decompositions of coefficients and the decompositions of
\hat{v}_{\mu}\hat{v}_{\nu}P_{k}(Lh_{\mu\nu})
in (
6.28
) and
\hat{v}_{\mu}\hat{v}_{\nu}P_{k}(\partial_{t}Lh_{\mu\nu})
in (
6.31
), we have

\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)C_{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2};\tilde{L},\mu\nu}^{{\mathcal{L}};\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\Gamma_{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt\big|

\lesssim\sum_{\tilde{h}\in\{F,\underline{F},\omega_{k},\vartheta_{mn}\}}\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\big|I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}\big|+\mathfrak{R}_{k}+\big|II_{k}\big|+\big|III_{k}\big|,

(6.80)

where

I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}c_{\rho_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\big(T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};\alpha}(v)U^{\tilde{h}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}}\big)^{\mu}(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt

(6.81)

\mathfrak{R}_{k}=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}c_{\rho_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\big[\partial_{\alpha}\mathcal{R}_{k}

+a_{\alpha}\big(T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)\square\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu}\big)^{\mu}\big]\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt,

(6.82)

II_{k}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\tilde{C}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\partial_{\alpha}P_{k}(\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times\psi_{\leq H(5)\delta_{1}m}(v)\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt.

(6.83)

III_{k}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\tilde{C}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v)\partial_{\alpha}P_{k}(\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}h_{\mu\nu})(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times\psi_{>H(5)\delta_{1}m}(v)\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt,

(6.84)

where
a_{0}=1
and
a_{i}=0
and the Fourier multiplier operators
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};\alpha}(v)
are defined as follow,

T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};0}(v)=-iT_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)|\nabla|,\quad T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};j}(v):=\partial_{j}T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v).

(6.85)

Recall (
3.41
). For the main term of
\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}]=-(1+|v|^{2})^{-1}\Lambda_{1}[v^{0}]
, we have

P_{k}h_{00}-\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}P_{k}h_{ij}=G_{k}+B_{k},\quad B_{k}:=P_{k}\underline{F}-\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}R_{i}R_{j}P_{k}\underline{F}=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\big(\tilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\underline{F}}(v)U^{\underline{F}}\big)^{\mu},

(6.86)

where
G_{k}
denotes the good part of
P_{k}h_{00}-\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}P_{k}h_{ij}
, which doesn’t depend on
\underline{F}
