, and the symbol of the Fourier multiplier operator is given as follows,

\tilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\underline{F}}(v)(\xi)=c_{\mu}|\xi|^{-1}(1-(\hat{v}\cdot\xi/|\xi|)^{2})\psi_{k}(\xi).

(6.87)

Similar to the decomposition (
6.80
), from (
6.86
), we have

\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\tilde{L}\mathcal{L}_{2}\Lambda_{1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v}){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

\times\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt\big|\lesssim\big|J_{k}^{1}\big|+\big|J_{k}^{2}\big|,

where

J_{k}^{1}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\langle v\rangle^{-1}(\tilde{L}G_{k})(t,x+t\hat{v}){C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)

\times\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt,

(6.88)

J_{k}^{2}=\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\langle v\rangle^{-1}\tilde{L}\big(\tilde{T}_{k;\mu}^{\underline{F}}(v)U^{\underline{F}}\big)^{\mu}(t,x+t\hat{v})

\times{C}_{\mathcal{L}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{2};\tilde{L}}^{\mathcal{L};\rho,\rho_{1},\rho_{2}}(x,v)\cdot\nabla_{x}\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t,x,v)\psi_{>m/10}(|x|+|v|)\psi_{<m-10}(|x+t\hat{v}|-|t|)dxdvdt,

(6.89)

Recall (
2.41
) and (
2.43
). Note that, if
\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})=0
, then the commutator
E_{\tilde{L},k}^{comm}
vanishes in
\mathcal{R}_{k}
. From the estimate (
3.190
) in Lemma
3.20
, the decay estimate of the density type function in Lemma
3.1
, the decay estimate (
3.7
) in Lemma
3.2
, the estimate (
3.186
) in Lemma
3.19
, and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
, we have

\begin{split}\|\partial_{\alpha}\mathcal{R}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})\geq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial_{\alpha}G_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim 2^{-m+0.999k-5k_{+}}\epsilon_{1},\\
\|\partial_{\alpha}\mathcal{R}_{k}(t,x)\psi_{\geq m-100}(|x|)\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{c}(\rho_{2})=0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim 2^{-10k_{+}}\min\{2^{-m+H(4)\delta_{0}m+k},2^{-2m+H(4)\delta_{0}m}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.\end{split}

(6.90)

From the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{\infty}_{x,v}
type multilinear estimate, the estimate of coefficients in (
2.24
) and (
6.79
) and the above estimate, we have

\sum_{k\in[-2H(4)\delta_{0}m,H(4)\delta_{0}m]\cap\mathbb{Z}}|\mathfrak{R}_{k}|+|III_{k}|+|J_{k}^{1}|\lesssim 2^{2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.91)

In the above estimate, we have used the extra decay comes from the smallness of coefficient
\tilde{C}(t,x,v)
as
v
is relatively large (see (
6.84
)) in the above estimate of
III_{k}
.

It remains to estimate
I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}
,
II_{k}
, and
J_{k}^{2}
for the case
k\in[-2H(4)\delta_{0}m,H(4)\delta_{0}m]
. We use the normal form transformation by doing integration by parts in time to utilize the null structure in
I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}
and
J_{k}^{2}
, see (
6.29
) and (
6.87
). As a result, on the Fourier side, we have

I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}=\sum_{j=1,2}(-1)^{j-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\overline{\widehat{\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{h}}}(t_{j},\xi,v)}e^{it_{j}\hat{v}\cdot\xi-it_{j}\mu|\xi|}T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};\alpha;1}(v,\xi)i(\hat{v}\cdot\xi-\mu|\xi|)^{-1}\widehat{(V^{\tilde{h}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}})^{\mu}}(t_{j},\xi)d\xi dv

+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\hat{v}\cdot\xi-it\mu|\xi|}T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};\alpha;1}(v,\xi)i(\hat{v}\cdot\xi-\mu|\xi|)^{-1}\partial_{t}\big(\overline{\widehat{\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{h}}}(t,\xi,v)}\widehat{(V^{\tilde{h}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}})^{\mu}}(t,\xi)\big)d\xi dvdt

(6.92)

where

\mathcal{N}_{\tilde{h}}(t,x,v):=\big(\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\rho}(x,v)\big)^{2}c_{\rho_{2}}^{\tilde{L}}(x,v)\Lambda^{\rho}{u}^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)\Lambda^{\rho_{1}}u^{\Gamma_{1}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}(t,x,v).

Similar formula holds for
J_{k}^{2}
, we omit details here.

From the detailed formulas of symbols
T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h}}(v)(\xi)
in (
6.29
), after redoing the proof of lineaer decay estimate in Lemma
3.2
, we have

\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-i\mu t|\xi|}T_{k;\mu}^{\tilde{h};\alpha;1}(v,\xi)i(\hat{v}\cdot\xi-\mu|\xi|)^{-1}\widehat{(V^{\tilde{h}^{\tilde{L}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}})^{\mu}}(t_{j},\xi)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\xi\big|\lesssim 2^{-m+H(2)\delta_{1}m+\delta_{1}m}\epsilon_{1}.

From the above estimate, we know that we lose little but gain at least
2^{-m+H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m}
from doing integration by parts in time.

The estimate of
II_{k}
is similar. Recall (
6.83
), by exploiting the fact that
v
is small in
II_{k}
, we also doing normal form transformation for
II_{k}
, we lose at most
(1+|v|)^{2}2^{|k|}
, which is less than
2^{10H(5)\delta_{0}m}
as
|v|\leq 2^{H(5)\delta_{1}m}
and
k\in[-2H(4)\delta_{0}m,H(4)\delta_{0}m]
but gain at least
2^{-m+H(N_{0}+3)\delta_{0}m}
.

To sum up, similar to the procedure we did in the estimate of
I_{\rho;3}^{\mathcal{L}}(k,t_{1},t_{2})
in Lemma
6.4
, we have

\big|I_{\tilde{h};k}^{\mu}\big|+|II_{k}|+|J_{k}^{2}|\lesssim 2^{-m/2}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(6.93)

Hence finishing the proof of our desired estimate (
6.75
).
∎

7.
The
Z
-norm estimate for the Vlasov part

Recall the equation satisfied by
u^{\mathcal{L}}
in (
2.13
) and the definition of the correction term in (
2.96
). As a result of direct computations, after doing integration by parts in
x
once for
\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{2}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}
, based on the order of derivatives in
v
and the number of vector fields in
\cup_{n}\mathcal{P}_{n}
distributed, we classify the nonlinearity of the equation satisfied by
\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(t,v)
as follows,

\partial_{t}\big(\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(t,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(t,v)\big)=\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|,\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)dx,

(7.1)

where, if
\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\mathcal{L}_{2}=\mathcal{L}
, we have

\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)=\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{v}-t\nabla_{v}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\cdot\big((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta}\big)(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v)

+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}]\big)(t,x+t\hat{v})\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}u^{\mathcal{L}}(t,x,v),

and if
|\alpha_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|<|\alpha|+|\mathcal{L}|
, then
\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)
is defined as follows,

\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)=c_{1}(v)\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{1}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[\mathcal{L}_{1}(v^{0})^{-1}]\big))(t,x+t\hat{v})\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{2}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)

+c_{2}(v)\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{1}}\big(\mathcal{L}_{1}((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta})\big)(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\nabla_{v}\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{2}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)

+c_{3}(v)t(\nabla_{v}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x})\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{1}}\big(\mathcal{L}_{1}((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta})\big)(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\nabla_{v}^{\alpha_{2}}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v).

(7.2)

For simplicity of notation, we suppress the dependence of coefficients
c_{i}(v),i\in\{1,2,3\},
with respect to
\alpha,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\mathcal{L},\mathcal{L}_{1},
and
\mathcal{L}_{2}
.

Proposition 7.1
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions
(
2.88
)
and
(
2.91
)
, the following estimate holds for any
\mathcal{L}\in\mathcal{P}_{n},
and any time
\tau\in[0,T)
,

\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v){u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(\tau,v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\big(\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(\tau,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(\tau,v)\big)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0},

(7.3)

where
{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(t,v)
was defined in
(
2.96
)
.

Proof.

\oplus
The estimate of correction term
{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(\tau,v)
.

Recall (
2.96
).
From the pointwise estimate (
3.72
) and (
3.74
) and the decay estimate (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, the following estimate holds after using the equality (
3.72
) once and gaining the decay rate of distance with respect to the light cone,

\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v){u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(\tau,v)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}+\sum_{|\mathcal{L}|+|\rho|\leq N_{0}}\int_{0}^{\tau}(1+t)^{-2+2H(\mathcal{L},\rho)\delta_{0}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}dt\lesssim\epsilon_{0}.

(7.4)

\oplus
The estimate of
\nabla_{v}^{\alpha}\widehat{u^{\mathcal{L}}}(\tau,0,v)-\nabla_{v}\cdot{u^{\mathcal{L};\alpha}_{corr}}(\tau,v)
.

Recall (
7.2
). Note that, from (
2.16
), we can represent
\nabla_{v}
as a linear combination of second set of vector fields as follows,

\nabla_{v}=\sum_{\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\iota|=1}c_{\iota}(x,v)\Lambda^{\iota},\quad|c_{\iota}(x,v)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle\langle v\rangle.

(7.5)

From the equality (
2.23
) in Lemma
2.2
, if
|\alpha_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|<|\alpha|+|\mathcal{L}|
, we reduce
\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)
further as follows,

\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)=\sum_{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}|\leq|\mathcal{L}_{1}|+|\alpha_{1}|}\sum_{\rho,\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|\leq|\alpha_{2}|,|\iota|\leq 1}\tilde{c}^{1}_{\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)v\cdot\nabla_{x}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}(\Lambda_{\geq 1}[(v^{0})^{-1}])(t,x+t\hat{v},v)

\times\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)+\tilde{c}^{2}_{\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\Lambda^{\iota}\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)

+\tilde{c}^{3}_{\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)t\nabla_{x}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}((v^{0})^{-1}v_{\alpha}v_{\beta}\nabla_{x}g^{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v},v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v),

(7.6)

where in the above equality we suppress the dependence of coefficients with respect to the order of derivatives. From the estimate (
2.24
) in Lemma
2.2
and the estimate (
7.5
), the rough estimate holds

\sum_{i=1,2,3}|\tilde{c}^{i}_{\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)|\lesssim(\langle x\rangle+\langle v\rangle)^{2|\alpha|+10}.

(7.7)

Let
t\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T),m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
. Based on the total number of vector fields act on the Vlasov part, we separate into two cases as follows.

Subcase
1
:
If
|\alpha_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\geq N_{0}/2+5
.

For this case, we know that the total number of vector fields act on the perturbed metric is less than
N_{0}/2-4
. From the equality (
3.72
), the estimate (
3.74
), the estimate of coefficients in (
7.7
), the decay estimate of perturbed metric (
3.17
) in Lemma
3.3
, and the decay estimate of high order terms in Lemma
3.6

and Lemma
3.8
, we have

\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)dx\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|\langle x\rangle^{2}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\lesssim\sum_{\rho\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|\leq|\alpha|}2^{-2m+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(7.8)

Subcase
2
:
If
|\alpha_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq N_{0}/2+4
.

From the
L^{2}
-estimate of the high order terms in Lemma
3.6
and Lemma
3.7
, and the decay estimate of density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, we have

\|\langle x\rangle^{2}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\Lambda_{\geq 3}[\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}](t,x,v)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2^{-2m/3-m/2+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(7.9)

For the quadratic terms of
\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)
, after doing dyadic decomposition for the metric component and using (
7.5
) and the equality (
2.23
) in Lemma
2.2
, from the estimate (
2.24
) in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\Lambda_{2}[\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}](t,x,v)=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{i=1,2,3}\sum_{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}|\leq|\mathcal{L}_{1}|+|\alpha_{1}|}\sum_{\rho,\iota\in\mathcal{S},|\rho|\leq|\alpha_{2}|,|\iota|\leq 1}H_{k;i}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v),

where

\begin{split}H_{k;1}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)&={c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,1}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\nabla_{x}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v),\\
H_{k;2}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)&={c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,2}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\nabla_{x}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\iota\circ\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v),\\
H_{k;3}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)&=t{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t,x,v)P_{k}(\nabla_{x}^{2}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta})(t,x+t\hat{v})\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v),\\
\sum_{i=1,2,3}|{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,i}(t,x,v)|+|t\partial_{t}{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,i}(t,x,v)|&\lesssim(\langle x\rangle+\langle v\rangle)^{2|\alpha|+10}\end{split}

(7.10)

From the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type multilinear estimate and the decay estimate of density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, we can rule out the case
k\notin[-m/2,{m/10}]
as follows,

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1,2,3}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\notin[-m/2,{m/10}]}\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)H_{k;i}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)dx\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},k\notin[-m/2,{m/10}]}\big(2^{-3m/2+k/2}+2^{-m/2+3k/2}\big)2^{-10k_{+}+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\\
&\lesssim 2^{-11m/10}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.\end{split}

(7.11)

For the case
k\in[-m/2,{m/10}]
, same as what we did in the proof of Lemma
6.4
, we do normal form transformation. Note that the price of using normal form transformation is minor because the weight function we used in the low order energy is much weaker than the weight function we propagated in the energy estimate, see (
2.93
) and (
2.94
).

Recall (
7.10
). As a typical example, which is also the most difficult case because of the extra loss of “
t
”, we estimate
H_{k;3}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)
in details. With minor modifications, the estimate of
H_{k;i}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v),i\in\{1,2\}
follows in the same way.

For any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{m-1},2^{m}]\subset[0,T],m\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},k\in[-m/2,{m/10}]\cap\mathbb{Z}
, after doing normal form transformation, we have

\big|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{k;3}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)dxdt\big|\lesssim\sum_{\mu\in\{+,-\}}\big|End^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k,\mu}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big|+\big|\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu;1}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big|+\big|\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu;2}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big|,

where

\begin{split}End^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu}(t_{1},t_{2},v)&:=\sum_{l=1,2}(-1)^{l}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}t_{l}{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t_{l},x,v)\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}((U^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu})(t_{l},x+t_{l}\hat{v},v)\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t_{l},x,v)dx,\\
\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu;1}(t_{1},t_{2},v)&:=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)\big[t{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t,x,v)\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}(v)((e^{-it|\nabla|}\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu})(t,x+t\hat{v})\\
&+\partial_{t}\big(t{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t,x,v)\big)\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}(v)((U^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu})(t,x+t\hat{v})\big]dxdt,\\
\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;2}(t_{1},t_{2},v)&:=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}t{c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t,x,v)\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}(v)((U^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}})^{\mu})(t,x+t\hat{v})\partial_{t}\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x,v)dxdt,\end{split}

(7.12)

and the Fourier multiplier operator
\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}(v)
is given as follows,

\widetilde{M}^{\mu}_{k}(v)(f)(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{ix\cdot\xi}\frac{\xi\otimes\xi\psi_{k}(\xi)}{|\xi|\big(\mu|\xi|-\hat{v}\cdot\xi\big)}\widehat{f}(\xi)d\xi.

From the
L^{2}_{x,v}-L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}-L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
type multilinear estimate and the decay estimate of density type function (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, we have

\big\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)End^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k,\mu}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+(1/2-\gamma)k_{-}-10k_{+}+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(7.13)

Similarly, by using the same strategy, from the bilinear estimate (
6.58
) for
\Lambda_{1}[\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}]
and the estimate (
3.1
) in Proposition
3.1
for
\Lambda_{\geq 2}[\partial_{t}V^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1}h_{\alpha\beta}}]
, we have

\big\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu;1}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+(1/2-\gamma)k_{-}-10k_{+}+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}+2^{-m+k_{+}/2}\epsilon^{3}_{1}.

(7.14)

Lastly, we estimate
\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;2}(t_{1},t_{2},v)
. Due to the fact that we have
|\rho|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq|\alpha_{2}|+|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq N_{0}/2+4
for the case are considering. Recall (
2.5
) and the equality (
2.3
) in Lemma
2.1
. From the decay estimate (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
, the following estimate holds after distributing derivative and put the metric part in
L^{\infty}
,

\|\langle v\rangle^{20}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v){c}_{\alpha\beta}^{\rho,\iota,3}(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\partial_{t}\Lambda^{\rho}u^{\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-5m/2+2d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

From the above estimate and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\big\|\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\mathcal{H}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}_{k;\mu;2}(t_{1},t_{2},v)\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-m/2+(1/2-\gamma)k_{-}-10k_{+}+3d(|\mathcal{L}|+3,|\rho|)\delta_{0}m}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(7.15)

After combining the above obtained estimates, we have

\sum_{i=1,2,3}\sum_{k\in[-m/2,{m/10}]\cap\mathbb{Z}}\big\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)H_{k;i}^{\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\rho,\iota}(t,x,v)dxdt\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim 2^{-m/3}\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

(7.16)

To sum up, from the estimates (
7.8
), (
7.9
), (
7.11
), and (
7.16
), we have

\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|,\mathcal{L}_{1}\circ\mathcal{L}_{2}\preceq\mathcal{L}}\|\int_{0}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\omega^{\mathcal{L}}_{\alpha}}(v)\mathfrak{R}_{\mathcal{L};\mathcal{L}_{1},\mathcal{L}_{2}}^{\alpha;\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(t,x,v)dxd\tau\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Recall (
7.1
). Our desired estimate (
7.3
) holds from the above estimate and (
7.4
).
∎
